// ── Reduced motion CSS (WCAG 2.3.3) — shared across all STEAM Lab tools ──
(function() {
  if (typeof document === 'undefined') return;
  if (document.getElementById('allo-stem-motion-reduce-css')) return;
  var st = document.createElement('style');
  st.id = 'allo-stem-motion-reduce-css';
  st.textContent = '@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) { *, *::before, *::after { animation-duration: 0.01ms !important; animation-iteration-count: 1 !important; transition-duration: 0.01ms !important; scroll-behavior: auto !important; } }';
  if (document.head) document.head.appendChild(st);
})();

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════
// stem_tool_atctower.js — ATC Tower: Air Traffic Control Simulator
// Teaches: spatial reasoning, rate problems, vectors, sequencing,
// communication protocols, and decision-making under pressure
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════

window.StemLab = window.StemLab || {
  _registry: {}, _order: [],
  registerTool: function(id, config) { config.id = id; config.ready = config.ready !== false; this._registry[id] = config; if (this._order.indexOf(id) === -1) this._order.push(id); console.log('[StemLab] Registered tool: ' + id); },
  isRegistered: function(id) { return !!this._registry[id]; },
  getRegisteredTools: function() { var self = this; return this._order.map(function(id) { return self._registry[id]; }).filter(Boolean); },
  renderTool: function(id, ctx) { var tool = this._registry[id]; if (!tool || !tool.render) return null; try { return tool.render(ctx); } catch(e) { console.error('[StemLab] Error rendering ' + id, e); return null; } }
};

if (!(window.StemLab.isRegistered && window.StemLab.isRegistered('atcTower'))) {

(function() {
  'use strict';
  // ── Accessibility live region (WCAG 4.1.3) ──
  (function() {
    if (document.getElementById('allo-live-atcTower')) return;
    var lr = document.createElement('div');
    lr.id = 'allo-live-atcTower';
    lr.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
    lr.setAttribute('aria-atomic', 'true');
    lr.setAttribute('role', 'status');
    lr.className = 'sr-only';
    lr.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;padding:0;margin:-1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0,0,0,0);border:0';
    document.body.appendChild(lr);
  })();


  // ── ATC MATH HELPERS ──
  var toRad = function(d) { return d * Math.PI / 180; };
  var toDeg = function(r) { return r * 180 / Math.PI; };
  var distNm = function(x1, y1, x2, y2) { return Math.sqrt((x2-x1)*(x2-x1) + (y2-y1)*(y2-y1)); };
  var bearingDeg = function(x1, y1, x2, y2) { return (toDeg(Math.atan2(x2-x1, -(y2-y1))) + 360) % 360; };

  // ── ATC SIMULATION MODEL ──
  // Positions use a fixed world grid: 3 world units = 1 nautical mile.
  // The teaching simulation runs at 120x flight time; spawn timers and UI
  // feedback use real seconds. Canvas size never changes aircraft physics.
  var ATC_UNITS_PER_NM = 3;
  var ATC_TIME_ACCELERATION = 120;
  var normalizeHeading = function(value) { return ((value % 360) + 360) % 360; };
  var headingDifference = function(a, b) { return ((a - b + 540) % 360) - 180; };
  var moveToward = function(value, target, amount) { return value + Math.max(-amount, Math.min(amount, target - value)); };
  var runwayGeometry = function(rwy) {
    var vx = Math.sin(toRad(rwy.hdg)), vy = -Math.cos(toRad(rwy.hdg));
    var threshold = { x: rwy.x - vx * rwy.len / 2, y: rwy.y - vy * rwy.len / 2 };
    return { vx: vx, vy: vy, threshold: threshold,
      finalEntry: { x: threshold.x - vx * 36, y: threshold.y - vy * 36 },
      end: { x: rwy.x + vx * rwy.len / 2, y: rwy.y + vy * rwy.len / 2 } };
  };
  var runwayLabel = function(rwy, airport) {
    if (!rwy) return '—';
    var label = String(Math.round(normalizeHeading(rwy.hdg) / 10) || 36).padStart(2, '0');
    var parallel = airport && airport.runways ? airport.runways.filter(function(r) { return r.hdg === rwy.hdg; }) : [];
    if (parallel.length > 1) {
      var geometry = runwayGeometry(rwy);
      parallel.sort(function(a, b) { return (a.x * -geometry.vy + a.y * geometry.vx) - (b.x * -geometry.vy + b.y * geometry.vx); });
      label += (parallel.length === 2 ? ['L', 'R'] : ['L', 'C', 'R'])[parallel.indexOf(rwy)] || '';
    }
    return label;
  };
  // Crossing runways share one protected movement area. Occupancy lasts two
  // real seconds (four accelerated flight minutes) in this teaching model.
  var runwayGroup = function(rwy, airport) {
    return airport.id === 'crossing' ? 'intersection' : 'runway-' + airport.runways.indexOf(rwy);
  };
  var runwayAvailable = function(rwy, airport, busyUntil, time) {
    return (busyUntil[runwayGroup(rwy, airport)] || 0) <= time;
  };
  var reserveRunway = function(rwy, airport, busyUntil, time) {
    busyUntil[runwayGroup(rwy, airport)] = time + 2;
  };
  var aircraftDistanceNm = function(ac, rwy) {
    var target = rwy ? runwayGeometry(rwy).threshold : { x: 200, y: 200 };
    return distNm(ac.x, ac.y, target.x, target.y) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM;
  };
  var aircraftEtaMinutes = function(ac, rwy) {
    if (ac.state === 'holding' || ac.state === 'goAround' || !(ac.speed > 0)) return null;
    var distance = aircraftDistanceNm(ac, rwy);
    if (ac.state === 'approach' && rwy && ac.approachStage !== 'final') {
      var geometry = runwayGeometry(rwy);
      distance = (distNm(ac.x, ac.y, geometry.finalEntry.x, geometry.finalEntry.y) + 36) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM;
    }
    return distance / ac.speed * 60;
  };
  // ── ATC SEQUENCING AND SHORT-HORIZON WATCH ──
  // These are planning estimates, not clearances. The watch holds present
  // headings/speeds and the current altitude target fixed; turns, approach
  // guidance, and changing targets can change the result. Its horizon uses
  // flight seconds, not the accelerated shift clock.
  var atcFinite = function(value) { return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value); };
  var atcActiveAircraft = function(ac) { return ac && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed' && ac.state !== 'crashed'; };
  var atcPlanningEta = function(ac, rwy) {
    if (!ac || !atcFinite(ac.x) || !atcFinite(ac.y) || !atcFinite(ac.speed) || ac.speed <= 0) return null;
    if (rwy && (!atcFinite(rwy.hdg) || !atcFinite(rwy.x) || !atcFinite(rwy.y) || !atcFinite(rwy.len))) return null;
    var eta = aircraftEtaMinutes(ac, rwy);
    return atcFinite(eta) && eta >= 0 ? eta : null;
  };
  var sortedFlightStrips = function(aircraft, airport) {
    // The first runway supplies a planning estimate for unassigned strips;
    // runwayScheduleHints only puts explicitly assigned aircraft in a queue.
    var fallbackRunway = airport && airport.runways && airport.runways[0];
    return (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).filter(atcActiveAircraft).map(function(ac, index) {
      var fuel = atcFinite(ac.fuel) ? ac.fuel : Infinity;
      var priority = (ac.emergency && ac.emergency.priority !== false) || fuel < 5 ? 0 : fuel < 10 ? 1 : 2;
      var eta = atcPlanningEta(ac, ac.assignedRunway || fallbackRunway);
      return { aircraft: ac, priority: priority, eta: eta == null ? Infinity : eta, index: index };
    }).sort(function(a, b) {
      return a.priority - b.priority || (a.eta < b.eta ? -1 : a.eta > b.eta ? 1 : 0) || a.index - b.index;
    }).map(function(entry) { return entry.aircraft; });
  };
  var atcWatchMotion = function(ac) {
    if (!atcActiveAircraft(ac) || !atcFinite(ac.x) || !atcFinite(ac.y) || !atcFinite(ac.heading) || !atcFinite(ac.speed) || !atcFinite(ac.altitude)) return null;
    // Sanitized bounds keep corrupted optional snapshots from producing huge
    // projections. Normal aircraft speeds and altitudes are within these bounds.
    var speed = Math.max(0, Math.min(600, ac.speed));
    var altitude = Math.max(0, Math.min(50000, ac.altitude));
    var target = atcFinite(ac.targetAltitude) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(50000, ac.targetAltitude)) : null;
    var rate = atcFinite(ac.verticalRate) ? Math.max(-3000, Math.min(3000, ac.verticalRate)) : target == null ? 0 : Math.sign(target - altitude) * (ac.state === 'approach' ? 1800 : 1200);
    var capture = target != null && rate !== 0 && (target - altitude) * rate > 0 ? (target - altitude) / (rate / 60) : Infinity;
    if (target === altitude) rate = 0;
    var turning = ac.state === 'holding' || ac.state === 'goAround' || (ac.state === 'approach' && ac.approachStage !== 'final') || (atcFinite(ac.targetHeading) && Math.abs(headingDifference(ac.targetHeading, ac.heading)) > 1);
    return { aircraft: ac, vx: Math.sin(toRad(ac.heading)) * speed / 3600, vy: -Math.cos(toRad(ac.heading)) * speed / 3600,
      altitude: altitude, target: target, rate: rate / 60, capture: capture, turning: turning,
      altitudeAt: function(seconds) {
        var value = altitude + rate / 60 * Math.min(seconds, capture);
        return Math.max(0, Math.min(50000, value));
      } };
  };
  var atcHorizontalWindow = function(px, py, vx, vy, minimum, horizon) {
    var speedSquared = vx * vx + vy * vy;
    var offset = px * px + py * py - minimum * minimum;
    if (speedSquared < 1e-12) return offset < 0 ? [0, horizon] : null;
    var linear = 2 * (px * vx + py * vy);
    var discriminant = linear * linear - 4 * speedSquared * offset;
    if (discriminant <= 0) return null; // A tangent meets, but does not cross, the boundary.
    var root = Math.sqrt(discriminant);
    var start = Math.max(0, (-linear - root) / (2 * speedSquared));
    var end = Math.min(horizon, (-linear + root) / (2 * speedSquared));
    return end - start > 1e-8 ? [start, end] : null;
  };
  var atcSimultaneousWindow = function(a, b, horizontal, verticalMinimum, horizon) {
    if (!horizontal) return null;
    var breaks = [0, horizon];
    [a.capture, b.capture].forEach(function(time) { if (time > 0 && time < horizon) breaks.push(time); });
    breaks.sort(function(x, y) { return x - y; });
    for (var i = 0; i < breaks.length - 1; i++) {
      var start = Math.max(horizontal[0], breaks[i]);
      var end = Math.min(horizontal[1], breaks[i + 1]);
      if (end - start <= 1e-8) continue;
      var z = b.altitudeAt(start) - a.altitudeAt(start);
      var slope = ((b.altitudeAt(end) - a.altitudeAt(end)) - z) / (end - start);
      if (Math.abs(slope) < 1e-9) {
        if (Math.abs(z) < verticalMinimum) return [start, end];
      } else {
        var first = start + (-verticalMinimum - z) / slope;
        var last = start + (verticalMinimum - z) / slope;
        var insideStart = Math.max(start, Math.min(first, last));
        var insideEnd = Math.min(end, Math.max(first, last));
        if (insideEnd - insideStart > 1e-8) return [insideStart, insideEnd];
      }
    }
    return null;
  };
  var separationWatch = function(aircraft, horizonSeconds) {
    var horizon = atcFinite(horizonSeconds) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(180, horizonSeconds)) : 120;
    var motions = (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).slice(0, 32).map(atcWatchMotion).filter(Boolean);
    var result = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < motions.length; i++) for (var j = i + 1; j < motions.length; j++) {
      var a = motions[i], b = motions[j];
      var px = (b.aircraft.x - a.aircraft.x) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM;
      var py = (b.aircraft.y - a.aircraft.y) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM;
      var vx = b.vx - a.vx, vy = b.vy - a.vy;
      var distance = Math.hypot(px, py);
      var vertical = Math.abs(b.altitude - a.altitude);
      var heavy = function(ac) { return ac.type && (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super'); };
      var minimum = heavy(a.aircraft) || heavy(b.aircraft) ? 6 : 3;
      var speedSquared = vx * vx + vy * vy;
      var closestTime = speedSquared < 1e-12 ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(horizon, -(px * vx + py * vy) / speedSquared));
      var closestDistance = Math.hypot(px + vx * closestTime, py + vy * closestTime);
      var closing = distance > 1e-8 ? -(px * vx + py * vy) / distance * 3600 : 0;
      var conflict = distance < minimum && vertical < 1000;
      var breach = atcSimultaneousWindow(a, b, atcHorizontalWindow(px, py, vx, vy, minimum, horizon), 1000, horizon);
      var narrowingVertically = Math.abs(b.altitudeAt(horizon) - a.altitudeAt(horizon)) < vertical - 1;
      var caution = (closing > 0.01 || narrowingVertically) && atcSimultaneousWindow(a, b, atcHorizontalWindow(px, py, vx, vy, minimum + 1.5, horizon), 1500, horizon);
      if (!conflict && !breach && !caution) continue;
      result.push({ a: a.aircraft, b: b.aircraft, severity: conflict ? 'conflict' : breach ? 'projected' : 'caution',
        currentDistanceNm: distance, currentVerticalFt: vertical, minimumNm: minimum, closingKnots: closing,
        closestTimeSeconds: closestTime, closestDistanceNm: closestDistance, closestVerticalFt: Math.abs(b.altitudeAt(closestTime) - a.altitudeAt(closestTime)),
        breachTimeSeconds: conflict ? 0 : breach ? breach[0] : null, horizonSeconds: horizon, turning: a.turning || b.turning });
    }
    var severityOrder = { conflict: 0, projected: 1, caution: 2 };
    result.sort(function(a, b) { return severityOrder[a.severity] - severityOrder[b.severity] || (a.breachTimeSeconds == null ? a.closestTimeSeconds : a.breachTimeSeconds) - (b.breachTimeSeconds == null ? b.closestTimeSeconds : b.breachTimeSeconds); });
    return result;
  };
  var atcArrivalSpacingMinutes = function(lead, follow) {
    var heavy = function(ac) { return ac.type && (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super'); };
    var speed = Math.max(1, Math.min(lead.speed, follow.speed));
    return Math.max(2 * ATC_TIME_ACCELERATION / 60, (heavy(lead) || heavy(follow) ? 6 : 3) / speed * 60);
  };
  var runwayScheduleHints = function(aircraft, airport, busyUntil, time) {
    if (!airport || !Array.isArray(airport.runways)) return [];
    var now = atcFinite(time) ? Math.max(0, time) : 0;
    var busy = busyUntil || {};
    var active = (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).filter(atcActiveAircraft);
    return airport.runways.map(function(rwy) {
      var group = runwayGroup(rwy, airport);
      var until = atcFinite(busy[group]) ? busy[group] : 0;
      var remaining = Math.max(0, until - now);
      // Crossing runways share their arrival queue as well as occupancy.
      var arrivals = active.filter(function(ac) {
        return ac.assignedRunway && airport.runways.indexOf(ac.assignedRunway) !== -1 && runwayGroup(ac.assignedRunway, airport) === group;
      }).map(function(ac, index) { return { aircraft: ac, etaMinutes: atcPlanningEta(ac, ac.assignedRunway), index: index }; }).filter(function(entry) {
        return entry.etaMinutes != null;
      }).sort(function(a, b) { return a.etaMinutes - b.etaMinutes || a.index - b.index; });
      var nearest = arrivals[0] || null;
      var spacing = null, required = null, spacingPair = null;
      for (var i = 1; i < arrivals.length; i++) {
        var lead = arrivals[i - 1], follow = arrivals[i];
        // Four flight minutes correspond to the model's two shift seconds of
        // runway occupancy. The distance-based guide is deliberately simple.
        var guide = atcArrivalSpacingMinutes(lead.aircraft, follow.aircraft);
        var gap = follow.etaMinutes - lead.etaMinutes;
        if (spacing == null || gap - guide < spacing - required) { spacing = gap; required = guide; spacingPair = [lead.aircraft, follow.aircraft]; }
      }
      var arrivalDuringOccupancy = nearest && nearest.etaMinutes * 60 / ATC_TIME_ACCELERATION < remaining;
      return { runway: rwy, group: group, occupied: remaining > 0, occupancySeconds: remaining,
        arrivals: arrivals, next: nearest, spacingMinutes: spacing, requiredSpacingMinutes: required, spacingPair: spacingPair,
        risk: arrivalDuringOccupancy ? 'occupied' : spacing != null && spacing < required ? 'spacing' : null };
    });
  };
  var atcArrivalBoard = function(aircraft, airport, plans) {
    if (!airport || !Array.isArray(airport.runways)) return { groups: [], pending: [], horizonMinutes: 5 };
    var groups = [], estimated = [], maximum = 0;
    (Array.isArray(plans) ? plans : []).forEach(function(plan) {
      if (!plan || airport.runways.indexOf(plan.runway) === -1) return;
      var existing = groups.find(function(group) { return group.id === plan.group; });
      if (existing) { if (existing.runways.indexOf(plan.runway) === -1) existing.runways.push(plan.runway); return; }
      var occupiedMinutes = atcFinite(plan.occupancySeconds) ? Math.max(0, plan.occupancySeconds) * (ATC_TIME_ACCELERATION / 60) : 0;
      if (!atcFinite(occupiedMinutes)) occupiedMinutes = 0;
      var group = { id: plan.group, runways: [plan.runway], occupied: !!plan.occupied, occupancyMinutes: occupiedMinutes, entries: [] };
      (Array.isArray(plan.arrivals) ? plan.arrivals : []).filter(function(entry) {
        return entry && atcActiveAircraft(entry.aircraft) && atcFinite(entry.etaMinutes) && entry.etaMinutes >= 0;
      }).slice().sort(function(a, b) { return a.etaMinutes - b.etaMinutes; }).forEach(function(entry, index) {
        var previous = group.entries[index - 1];
        var gap = previous ? entry.etaMinutes - previous.etaMinutes : null;
        var target = previous ? atcArrivalSpacingMinutes(previous.aircraft, entry.aircraft) : null;
        group.entries.push({ aircraft: entry.aircraft, etaMinutes: entry.etaMinutes, order: index + 1,
          gapMinutes: gap, requiredGapMinutes: target, spacingConcern: previous ? gap < target : false,
          occupancyConcern: entry.etaMinutes < occupiedMinutes });
        estimated.push(entry.aircraft);
        maximum = Math.max(maximum, entry.etaMinutes);
      });
      maximum = Math.max(maximum, occupiedMinutes);
      groups.push(group);
    });
    var pending = (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).filter(function(ac) { return atcActiveAircraft(ac) && estimated.indexOf(ac) === -1; }).map(function(ac) {
      var assigned = airport.runways.indexOf(ac.assignedRunway) !== -1;
      return { aircraft: ac, reason: ac.state === 'holding' ? 'holding' : ac.state === 'goAround' ? 'goAround' : !assigned ? 'unassigned' : 'unavailable' };
    });
    return { groups: groups, pending: pending, horizonMinutes: Math.max(5, Math.ceil(maximum / 5) * 5) };
  };
  // ── END ATC SEQUENCING AND SHORT-HORIZON WATCH ──
  var aircraftSeparation = function(a, b) {
    var distance = distNm(a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM;
    var minimum = (a.type.cat === 'Heavy' || a.type.cat === 'Super' || b.type.cat === 'Heavy' || b.type.cat === 'Super') ? 6 : 3;
    return { dist: distance, min: minimum, conflict: distance < minimum && Math.abs(a.altitude - b.altitude) < 1000 };
  };
  var aircraftCanLand = function(ac, rwy) {
    if (ac.state !== 'approach' || ac.approachStage !== 'final' || !rwy || ac.altitude > 350) return false;
    var geometry = runwayGeometry(rwy);
    var close = distNm(ac.x, ac.y, geometry.threshold.x, geometry.threshold.y) < 3;
    return close && Math.abs(headingDifference(ac.heading, rwy.hdg)) < 20;
  };
  var advanceAircraft = function(ac, elapsedSeconds) {
    var remaining = Math.max(0, elapsedSeconds) * ATC_TIME_ACCELERATION;
    // Small flight-time steps preserve the same turns and captures at any FPS.
    while (remaining > 0.000001 && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed') {
      var dt = Math.min(1, remaining);
      remaining -= dt;
      var turnRate = 3;
      if (ac.state === 'approach' && ac.assignedRunway) {
        var geometry = runwayGeometry(ac.assignedRunway);
        if (!ac.approachStage) ac.approachStage = 'entry';
        var entryDistance = distNm(ac.x, ac.y, geometry.finalEntry.x, geometry.finalEntry.y);
        if (ac.approachStage === 'entry' && entryDistance < 3) ac.approachStage = 'final';
        var waypoint = ac.approachStage === 'final' ? geometry.threshold : geometry.finalEntry;
        ac.targetHeading = bearingDeg(ac.x, ac.y, waypoint.x, waypoint.y);
        if (ac.approachStage === 'final') {
          var finalDistance = aircraftDistanceNm(ac, ac.assignedRunway);
          ac.targetAltitude = Math.max(0, finalDistance * 318);
        } else ac.targetAltitude = 3800;
        // Localizer guidance uses a tighter training turn than manual vectors.
        turnRate = 6;
      } else if (ac.state === 'holding') {
        var holdRadius = 24;
        if (!ac.holdCenter) {
          ac.holdCenter = {
            x: Math.max(holdRadius + 12, Math.min(388 - holdRadius, ac.x + Math.cos(toRad(ac.heading)) * holdRadius)),
            y: Math.max(holdRadius + 12, Math.min(388 - holdRadius, ac.y + Math.sin(toRad(ac.heading)) * holdRadius))
          };
        }
        var holdAngle = Math.atan2(ac.y - ac.holdCenter.y, ac.x - ac.holdCenter.x) + Math.PI / 6;
        ac.targetHeading = bearingDeg(ac.x, ac.y, ac.holdCenter.x + Math.cos(holdAngle) * holdRadius, ac.holdCenter.y + Math.sin(holdAngle) * holdRadius);
        turnRate = 6;
      }
      if (ac.targetHeading != null) {
        var turn = headingDifference(normalizeHeading(ac.targetHeading), ac.heading);
        ac.heading = normalizeHeading(ac.heading + Math.max(-turnRate * dt, Math.min(turnRate * dt, turn)));
        if (Math.abs(turn) <= turnRate * dt && ac.state !== 'approach' && ac.state !== 'holding') ac.targetHeading = null;
      }
      if (ac.targetSpeed != null) {
        ac.speed = moveToward(ac.speed, ac.targetSpeed, 2 * dt);
        if (Math.abs(ac.speed - ac.targetSpeed) < 0.001) ac.targetSpeed = null;
      }
      if (ac.targetAltitude != null) {
        var verticalRate = ac.state === 'approach' ? 1800 : 1200;
        ac.altitude = moveToward(ac.altitude, ac.targetAltitude, verticalRate / 60 * dt);
      }
      var distance = ac.speed / 3600 * ATC_UNITS_PER_NM * dt;
      ac.x += Math.sin(toRad(ac.heading)) * distance;
      ac.y -= Math.cos(toRad(ac.heading)) * distance;
      ac.fuel = Math.max(0, ac.fuel - dt / 60);
      // Stop on capture so a long frame cannot fly through the threshold.
      if (aircraftCanLand(ac, ac.assignedRunway)) { ac.touchdownReady = true; break; }
    }
  };

  // Radar labels use CSS pixels so their reading size does not shrink with airspace scale.
  var atcRadarCallsign = function(ac) {
    var name = ac && typeof ac.callsign === 'string' ? ac.callsign.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : '';
    return name || 'Flight ' + (ac && ac.id != null ? ac.id : 'unknown');
  };
  var atcRadarTagLayout = function(aircraft, width, height, selectedId, conflictIds) {
    if (typeof width !== 'number' || typeof height !== 'number' || !isFinite(width) || !isFinite(height) || width <= 0 || height <= 0) return [];
    conflictIds = Array.isArray(conflictIds) ? conflictIds : [];
    var padding = Math.min(6, width / 4, height / 4);
    var scale = Math.min(width, height) / 400;
    var used = [];
    var tracks = (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).filter(function(ac) {
      return ac && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed' && typeof ac.x === 'number' && typeof ac.y === 'number' && isFinite(ac.x) && isFinite(ac.y);
    }).map(function(ac, index) {
      var selected = ac.id === selectedId;
      var conflict = conflictIds.indexOf(ac.id) !== -1;
      var priority = !!((ac.emergency && ac.emergency.priority !== false) || ac.fuel < 5);
      return { ac: ac, index: index, selected: selected, conflict: conflict, priority: priority, rank: selected ? 3 : priority ? 2 : conflict ? 1 : 0 };
    });
    // Important tracks reserve room first, then draw last above ordinary traffic.
    tracks.sort(function(a, b) { return b.rank - a.rank || a.index - b.index; });
    var tags = tracks.map(function(track) {
      var name = atcRadarCallsign(track.ac);
      var w = Math.min(Math.max(track.selected ? 156 : 136, name.length * 7 + 18), width - padding * 2);
      var h = Math.min(54, height - padding * 2);
      var ax = track.ac.x * scale, ay = track.ac.y * scale;
      var candidates = [[ax + 14, ay - h / 2], [ax - w - 14, ay - h / 2], [ax + 14, ay + 14], [ax - w - 14, ay + 14], [ax - w / 2, ay - h - 14], [ax - w / 2, ay + 14]];
      [padding, Math.max(padding, width - padding - w), Math.max(padding, (width - w) / 2)].forEach(function(x) {
        for (var y = padding; y <= height - padding - h; y += h + 6) candidates.push([x, y]);
        candidates.push([x, height - padding - h]);
      });
      var best = null, bestCost = Infinity;
      candidates.forEach(function(candidate) {
        var x = Math.max(padding, Math.min(width - padding - w, candidate[0]));
        var y = Math.max(padding, Math.min(height - padding - h, candidate[1]));
        var overlap = used.reduce(function(sum, other) {
          return sum + Math.max(0, Math.min(x + w + 3, other.x + other.width + 3) - Math.max(x - 3, other.x - 3)) * Math.max(0, Math.min(y + h + 3, other.y + other.height + 3) - Math.max(y - 3, other.y - 3));
        }, 0);
        var dx = x + w / 2 - ax, dy = y + h / 2 - ay;
        var cost = overlap * 1000000 + dx * dx + dy * dy;
        if (cost < bestCost) { bestCost = cost; best = { x: x, y: y }; }
      });
      var tag = { id: track.ac.id, x: best.x, y: best.y, width: w, height: h, anchorX: ax, anchorY: ay,
        selected: track.selected, priority: track.priority, conflict: track.conflict, callsign: name, rank: track.rank, index: track.index };
      used.push(tag);
      return tag;
    });
    return tags.sort(function(a, b) { return a.rank - b.rank || a.index - b.index; });
  };

  // Readbacks and draft previews describe instructions without applying them.
  var atcAircraftReadback = function(ac) {
    if (!atcActiveAircraft(ac) || !atcFinite(ac.heading) || !atcFinite(ac.altitude) || ac.altitude < 0 || !atcFinite(ac.speed) || ac.speed <= 0) return null;
    var approach = ac.state === 'approach', hold = ac.state === 'holding';
    return { mode: approach ? 'approach' : hold ? 'hold' : ac.state === 'goAround' ? 'goAround' : 'manual', rows: [
      { id: 'heading', current: normalizeHeading(ac.heading), target: atcFinite(ac.targetHeading) ? normalizeHeading(ac.targetHeading) : null, automatic: approach || hold },
      { id: 'altitude', current: ac.altitude, target: atcFinite(ac.targetAltitude) ? ac.targetAltitude : null, automatic: approach },
      { id: 'speed', current: ac.speed, target: atcFinite(ac.targetSpeed) ? ac.targetSpeed : null, automatic: false }
    ] };
  };
  var atcTrafficFrozen = function(game, hidden) {
    return !!(hidden || game && (game.gameOver || game.paused || game.showHelp || game.commandEntry || game.mathChallenge));
  };
  var atcImmediateCommandInfo = function(ac, key, airport, runwayIndex) {
    if (!atcActiveAircraft(ac) || !atcFinite(ac.altitude) || ac.altitude < 0 || ['d', 'u', 'p', 'r'].indexOf(key) === -1) return null;
    var approach = ac.state === 'approach';
    var info = { key: key, cancelsApproach: false, releasesHold: false, targetAltitude: null, nextRunway: null, nextState: ac.state, unchanged: false };
    if (key === 'd' || key === 'u') {
      var base = approach || !atcFinite(ac.targetAltitude) ? ac.altitude : ac.targetAltitude;
      info.targetAltitude = Math.max(1000, Math.min(45000, base + (key === 'd' ? -1000 : 1000)));
      info.cancelsApproach = approach;
      info.nextState = approach ? 'inbound' : ac.state;
      info.unchanged = !approach && info.targetAltitude === ac.targetAltitude;
    } else if (key === 'p') {
      info.releasesHold = ac.state === 'holding';
      info.nextState = info.releasesHold ? 'inbound' : 'holding';
      info.cancelsApproach = approach;
      info.targetAltitude = approach ? ac.altitude : null;
    } else {
      if (!airport || !Array.isArray(airport.runways) || !airport.runways.length) return null;
      info.nextRunway = Number.isInteger(runwayIndex) ? airport.runways[runwayIndex] || null : airport.runways[(airport.runways.indexOf(ac.assignedRunway) + 1) % airport.runways.length];
      info.unchanged = info.nextRunway === ac.assignedRunway;
      info.cancelsApproach = approach && !info.unchanged;
      info.targetAltitude = info.cancelsApproach ? ac.altitude : null;
      info.nextState = info.cancelsApproach ? 'inbound' : ac.state;
    }
    return info;
  };
  var atcTrafficReview = function(aircraft, watch, runwayPlan) {
    var counts = { conflict: 0, projected: 0, caution: 0 };
    (Array.isArray(watch) ? watch : []).forEach(function(pair) { if (pair && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(counts, pair.severity)) counts[pair.severity]++; });
    var areas = [];
    (Array.isArray(runwayPlan) ? runwayPlan : []).forEach(function(plan) {
      if (!plan || typeof plan.group !== 'string' || ['occupied', 'spacing'].indexOf(plan.risk) === -1) return;
      var existing = areas.find(function(area) { return area.group === plan.group; });
      if (!existing) areas.push({ group: plan.group, risk: plan.risk });
      else if (plan.risk === 'occupied') existing.risk = 'occupied';
    });
    var priority = (Array.isArray(aircraft) ? aircraft : []).filter(function(ac) { return atcActiveAircraft(ac) && (ac.emergency && ac.emergency.priority !== false || atcFinite(ac.fuel) && ac.fuel < 5); });
    return { spacingCounts: counts, spacingTotal: counts.conflict + counts.projected + counts.caution, runwayAreas: areas, priorityAircraft: priority };
  };
  var atcCommandPreview = function(ac, kind, value, airport) {
    if (!atcAircraftReadback(ac) || (kind !== 'heading' && kind !== 'speed') || (typeof value !== 'number' && typeof value !== 'string') || !String(value).trim()) return null;
    var proposed = Number(value);
    if (!isFinite(proposed) || !Number.isInteger(proposed) || (kind === 'heading' ? proposed < 0 || proposed > 360 : proposed < 60 || proposed > 250)) return null;
    if (kind === 'heading') proposed = normalizeHeading(proposed);
    var current = kind === 'heading' ? normalizeHeading(ac.heading) : ac.speed;
    var delta = kind === 'heading' ? headingDifference(proposed, current) : proposed - current;
    var result = { kind: kind, value: proposed, current: current, delta: delta,
      cancelsApproach: kind === 'heading' && ac.state === 'approach', releasesHold: kind === 'heading' && ac.state === 'holding',
      maintainAltitude: kind === 'heading' && ac.state === 'approach' ? ac.altitude : null,
      turn: kind === 'heading' ? (delta < 0 ? 'left' : delta > 0 ? 'right' : 'straight') : null,
      turnDegrees: kind === 'heading' ? Math.abs(delta) : null, distanceNm: null, currentEtaMinutes: null, proposedEtaMinutes: null };
    var rwy = ac.assignedRunway || (airport && Array.isArray(airport.runways) && airport.runways[0]);
    if (kind === 'speed' && ac.state !== 'holding' && ac.state !== 'goAround' && rwy && ['x', 'y', 'hdg', 'len'].every(function(key) { return atcFinite(rwy[key]); }) && atcFinite(ac.x) && atcFinite(ac.y)) {
      result.distanceNm = aircraftDistanceNm(ac, rwy);
      result.currentEtaMinutes = result.distanceNm / ac.speed * 60;
      result.proposedEtaMinutes = result.distanceNm / proposed * 60;
    }
    return result;
  };

  // ── AIRPORT CONFIGURATIONS ──
  var AIRPORTS = [
    { id: 'simple', name: 'Smallville Regional', code: 'SVR', runways: [{ hdg: 270, x: 150, y: 200, len: 40 }], difficulty: 'Beginner', desc: '1 runway, low traffic. Learn the basics.', maxTraffic: 4, spawnRate: 8 },
    { id: 'parallel', name: 'Metro International', code: 'MIA', runways: [{ hdg: 270, x: 150, y: 170, len: 40 }, { hdg: 270, x: 150, y: 230, len: 40 }], difficulty: 'Intermediate', desc: '2 parallel runways. Manage simultaneous approaches.', maxTraffic: 6, spawnRate: 6 },
    { id: 'crossing', name: 'Crosswind City', code: 'XWC', runways: [{ hdg: 270, x: 150, y: 200, len: 40 }, { hdg: 180, x: 150, y: 200, len: 40 }], difficulty: 'Advanced', desc: 'Crossing runways! Sequences must be conflict-free.', maxTraffic: 8, spawnRate: 5 },
    { id: 'busy', name: "O'Hare Challenge", code: 'ORD', runways: [{ hdg: 270, x: 120, y: 160, len: 35 }, { hdg: 270, x: 120, y: 200, len: 35 }, { hdg: 270, x: 120, y: 240, len: 35 }, { hdg: 320, x: 180, y: 120, len: 35 }], difficulty: 'Expert', desc: '4 runways, heavy traffic. Can you handle the pressure?', maxTraffic: 12, spawnRate: 3 },
  ];

  // ── AIRCRAFT TYPES ──
  var AC_TYPES = [
    { id: 'C172', name: 'Cessna 172', cat: 'Light', speed: 90, icon: '🛩️', color: '#60a5fa', size: 4 },
    { id: 'B738', name: 'Boeing 737', cat: 'Medium', speed: 140, icon: '✈️', color: '#fbbf24', size: 6 },
    { id: 'A320', name: 'Airbus A320', cat: 'Medium', speed: 135, icon: '✈️', color: '#4ade80', size: 6 },
    { id: 'B77W', name: 'Boeing 777', cat: 'Heavy', speed: 145, icon: '🛫', color: '#f97316', size: 8 },
    { id: 'A388', name: 'Airbus A380', cat: 'Super', speed: 140, icon: '🛫', color: '#ef4444', size: 10 },
  ];

  // ── ATC PURPOSEFUL PRACTICE ──
  // Exercises use the shipped accelerated teaching model. They begin paused,
  // contain only the specified aircraft, and do not add random arrivals.
  var ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS = [
    {
      id: 'first_landing', airportId: 'simple', title: 'Guide one arrival',
      briefing: 'Select TRAIN101, assign runway 27, then clear its approach. Resume to watch automatic approach guidance capture final and descend. Pause whenever you want to inspect its distance, altitude, or fuel.',
      hint: 'Approach clearance needs an assigned runway. The aircraft begins aligned with the runway and has ample fuel.',
      expectedOrder: [101],
      aircraft: [{ id: 101, callsign: 'TRAIN101', typeId: 'C172', x: 275, y: 200, heading: 270, speed: 90, altitude: 5000, fuel: 80, runwayIndex: -1 }]
    },
    {
      id: 'arrival_sequence', airportId: 'simple', title: 'Compare distance and speed',
      briefing: 'NEAR101 is closer, but FAST102 is faster. Compare distance ÷ speed before choosing the arrival order. Land FAST102 first, then NEAR101, with enough spacing for this model’s protected runway area.',
      hint: 'The initial direct estimates are about 18.6 flight minutes for FAST102 and 20.0 for NEAR101. Hold NEAR101 while FAST102 approaches, then release it after the runway area is clear. Holds change the estimates.',
      expectedOrder: [102, 101],
      aircraft: [
        { id: 101, callsign: 'NEAR101', typeId: 'C172', x: 260, y: 200, heading: 270, speed: 90, altitude: 9000, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: 0 },
        { id: 102, callsign: 'FAST102', typeId: 'B738', x: 300, y: 200, heading: 270, speed: 140, altitude: 5500, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: 0 }
      ]
    },
    {
      id: 'separation_recovery', airportId: 'parallel', title: 'Protect converging traffic',
      briefing: 'SOUTH101 and EAST102 are at the same altitude on converging courses. Before resuming, assign one aircraft a different altitude. Maintain at least 1,000 feet of vertical separation through six shift seconds without a model separation alert.',
      hint: 'Select either aircraft and raise its altitude target by 1,000 feet. Altitude changes happen progressively. Watch the two tracks cross while their altitude difference preserves the model’s separation.',
      expectedOrder: [],
      aircraft: [
        { id: 101, callsign: 'SOUTH101', typeId: 'B738', x: 260, y: 170, heading: 180, speed: 140, altitude: 5000, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: -1 },
        { id: 102, callsign: 'EAST102', typeId: 'B738', x: 200, y: 230, heading: 90, speed: 140, altitude: 5000, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: -1 }
      ]
    },
    {
      id: 'crossing_priority', airportId: 'crossing', title: 'Prioritize across crossing runways',
      briefing: 'MED101 requests medical priority on runway 18. ROUT102 is assigned runway 27. These runways share one protected movement area in this model. Land MED101 first, then ROUT102 after the area clears.',
      hint: 'Hold ROUT102 while MED101 approaches. After MED101 lands, wait for the shared area to clear before releasing ROUT102. Priority changes the sequence while separation remains necessary.',
      expectedOrder: [101, 102],
      aircraft: [
        { id: 101, callsign: 'MED101', typeId: 'B738', x: 150, y: 90, heading: 180, speed: 140, altitude: 6000, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: 1, emergency: true },
        { id: 102, callsign: 'ROUT102', typeId: 'A320', x: 270, y: 200, heading: 270, speed: 135, altitude: 6000, fuel: 100, runwayIndex: 0 }
      ]
    }
  ];
  var atcPracticeSnapshot = function(practice) {
    if (!practice || practice.version !== 1) return null;
    var scenario = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === practice.id; });
    if (!scenario) return null;
    var allowed = scenario.aircraft.map(function(ac) { return ac.id; });
    var ids = function(values) {
      return (Array.isArray(values) ? values : []).slice(0, 12).filter(function(id, index, all) { return allowed.indexOf(id) !== -1 && all.indexOf(id) === index; });
    };
    return { version: 1, id: scenario.id, landedIds: ids(practice.landedIds), lostIds: ids(practice.lostIds),
      completed: practice.completed === true,
      completedAt: typeof practice.completedAt === 'number' && Number.isFinite(practice.completedAt) ? Math.max(0, Math.min(86400, practice.completedAt)) : null };
  };
  var createPracticeShift = function(scenarioId, shiftId) {
    var scenario = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === scenarioId; });
    if (!scenario) return null;
    var airport = AIRPORTS.find(function(item) { return item.id === scenario.airportId; });
    if (!airport) return null;
    var aircraft = scenario.aircraft.map(function(seed) {
      var type = AC_TYPES.find(function(item) { return item.id === seed.typeId; });
      return { id: seed.id, callsign: seed.callsign, type: type, x: seed.x, y: seed.y, heading: seed.heading,
        speed: seed.speed, altitude: seed.altitude, fuel: seed.fuel, spawnTime: 0,
        state: 'inbound', assignedRunway: airport.runways[seed.runwayIndex] || null,
        targetHeading: null, targetSpeed: null, targetAltitude: null, approachStage: null,
        touchdownReady: false, fuelWarningIssued: false, holdCenter: null,
        emergency: seed.emergency ? { type: 'medical', label: 'MEDICAL PRIORITY', msg: 'requests priority for a medical situation.', priority: true,
          lesson: 'In this exercise, give MED101 priority while protecting separation and the shared runway area.' } : null };
    });
    return { shiftId: typeof shiftId === 'string' && /^[\w-]{1,80}$/.test(shiftId) ? shiftId : 'atc-practice-' + Date.now().toString(36),
      airport: airport, trainingMode: 'guided', aircraft: aircraft, selected: aircraft[0].id,
      time: 0, score: 0, landed: 0, conflicts: 0, conflictEpisodes: 0, conflictPairKeys: {},
      streak: 0, sessionBestStreak: 0, goArounds: 0, mathCorrect: 0, landedHeavy: false,
      gameOver: false, paused: true, committed: false, showHelp: false, commandEntry: null,
      wind: { dir: airport.runways[0].hdg, speed: 0 }, nextSpawn: airport.spawnRate,
      messages: [{ text: scenario.briefing, time: 0, color: '#60a5fa' }], decisionLog: [],
      mathChallenge: null, runwayBusyUntil: {},
      practice: { version: 1, id: scenario.id, landedIds: [], lostIds: [], completed: false, completedAt: null } };
  };
  var recordPracticeOutcome = function(game, event, ac) {
    var practice = atcPracticeSnapshot(game && game.practice);
    if (!practice || !ac || (event !== 'landing' && event !== 'lost')) return;
    var scenario = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === practice.id; });
    if (!scenario.aircraft.some(function(seed) { return seed.id === ac.id; })) return;
    var field = event === 'landing' ? 'landedIds' : 'lostIds';
    if (practice[field].indexOf(ac.id) === -1) practice[field].push(ac.id);
    game.practice = practice;
  };
  var scenarioProgress = function(game) {
    var practice = atcPracticeSnapshot(game && game.practice);
    if (!practice) return null;
    var scenario = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === practice.id; });
    var noConflicts = !(game.conflictEpisodes > 0 || game.conflicts > 0);
    var noLosses = practice.lostIds.length === 0;
    var checks = [];
    var wrongOrder = false;
    var spacing = scenario.id === 'arrival_sequence' || scenario.id === 'crossing_priority';
    if (scenario.id === 'separation_recovery') {
      var roster = scenario.aircraft.map(function(seed) { return (game.aircraft || []).find(function(ac) { return ac.id === seed.id && atcActiveAircraft(ac); }); });
      var validAltitudes = roster.every(function(ac) { return ac && atcFinite(ac.altitude) && ac.altitude >= 0; });
      var vertical = validAltitudes ? Math.abs(roster[0].altitude - roster[1].altitude) : 0;
      checks.push({ id: 'vertical', label: 'Build at least 1,000 feet of vertical separation.', done: vertical >= 1000 });
      checks.push({ id: 'time', label: 'Protect traffic through six shift seconds.', done: atcFinite(game.time) && game.time >= 6 });
    } else {
      var landed = scenario.expectedOrder.every(function(id) { return practice.landedIds.indexOf(id) !== -1; });
      checks.push({ id: 'landings', label: scenario.expectedOrder.length === 1 ? 'Land TRAIN101.' : 'Land both exercise aircraft.', done: landed });
      if (scenario.expectedOrder.length > 1) {
        wrongOrder = practice.landedIds.some(function(id, index) { return id !== scenario.expectedOrder[index]; });
        checks.push({ id: 'order', label: scenario.id === 'arrival_sequence' ? 'Land FAST102 before NEAR101.' : 'Land MED101 before ROUT102.', done: landed && !wrongOrder });
      }
    }
    checks.push({ id: 'separation', label: 'Maintain the model’s separation without an alert.', done: noConflicts });
    checks.push({ id: 'traffic', label: 'Keep every exercise aircraft in the exercise until its objective is met.', done: noLosses });
    if (spacing) checks.push({ id: 'spacing', label: 'Complete the sequence without a go-around.', done: !(game.goArounds > 0) });
    var failed = !noConflicts || !noLosses || wrongOrder || (spacing && game.goArounds > 0);
    var complete = !failed && checks.every(function(check) { return check.done; });
    var feedback = complete ? 'Exercise complete. Review your decisions or end the shift to save your debrief.' :
      !noLosses ? 'An exercise aircraft left before meeting its objective. Retry with a new plan; your previous decisions are available in the debrief.' :
      !noConflicts ? 'A model separation alert occurred. Retry and act earlier while the exercise is paused.' :
      wrongOrder ? 'The arrival order differed from the exercise objective. Retry and compare the priority and arrival estimates before clearing approaches.' :
      spacing && game.goArounds > 0 ? 'A go-around provides a safe recovery. Retry this spacing exercise with more time between arrivals to meet its no-go-around objective.' : scenario.hint;
    return { scenario: scenario, checks: checks, complete: complete, failed: failed, feedback: feedback };
  };
  // Coaching follows the exercise's measured objective. Pending instructions
  // explain a plan, but only actual altitude and elapsed time count as evidence.
  var atcPracticeDecisionState = function(game) {
    var progress = scenarioProgress(game);
    if (!progress) return null;
    var scenario = progress.scenario;
    var result = { id: scenario.id, kind: 'arrival', verticalFeet: null, pendingVerticalFeet: null,
      shiftSeconds: game && atcFinite(game.time) ? Math.max(0, game.time) : 0, nextCallsign: null };
    if (progress.failed) { result.kind = 'review'; return result; }
    if (progress.complete) { result.kind = 'complete'; return result; }
    var active = (game.aircraft || []).filter(atcActiveAircraft);
    if (scenario.id === 'separation_recovery') {
      var roster = scenario.aircraft.map(function(seed) { return active.find(function(ac) { return ac.id === seed.id; }); });
      if (roster.some(function(ac) { return !ac || !atcFinite(ac.altitude) || ac.altitude < 0; })) { result.kind = 'unavailable'; return result; }
      result.verticalFeet = Math.abs(roster[0].altitude - roster[1].altitude);
      var manual = roster.every(function(ac) { return ac.state !== 'approach' && (ac.targetAltitude == null || (atcFinite(ac.targetAltitude) && ac.targetAltitude >= 0)); });
      if (manual) {
        var targets = roster.map(function(ac) { return ac.targetAltitude == null ? ac.altitude : ac.targetAltitude; });
        result.pendingVerticalFeet = Math.abs(targets[0] - targets[1]);
      }
      result.kind = result.verticalFeet >= 1000 ? 'vertical-watch' : result.pendingVerticalFeet >= 1000 ? 'vertical-pending' : 'vertical-plan';
    } else if (scenario.expectedOrder.length > 1) {
      var practice = atcPracticeSnapshot(game.practice);
      var nextId = scenario.expectedOrder.find(function(id) { return practice.landedIds.indexOf(id) === -1; });
      var next = active.find(function(ac) { return ac.id === nextId; });
      if (!next) { result.kind = 'unavailable'; return result; }
      result.nextCallsign = next.callsign;
      result.kind = game.selected !== nextId ? 'sequence-wait' : practice.landedIds.length ? 'sequence-next' : 'sequence-first';
    }
    return result;
  };
  var atcExerciseResultSnapshot = function(input) {
    if (!input) return null;
    var id = input.scenario ? input.scenario.id : input.id;
    var initial = scenarioProgress(createPracticeShift(id, 'atc-result-schema'));
    if (!initial) return null;
    var checks = initial.checks.map(function(check) {
      var saved = (Array.isArray(input.checks) ? input.checks : []).find(function(item) { return item && item.id === check.id; });
      return { id: check.id, label: check.label, done: !!saved && saved.done === true };
    });
    return { id: id, checks: checks, complete: input.failed !== true && input.complete === true && checks.every(function(check) { return check.done; }),
      failed: input.failed === true, feedback: typeof input.feedback === 'string' ? input.feedback.slice(0, 1500) : initial.feedback };
  };
  // ── END ATC PURPOSEFUL PRACTICE ──

  // Durable, bounded skill evidence complements the eight most recent debriefs.
  var atcPracticeLearning = function(input, history) {
    var raw = input && input.version === 1 && input.records && typeof input.records === 'object' && !Array.isArray(input.records) ? input.records : {};
    var result = { version: 1, records: {} };
    var recent = atcShiftHistory(history);
    ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.forEach(function(scenario) {
      var stored = raw[scenario.id] && typeof raw[scenario.id] === 'object' ? raw[scenario.id] : {};
      var validate = function(value) { var checked = atcExerciseResultSnapshot(value); return checked && checked.id === scenario.id ? checked : null; };
      var completed = validate(stored.completedResult);
      if (!completed || !completed.complete) completed = null;
      var last = validate(stored.lastResult);
      var seen = {};
      var matching = recent.filter(function(item) {
        if (seen[item.shiftId] || !item.practice || item.practice.id !== scenario.id || item.airportId !== scenario.airportId || !item.exerciseResult || item.exerciseResult.id !== scenario.id) return false;
        seen[item.shiftId] = true; return true;
      });
      matching.forEach(function(item) { if (!completed && item.exerciseResult.complete) completed = validate(item.exerciseResult); });
      if (!last && matching.length) last = validate(matching[0].exerciseResult);
      result.records[scenario.id] = {
        attempts: Math.max(Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(stored.attempts, 0, 100000, 0)), matching.length, completed ? 1 : 0),
        completedResult: completed, lastResult: last,
        lastShiftId: typeof stored.lastShiftId === 'string' && /^[\w-]{1,80}$/.test(stored.lastShiftId) ? stored.lastShiftId : matching.length ? matching[0].shiftId : null
      };
    });
    return result;
  };
  var atcRecordPracticeLearning = function(input, history, summary) {
    var learning = atcPracticeLearning(input, history);
    var scenario = summary.practice && ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === summary.practice.id && item.airportId === summary.airportId; });
    var evidence = atcExerciseResultSnapshot(summary.exerciseResult);
    if (!scenario || !evidence || evidence.id !== scenario.id) return learning;
    var record = learning.records[scenario.id];
    if (record.lastShiftId === summary.shiftId) return learning;
    record.attempts = Math.min(100000, record.attempts + 1);
    record.lastResult = evidence;
    record.lastShiftId = summary.shiftId;
    if (evidence.complete) record.completedResult = evidence;
    return learning;
  };
  var atcPracticePathway = function(data) {
    data = data || {};
    var learning = atcPracticeLearning(data.practiceLearning, data.shiftHistory);
    var history = atcShiftHistory(data.shiftHistory);
    var entries = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.map(function(scenario) {
      var record = learning.records[scenario.id];
      var complete = !!record.completedResult;
      var lastShift = history.find(function(item) { return item.shiftId === record.lastShiftId && item.airportId === scenario.airportId && item.practice && item.practice.id === scenario.id && item.exerciseResult && item.exerciseResult.id === scenario.id; }) || null;
      var note = lastShift && data.shiftReflections && data.shiftReflections[lastShift.shiftId];
      return { scenario: scenario, id: scenario.id, attempts: record.attempts, complete: complete, state: complete ? 'complete' : record.attempts ? 'attempted' : 'new',
        lastResult: record.lastResult, lastShift: lastShift, hasReflection: typeof note === 'string' && !!note.trim() };
    });
    var next = entries.find(function(entry) { return !entry.complete; });
    return { learning: learning, entries: entries, completedCount: entries.filter(function(entry) { return entry.complete; }).length,
      recommendedId: next ? next.id : null, allCompleted: !next };
  };
  // A carried note belongs to the latest matching attempt, never to unrelated traffic.
  var atcReviewNote = function(input, practiceId, airportId) {
    if (!input || typeof input.sourceShiftId !== 'string' || !/^[\w-]{1,80}$/.test(input.sourceShiftId) ||
        !AIRPORTS.some(function(airport) { return airport.id === airportId; }) || input.airportId !== airportId ||
        input.practiceId !== (practiceId || null) || typeof input.text !== 'string') return null;
    if (practiceId && !ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.some(function(scenario) { return scenario.id === practiceId && scenario.airportId === airportId; })) return null;
    var text = input.text.slice(0, 2000);
    return text.trim() ? { sourceShiftId: input.sourceShiftId, airportId: airportId, practiceId: practiceId || null, text: text } : null;
  };
  var atcPreviousReflection = function(data, practiceId, airportId) {
    data = data || {};
    var previous = atcShiftHistory(data.shiftHistory).find(function(item) {
      return item.airportId === airportId && (practiceId ? item.practice && item.practice.id === practiceId : !item.practice);
    });
    if (!previous) return null;
    return atcReviewNote({ sourceShiftId: previous.shiftId, airportId: previous.airportId, practiceId: previous.practice ? previous.practice.id : null,
      text: data.shiftReflections && data.shiftReflections[previous.shiftId] }, practiceId, airportId);
  };
  var atcPracticeActionProgress = function(progress, practice) {
    var scenario = progress && (progress.scenario || ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === progress.id; }));
    if (!scenario) return { value: 0, max: 1, percent: 0, unit: 'actions' };
    var value = 0, max = 0, unit = 'actions';
    if (scenario.expectedOrder.length) {
      unit = 'landings'; max = scenario.expectedOrder.length;
      var outcomes = atcPracticeSnapshot(practice);
      value = outcomes && outcomes.id === scenario.id ? scenario.expectedOrder.filter(function(id) { return outcomes.landedIds.indexOf(id) !== -1; }).length : (progress.checks.some(function(check) { return check.id === 'landings' && check.done; }) ? max : 0);
    } else {
      var actions = progress.checks.filter(function(check) { return check.id === 'vertical' || check.id === 'time'; });
      max = actions.length || 1; value = actions.filter(function(check) { return check.done; }).length;
    }
    return { value: value, max: max, percent: value / max * 100, unit: unit };
  };

  // ── ATC SHIFT RECOVERY ──
  // Save only the model, never DOM nodes, audio contexts, callbacks, or ctx.
  // Airport/type/runway references are rebuilt from the shipped configurations.
  var ATC_SHIFT_VERSION = 1;
  var atcBoundedNumber = function(value, minimum, maximum, fallback) {
    return typeof value === 'number' && isFinite(value) ? Math.max(minimum, Math.min(maximum, value)) : fallback;
  };
  var atcShortText = function(value, limit) { return typeof value === 'string' ? value.slice(0, limit) : ''; };
  var atcShiftId = function() { return 'atc-' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10); };
  var atcDecisionLog = function(events) {
    return (Array.isArray(events) ? events : []).slice(-40).filter(function(item) { return item && typeof item.text === 'string'; }).map(function(item) {
      return { type: atcShortText(item.type, 32), callsign: atcShortText(item.callsign, 64), text: atcShortText(item.text, 400), time: atcBoundedNumber(item.time, 0, 86400, 0) };
    });
  };
  var atcMathSnapshot = function(challenge) {
    if (!challenge || !['arrival_order', 'separation', 'descent', 'wind_correction'].includes(challenge.type)) return null;
    var nonnegative = function(key) { return typeof challenge[key] === 'number' && isFinite(challenge[key]) && challenge[key] >= 0 && challenge[key] <= 100000; };
    var positive = function(key) { return nonnegative(key) && challenge[key] > 0; };
    var expected;
    if (challenge.type === 'arrival_order') {
      if (!nonnegative('d1') || !nonnegative('d2') || !positive('s1') || !positive('s2')) return null;
      expected = challenge.d1 * challenge.s2 === challenge.d2 * challenge.s1 ? 'TIE' : challenge.d1 * challenge.s2 < challenge.d2 * challenge.s1 ? 'A' : 'B';
    } else if (challenge.type === 'separation') {
      if (!nonnegative('d') || !nonnegative('sep') || !positive('rate') || challenge.d < challenge.sep) return null;
      expected = Math.round((challenge.d - challenge.sep) / challenge.rate * 600) / 10;
    } else if (challenge.type === 'descent') {
      var altitude = nonnegative('altitude') ? challenge.altitude : nonnegative('alt') ? challenge.alt * 100 : null;
      if (altitude == null || !nonnegative('target') || altitude < challenge.target || !positive('dist') || !positive('speed')) return null;
      expected = Math.round((altitude - challenge.target) / (challenge.dist / challenge.speed * 60));
    } else {
      if (!nonnegative('rwyHdg') || !nonnegative('windDir') || challenge.rwyHdg > 360 || challenge.windDir > 360 || !nonnegative('windSpd')) return null;
      expected = Math.round(Math.abs(challenge.windSpd * Math.sin(toRad(challenge.windDir - challenge.rwyHdg))));
    }
    if (typeof expected === 'string' ? challenge.answer !== expected : !isFinite(expected) || Math.abs(challenge.answer - expected) > 1e-8) return null;
    var result = { type: challenge.type };
    ['d1', 's1', 'd2', 's2', 't1', 't2', 'd', 'rate', 'sep', 'alt', 'altitude', 'target', 'dist', 'speed', 'rwyHdg', 'windDir', 'windSpd', 'precision', 'exactAnswer'].forEach(function(key) {
      if (typeof challenge[key] === 'number' && isFinite(challenge[key])) result[key] = atcBoundedNumber(challenge[key], 0, 100000, 0);
    });
    if (challenge.type === 'arrival_order') {
      if (!['A', 'B', 'TIE'].includes(challenge.answer)) return null;
      result.answer = challenge.answer;
    } else {
      if (typeof challenge.answer !== 'number' || !isFinite(challenge.answer) || challenge.answer < 0 || challenge.answer > 100000) return null;
      result.answer = atcBoundedNumber(challenge.answer, 0, 100000, 0);
    }
    result.unit = challenge.type === 'separation' ? 'minutes' : challenge.type === 'descent' ? 'feet per minute' : challenge.type === 'wind_correction' ? 'knots' : '';
    result.explanation = atcShortText(challenge.explanation, 1500);
    result.answered = challenge.answered === true;
    result.skipped = result.answered && challenge.skipped === true;
    result.correct = result.answered && !result.skipped && challenge.correct === true;
    return result;
  };
  var serializeAtcShift = function(game, savedAt) {
    if (!game || !game.airport || game.committed) return null;
    var airport = AIRPORTS.find(function(item) { return item.id === game.airport.id; });
    if (!airport) return null;
    var saved = {
      version: ATC_SHIFT_VERSION, shiftId: atcShortText(game.shiftId, 80), airportId: airport.id,
      savedAt: atcBoundedNumber(savedAt, 0, 9007199254740991, Date.now()),
      trainingMode: game.trainingMode === 'guided' ? 'guided' : 'standard',
      gameOver: !!game.gameOver, landedHeavy: !!game.landedHeavy, selected: game.selected,
      wind: { dir: atcBoundedNumber(game.wind && game.wind.dir, 0, 360, 270), speed: atcBoundedNumber(game.wind && game.wind.speed, 0, 100, 10) },
      runwayBusyUntil: {}, conflictPairKeys: {}, aircraft: [], messages: [],
      mathChallenge: atcMathSnapshot(game.mathChallenge), decisionLog: atcDecisionLog(game.decisionLog), practice: atcPracticeSnapshot(game.practice)
    };
    saved.reviewNote = atcReviewNote(game.reviewNote, saved.practice && saved.practice.id, airport.id);
    saved.reviewNoteDismissed = !!saved.reviewNote && game.reviewNoteDismissed === true;
    if (!saved.shiftId) saved.shiftId = game.shiftId = atcShiftId();
    ['time', 'conflicts'].forEach(function(key) { saved[key] = atcBoundedNumber(game[key], 0, 86400, 0); });
    saved.score = atcBoundedNumber(game.score, -1000000, 1000000, 0);
    ['landed', 'conflictEpisodes', 'streak', 'sessionBestStreak', 'goArounds', 'mathCorrect'].forEach(function(key) { saved[key] = Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(game[key], 0, 100000, 0)); });
    saved.nextSpawn = atcBoundedNumber(game.nextSpawn, 0, 120, airport.spawnRate);
    airport.runways.forEach(function(rwy) {
      var key = runwayGroup(rwy, airport);
      saved.runwayBusyUntil[key] = atcBoundedNumber(game.runwayBusyUntil && game.runwayBusyUntil[key], 0, saved.time + 2, 0);
    });
    (Array.isArray(game.aircraft) ? game.aircraft : []).filter(function(ac) { return ac && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed'; }).slice(0, airport.maxTraffic).forEach(function(ac) {
      if (!ac.type || !AC_TYPES.some(function(type) { return type.id === ac.type.id; }) || typeof ac.id !== 'number' || !isFinite(ac.id)) return;
      var item = { id: ac.id, typeId: ac.type.id, callsign: atcShortText(ac.callsign, 64), state: ac.state,
        runwayIndex: airport.runways.indexOf(ac.assignedRunway), approachStage: ac.approachStage === 'final' ? 'final' : 'entry',
        touchdownReady: !!ac.touchdownReady, fuelWarningIssued: !!ac.fuelWarningIssued };
      ['x', 'y', 'heading', 'speed', 'altitude', 'fuel', 'spawnTime', 'targetHeading', 'targetSpeed', 'targetAltitude'].forEach(function(key) { item[key] = ac[key]; });
      if (ac.holdCenter) item.holdCenter = { x: ac.holdCenter.x, y: ac.holdCenter.y };
      if (ac.emergency) item.emergency = { type: atcShortText(ac.emergency.type, 24), label: atcShortText(ac.emergency.label, 64), msg: atcShortText(ac.emergency.msg, 240), lesson: atcShortText(ac.emergency.lesson, 1000), priority: !!ac.emergency.priority };
      saved.aircraft.push(item);
    });
    saved.aircraft.forEach(function(a, index) { saved.aircraft.slice(index + 1).forEach(function(b) {
      var key = [a.id, b.id].sort().join(':');
      if (game.conflictPairKeys && game.conflictPairKeys[key]) saved.conflictPairKeys[key] = true;
    }); });
    saved.messages = (Array.isArray(game.messages) ? game.messages : []).slice(0, 40).filter(function(msg) { return msg && typeof msg.text === 'string'; }).map(function(msg) {
      return { text: atcShortText(msg.text, 1500), time: atcBoundedNumber(msg.time, 0, saved.time, 0), color: /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(msg.color || '') ? msg.color : '#cbd5e1' };
    });
    // Apply the same validation to live checkpoints and restored data.
    var validated = restoreAtcShift(saved);
    if (!validated) return null;
    saved.aircraft = validated.aircraft.map(function(ac) {
      var item = Object.assign({}, ac, { typeId: ac.type.id, runwayIndex: airport.runways.indexOf(ac.assignedRunway) });
      delete item.type; delete item.assignedRunway;
      return item;
    });
    saved.selected = validated.selected;
    return saved;
  };
  var restoreAtcShift = function(saved) {
    if (!saved || saved.version !== ATC_SHIFT_VERSION || typeof saved.shiftId !== 'string' || !/^[\w-]{1,80}$/.test(saved.shiftId) || !Array.isArray(saved.aircraft)) return null;
    var airport = AIRPORTS.find(function(item) { return item.id === saved.airportId; });
    if (!airport || typeof saved.time !== 'number' || !isFinite(saved.time)) return null;
    var game = { shiftId: saved.shiftId, airport: airport, trainingMode: saved.trainingMode === 'guided' ? 'guided' : 'standard',
      aircraft: [], selected: null, paused: true, gameOver: !!saved.gameOver, committed: false,
      showHelp: false, commandEntry: null, runwayBusyUntil: {}, conflictPairKeys: {}, landedHeavy: !!saved.landedHeavy,
      wind: { dir: atcBoundedNumber(saved.wind && saved.wind.dir, 0, 360, 270), speed: atcBoundedNumber(saved.wind && saved.wind.speed, 0, 100, 10) },
      messages: [], mathChallenge: atcMathSnapshot(saved.mathChallenge), decisionLog: atcDecisionLog(saved.decisionLog), practice: atcPracticeSnapshot(saved.practice) };
    if (saved.practice != null && !game.practice) return null;
    if (game.practice && !ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.some(function(item) { return item.id === game.practice.id && item.airportId === airport.id; })) return null;
    game.reviewNote = atcReviewNote(saved.reviewNote, game.practice && game.practice.id, airport.id);
    game.reviewNoteDismissed = !!game.reviewNote && saved.reviewNoteDismissed === true;
    ['time', 'conflicts'].forEach(function(key) { game[key] = atcBoundedNumber(saved[key], 0, 86400, 0); });
    game.score = atcBoundedNumber(saved.score, -1000000, 1000000, 0);
    ['landed', 'conflictEpisodes', 'streak', 'sessionBestStreak', 'goArounds', 'mathCorrect'].forEach(function(key) { game[key] = Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(saved[key], 0, 100000, 0)); });
    game.sessionBestStreak = Math.max(game.streak, game.sessionBestStreak);
    game.nextSpawn = atcBoundedNumber(saved.nextSpawn, 0, 120, airport.spawnRate);
    airport.runways.forEach(function(rwy) {
      var key = runwayGroup(rwy, airport);
      game.runwayBusyUntil[key] = atcBoundedNumber(saved.runwayBusyUntil && saved.runwayBusyUntil[key], 0, game.time + 2, 0);
    });
    saved.aircraft.slice(0, airport.maxTraffic).forEach(function(item) {
      if (!item || typeof item.id !== 'number' || !isFinite(item.id) || game.aircraft.some(function(ac) { return ac.id === item.id; }) || !['inbound', 'approach', 'holding', 'goAround'].includes(item.state)) return;
      var type = AC_TYPES.find(function(candidate) { return candidate.id === item.typeId; });
      if (!type || ['x', 'y', 'heading', 'speed', 'altitude', 'fuel'].some(function(key) { return typeof item[key] !== 'number' || !isFinite(item[key]); })) return;
      var rwy = Number.isInteger(item.runwayIndex) && item.runwayIndex >= 0 ? airport.runways[item.runwayIndex] || null : null;
      var ac = { id: item.id, type: type, callsign: atcShortText(item.callsign, 64) || type.id,
        x: atcBoundedNumber(item.x, -100, 500, 200), y: atcBoundedNumber(item.y, -100, 500, 200),
        heading: normalizeHeading(item.heading), speed: atcBoundedNumber(item.speed, 1, 600, type.speed),
        altitude: atcBoundedNumber(item.altitude, 0, 45000, 5000), fuel: atcBoundedNumber(item.fuel, 0, 2400, 30),
        spawnTime: atcBoundedNumber(item.spawnTime, 0, game.time, 0), assignedRunway: rwy,
        state: item.state === 'approach' && !rwy ? 'inbound' : item.state,
        targetHeading: typeof item.targetHeading === 'number' && isFinite(item.targetHeading) ? normalizeHeading(item.targetHeading) : null,
        targetSpeed: item.targetSpeed == null ? null : atcBoundedNumber(item.targetSpeed, 1, 600, null),
        targetAltitude: item.targetAltitude == null ? null : atcBoundedNumber(item.targetAltitude, 0, 45000, null),
        approachStage: item.state === 'approach' && rwy ? (item.approachStage === 'final' ? 'final' : 'entry') : null,
        touchdownReady: !!item.touchdownReady && item.state === 'approach' && !!rwy,
        fuelWarningIssued: !!item.fuelWarningIssued, emergency: null };
      if (item.holdCenter && typeof item.holdCenter.x === 'number' && isFinite(item.holdCenter.x) && typeof item.holdCenter.y === 'number' && isFinite(item.holdCenter.y)) ac.holdCenter = { x: atcBoundedNumber(item.holdCenter.x, 0, 400, 200), y: atcBoundedNumber(item.holdCenter.y, 0, 400, 200) };
      if (item.emergency && ['engine', 'medical', 'birdstrike', 'hydraulic'].includes(item.emergency.type)) ac.emergency = { type: item.emergency.type, label: atcShortText(item.emergency.label, 64), msg: atcShortText(item.emergency.msg, 240), lesson: atcShortText(item.emergency.lesson, 1000), priority: !!item.emergency.priority };
      game.aircraft.push(ac);
    });
    if (game.practice) {
      game.trainingMode = 'guided';
      var seeds = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === game.practice.id; }).aircraft;
      var roster = seeds.map(function(ac) { return ac.id; });
      if (game.aircraft.some(function(ac) { return !seeds.some(function(seed) { return seed.id === ac.id && seed.typeId === ac.type.id; }); }) || roster.some(function(id) {
        return !game.aircraft.some(function(ac) { return ac.id === id; }) && game.practice.landedIds.indexOf(id) === -1 && game.practice.lostIds.indexOf(id) === -1;
      })) return null;
    }
    if (game.practice && !scenarioProgress(game).complete) {
      game.practice.completed = false;
      game.practice.completedAt = null;
    }
    game.selected = game.aircraft.some(function(ac) { return ac.id === saved.selected; }) ? saved.selected : null;
    game.aircraft.forEach(function(a, index) { game.aircraft.slice(index + 1).forEach(function(b) {
      var key = [a.id, b.id].sort().join(':');
      if (saved.conflictPairKeys && saved.conflictPairKeys[key] === true) game.conflictPairKeys[key] = true;
    }); });
    game.messages = (Array.isArray(saved.messages) ? saved.messages : []).slice(0, 40).filter(function(msg) { return msg && typeof msg.text === 'string'; }).map(function(msg) {
      return { text: atcShortText(msg.text, 1500), time: atcBoundedNumber(msg.time, 0, game.time, 0), color: /^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(msg.color || '') ? msg.color : '#cbd5e1' };
    });
    return game;
  };
  var atcShiftHistory = function(history) {
    return (Array.isArray(history) ? history : []).slice(0, 8).filter(function(item) {
      return item && typeof item.shiftId === 'string' && /^[\w-]{1,80}$/.test(item.shiftId) && AIRPORTS.some(function(airport) { return airport.id === item.airportId; });
    }).map(function(item) {
      var airport = AIRPORTS.find(function(candidate) { return candidate.id === item.airportId; });
      var result = { shiftId: item.shiftId, completedAt: atcBoundedNumber(item.completedAt, 0, 9007199254740991, 0), airportId: airport.id, airportName: airport.name,
        trainingMode: item.trainingMode === 'guided' ? 'guided' : 'standard', closed: !!item.closed,
        score: atcBoundedNumber(item.score, -1000000, 1000000, 0), time: atcBoundedNumber(item.time, 0, 86400, 0), conflictSeconds: atcBoundedNumber(item.conflictSeconds, 0, 86400, 0), decisionLog: atcDecisionLog(item.decisionLog), practice: atcPracticeSnapshot(item.practice), exerciseResult: atcExerciseResultSnapshot(item.exerciseResult) };
      ['landed', 'bestStreak', 'conflictEpisodes', 'mathCorrect', 'goArounds'].forEach(function(key) { result[key] = Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(item[key], 0, 100000, 0)); });
      return result;
    });
  };
  var recordAtcShift = function(data, game, completedAt) {
    data = data || {};
    if (!game || !game.airport || !game.shiftId) return null;
    var history = atcShiftHistory(data.shiftHistory);
    var previous = history.find(function(item) { return item.shiftId === game.shiftId; });
    if (data.lastCommittedShiftId === game.shiftId || previous) return { view: 'debrief', activeShift: null, lastShift: previous || data.lastShift };
    var summary = { shiftId: game.shiftId, completedAt: atcBoundedNumber(completedAt, 0, 9007199254740991, Date.now()),
      airportId: game.airport.id, airportName: game.airport.name, trainingMode: game.trainingMode === 'guided' ? 'guided' : 'standard',
      score: atcBoundedNumber(game.score, -1000000, 1000000, 0), landed: Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(game.landed, 0, 100000, 0)),
      bestStreak: Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(Math.max(game.sessionBestStreak || 0, game.streak || 0), 0, 100000, 0)),
      time: atcBoundedNumber(game.time, 0, 86400, 0), conflictSeconds: atcBoundedNumber(game.conflicts, 0, 86400, 0),
      conflictEpisodes: Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(game.conflictEpisodes, 0, 100000, 0)), mathCorrect: Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(game.mathCorrect, 0, 100000, 0)),
      goArounds: Math.floor(atcBoundedNumber(game.goArounds, 0, 100000, 0)), closed: !!game.gameOver, decisionLog: atcDecisionLog(game.decisionLog), practice: atcPracticeSnapshot(game.practice), exerciseResult: atcExerciseResultSnapshot(scenarioProgress(game)) };
    var learning = atcRecordPracticeLearning(data.practiceLearning, history, summary);
    return { view: 'debrief', activeShift: null, lastShift: summary, lastCommittedShiftId: game.shiftId, practiceLearning: learning,
      shiftHistory: [summary].concat(history).slice(0, 8),
      totalScore: atcBoundedNumber(data.totalScore, -100000000, 100000000, 0) + summary.score,
      totalLanded: atcBoundedNumber(data.totalLanded, 0, 100000000, 0) + summary.landed,
      bestStreak: Math.max(atcBoundedNumber(data.bestStreak, 0, 100000, 0), summary.bestStreak) };
  };

  // ── CALLSIGNS ──
  var AIRLINES = ['Delta', 'United', 'American', 'Southwest', 'JetBlue', 'Spirit', 'Alaska', 'Frontier', 'Air Canada', 'British'];
  var genCallsign = function() { return AIRLINES[Math.floor(Math.random() * AIRLINES.length)] + ' ' + (Math.floor(Math.random() * 900) + 100); };

  // ── ATC PHRASEOLOGY TEMPLATES ──
  var PHRASES = {
    cleared_approach: '{callsign}, cleared ILS runway {rwy}, maintain {speed} knots until established.',
    go_around: '{callsign}, go around. Fly heading {hdg}, climb and maintain 3,000.',
    hold: '{callsign}, enter the teaching holding pattern. Expect further clearance in {time} minutes.',
    speed: '{callsign}, reduce speed to {speed} knots.',
    heading: '{callsign}, turn {dir} heading {hdg}.',
    cleared_land: '{callsign}, runway {rwy}, cleared to land. Wind {windDir} at {windSpd}.',
    separation: 'Traffic alert: {callsign1} and {callsign2}, {dist} miles separation. Minimum required: {min} miles.',
  };

  // ── MATH CHALLENGE TEMPLATES ──
  var MATH_CHALLENGES = [
    { type: 'arrival_order', question: 'Aircraft A has {d1} nm to fly at a groundspeed of {s1} knots. Aircraft B has {d2} nm to fly at {s2} knots. At constant speeds, who arrives first: A, B, or both at the same time?',
      generate: function() {
        var d1 = 20 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
        var s1 = 120 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 60);
        var d2 = 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 35);
        var s2 = 100 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 80);
        var t1 = d1 / s1 * 60; var t2 = d2 / s2 * 60;
        // Compare integer products so mathematically equal ETAs remain ties.
        var answer = d1 * s2 === d2 * s1 ? 'TIE' : (d1 * s2 < d2 * s1 ? 'A' : 'B');
        return { d1: d1, s1: s1, d2: d2, s2: s2, answer: answer, t1: Math.round(t1 * 100) / 100, t2: Math.round(t2 * 100) / 100,
          explanation: 'A: ' + d1 + ' / ' + s1 + ' × 60 ≈ ' + (Math.round(t1 * 100) / 100) + ' min. B: ' + d2 + ' / ' + s2 + ' × 60 ≈ ' + (Math.round(t2 * 100) / 100) + ' min. ' + (answer === 'TIE' ? 'Both arrive at the same time.' : answer + ' arrives first.') + ' Knots are nautical miles per hour, so multiply hours by 60 to get minutes.' };
      }
    },
    { type: 'separation', question: 'Two aircraft are {d} nm apart, closing at a constant combined groundspeed of {rate} knots. How many minutes until their separation reaches {sep} nm? Round to the nearest tenth of a minute.',
      generate: function() {
        var d = 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20);
        var rate = 200 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 200);
        var sep = 3 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);
        var closingDist = d - sep;
        var minutes = closingDist / rate * 60;
        return { d: d, rate: rate, sep: sep, answer: Math.round(minutes * 10) / 10, precision: 1, unit: 'minutes', exactAnswer: minutes,
          explanation: 'Closing distance: ' + d + ' - ' + sep + ' = ' + closingDist + ' nm. At ' + rate + ' kts closing: ' + closingDist + '/' + rate + ' × 60 = ' + (Math.round(minutes * 10) / 10) + ' minutes.' };
      }
    },
    { type: 'descent', question: 'An aircraft at {altitude} ft needs to reach {target} ft after flying {dist} nm at a constant groundspeed of {speed} knots. What average descent rate in feet per minute is needed? Round to the nearest whole foot per minute.',
      generate: function() {
        var alt = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 6) + 2) * 50; // FL100-FL350
        var target = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4 + 2) * 1000;
        var dist = 30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 40);
        var speed = 200 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 80);
        var altChange = alt * 100 - target;
        var timeMin = dist / speed * 60;
        var fpm = Math.round(altChange / timeMin);
        return { alt: alt, altitude: alt * 100, target: target, dist: dist, speed: speed, answer: fpm, precision: 0, unit: 'feet per minute', exactAnswer: altChange / timeMin,
          explanation: 'Altitude to lose: ' + (alt * 100).toLocaleString() + ' - ' + target.toLocaleString() + ' = ' + altChange.toLocaleString() + ' ft. Time: ' + dist + ' / ' + speed + ' × 60 ≈ ' + (Math.round(timeMin * 100) / 100) + ' min. Rate: ' + altChange.toLocaleString() + ' / (' + dist + ' / ' + speed + ' × 60) ≈ ' + fpm + ' fpm. Keep the full time value until the final rounding.' };
      }
    },
    { type: 'wind_correction', question: 'Runway heading is {rwyHdg}°. Wind is from {windDir}° at {windSpd} knots. What is the magnitude of the crosswind component? Round to the nearest whole knot.',
      generate: function() {
        var rwyHdg = Math.floor(Math.random() * 36) * 10;
        var windDir = Math.floor(Math.random() * 36) * 10;
        var windSpd = 10 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30);
        var diff = Math.abs(windDir - rwyHdg);
        if (diff > 180) diff = 360 - diff;
        var crosswind = Math.round(windSpd * Math.sin(toRad(diff)));
        return { rwyHdg: rwyHdg, windDir: windDir, windSpd: windSpd, answer: Math.abs(crosswind), precision: 0, unit: 'knots', exactAnswer: Math.abs(windSpd * Math.sin(toRad(diff))),
          explanation: 'Angle between wind and runway: ' + diff + '°. Crosswind magnitude = |' + windSpd + ' × sin(' + diff + '°)| ≈ ' + Math.abs(crosswind) + ' knots. Zero crosswind can still mean a headwind or tailwind. Aircraft limits depend on the flight manual, runway conditions, and operating procedures.' };
      }
    },
  ];

  // Shared by the visible form and keyboard entry. Tolerances reflect only the
  // requested rounding, rather than allowing a percentage of a large answer.
  var mathQuestion = function(mc) {
    if (!mc) return '';
    var template = MATH_CHALLENGES.find(function(item) { return item.type === mc.type; });
    if (!template) return '';
    return template.question.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, function(match, key) {
      var value = key === 'altitude' && mc.altitude == null ? mc.alt * 100 : mc[key];
      return value == null ? match : String(value);
    });
  };
  var mathAnswerCorrect = function(mc, input) {
    if (!mc || input == null) return false;
    var text = String(input).trim();
    if (mc.type === 'arrival_order') {
      var choice = text.toUpperCase();
      if (choice === 'T' || choice === 'BOTH' || choice === 'SAME TIME') choice = 'TIE';
      return (choice === 'A' || choice === 'B' || choice === 'TIE') && choice === mc.answer;
    }
    if (!/^[+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+)$/.test(text)) return false;
    var value = Number(text);
    var answer = Number(mc.answer);
    if (!isFinite(value) || !isFinite(answer) || value < 0) return false;
    if (mc.type !== 'separation' && mc.type !== 'descent' && mc.type !== 'wind_correction') return false;
    var precision = mc.type === 'separation' ? 1 : 0;
    var tolerance = 0.5 / Math.pow(10, precision);
    return Math.abs(value - answer) <= tolerance + 1e-9;
  };

  // Store real decisions and outcomes, rather than reconstructing them from scores.
  // The bounded log is also included in recovery drafts and completed debriefs.
  var recordAtcDecision = function(game, type, aircraft, text) {
    if (!game) return;
    game.decisionLog = (game.decisionLog || []).concat([{
      type: type, callsign: aircraft ? aircraft.callsign : '',
      text: String(text), time: game.time || 0
    }]).slice(-40);
    if (type === 'landing') recordPracticeOutcome(game, 'landing', aircraft);
    if (type === 'missed' || (type === 'fuel' && aircraft && aircraft.state === 'departed')) recordPracticeOutcome(game, 'lost', aircraft);
  };

  // Standalone lesson experiments never write to a shift or award progress.
  var ATC_EXPLORER_FIELDS = {
    separation: { horizontal: [0, 10, 2], vertical: [0, 3000, 500] },
    sequencing: { distanceA: [5, 60, 30], speedA: [60, 240, 90], distanceB: [5, 60, 45], speedB: [60, 240, 180] },
    vectors: { current: [0, 360, 350], target: [0, 360, 10] },
    weather: { runway: [0, 360, 270], windFrom: [0, 360, 300], windSpeed: [0, 40, 20] },
    communication: {}
  };
  var atcLessonExplorer = function(kind, input) {
    if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ATC_EXPLORER_FIELDS, kind)) return null;
    input = input && typeof input === 'object' ? input : {};
    var values = {}, fields = ATC_EXPLORER_FIELDS[kind];
    Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(key) { var range = fields[key]; values[key] = atcBoundedNumber(input[key], range[0], range[1], range[2]); });
    var result = { kind: kind, values: values };
    if (kind === 'separation') {
      values.heavy = input.heavy === true;
      result.minimum = values.heavy ? 6 : 3;
      result.horizontalMet = values.horizontal >= result.minimum;
      result.verticalMet = values.vertical >= 1000;
      result.conflict = !result.horizontalMet && !result.verticalMet;
    } else if (kind === 'sequencing') {
      result.etaA = values.distanceA / values.speedA * 60;
      result.etaB = values.distanceB / values.speedB * 60;
      var difference = values.distanceA * values.speedB - values.distanceB * values.speedA;
      result.first = Math.abs(difference) < 1e-9 ? 'tie' : difference < 0 ? 'A' : 'B';
      result.gap = Math.abs(result.etaA - result.etaB);
      result.horizon = Math.max(5, Math.ceil(Math.max(result.etaA, result.etaB) / 5) * 5);
    } else if (kind === 'vectors') {
      result.turn = headingDifference(values.target, values.current);
      result.turnSeconds = Math.abs(result.turn) / 3;
    } else if (kind === 'weather') {
      var angle = toRad(headingDifference(values.windFrom, values.runway));
      var clean = function(value) { return Math.abs(value) < 1e-9 ? 0 : value; };
