      result.headwind = clean(values.windSpeed * Math.cos(angle));
      result.crosswind = clean(Math.abs(values.windSpeed * Math.sin(angle)));
    } else {
      values.sample = ['heading', 'altitude', 'runway'].indexOf(input.sample) !== -1 ? input.sample : 'heading';
      values.response = ['match', 'callsign', 'instruction'].indexOf(input.response) !== -1 ? input.response : '';
      result.answer = { heading: 'match', altitude: 'callsign', runway: 'instruction' }[values.sample];
      result.correct = values.response ? values.response === result.answer : null;
    }
    return result;
  };

  // ── EDUCATIONAL LESSONS ──
  var ATC_LESSONS = {
    separation: { title: 'Why Separation Matters', icon: '📏', content: 'Controllers keep aircraft separated to prevent collisions and wake encounters. This classroom model uses 3 nautical miles horizontally or 1,000 feet vertically; it expands horizontal spacing to 6 nautical miles for a pair involving a heavy or super aircraft. Real separation rules depend on airspace, equipment, flight phase, and the leading and following aircraft. Wake vortices can remain hazardous after the aircraft passes, so extra spacing and flight-path awareness matter.', formula: 'Model conflict: horizontal spacing below 3nm (6nm with heavy/super) AND vertical spacing below 1,000ft', source: 'https://www.faa.gov/air_traffic/publications/atpubs/atc_html/chap5_section_5.html', sourceLabel: 'FAA: radar separation and wake minima' },
    sequencing: { title: 'The Art of Sequencing', icon: '📋', content: 'Sequencing means choosing a landing order and spacing aircraft so the runway is ready for each arrival. Distance alone does not determine who arrives first: a faster aircraft farther away may catch a slower one. Compare their estimated arrival times, then consider wake category, runway assignment, and emergency priority. These estimates assume constant groundspeed along the remaining route; vectors, holding, and speed changes alter them.', formula: 'ETA in minutes = remaining route distance in nm / groundspeed in knots × 60', source: 'https://www.faa.gov/air_traffic/publications/atpubs/atc_html/chap3_section_10.html', sourceLabel: 'FAA: arrival procedures and sequencing' },
    vectors: { title: 'Radar Vectors', icon: '🧭', content: 'Controllers issue heading instructions, called vectors, to guide aircraft toward a route or approach. Headings run clockwise from north: 090° is east, 180° is south, and 270° is west. At a standard turn rate of 3° per second, a full circle takes two minutes, but aircraft do not always use that rate. At the same bank angle, higher airspeed produces a larger turn radius. Wind can make the ground track differ from the heading.', formula: 'Level-turn radius = airspeed² / (g × tan(bank)); use m/s for airspeed and 9.81 m/s² for g to get meters', source: 'https://www.faa.gov/regulations_policies/handbooks_manuals/aviation/phak', sourceLabel: 'FAA: Pilot’s Handbook of Aeronautical Knowledge' },
    communication: { title: 'ATC Phraseology', icon: '📻', content: 'Standard phrases help pilots and controllers understand each other. An approach clearance permits the approach; a landing clearance permits landing on the assigned runway. A go-around stops the landing attempt and begins a climb. Pilots acknowledge instructions and read back key details such as runway, heading, and altitude so errors can be caught. Roger means the message was received, while Wilco means it was understood and will be followed. The phonetic alphabet begins Alfa, Bravo, Charlie.', formula: 'Read back key details: callsign + runway / heading / altitude + clearance', source: 'https://www.faa.gov/air_traffic/publications/atpubs/aim_html/chap4_section_2.html', sourceLabel: 'FAA: radio communications phraseology' },
    weather: { title: 'Weather & ATC', icon: '🌧️', content: 'Thunderstorms, poor visibility, and strong winds can reduce runway capacity or change routes. Aircraft generally take off and land into the wind when practical, while runway selection also depends on traffic, runway condition, and procedures. Wind directions describe where the wind comes from. Split wind into a headwind or tailwind component along the runway and a crosswind component across it. An acceptable crosswind depends on the specific aircraft flight manual and operating conditions; there is no single limit for every aircraft.', formula: 'Headwind = wind speed × cos(angle); crosswind magnitude = |wind speed × sin(angle)|. Negative headwind means tailwind.', source: 'https://www.faa.gov/regulations_policies/handbooks_manuals/aviation/airplane_handbook', sourceLabel: 'FAA: Airplane Flying Handbook, approaches and landings' },
  };

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════
  // REGISTER TOOL

      var airportDiagram = function(apt, className) {
        var centerX = apt.runways.reduce(function(sum, rwy) { return sum + rwy.x; }, 0) / apt.runways.length;
        var centerY = apt.runways.reduce(function(sum, rwy) { return sum + rwy.y; }, 0) / apt.runways.length;
        return h('svg', { className: 'atc-airport-diagram ' + (className || ''), 'data-atctower-airport-diagram': apt.id, viewBox: '0 0 240 144', 'aria-hidden': true, focusable: false },
          [24,48,72,96,120].map(function(y) { return h('line', { key: 'h' + y, className: 'atc-diagram-grid', x1: 8, x2: 232, y1: y, y2: y }); }),
          [24,48,72,96,120,144,168,192,216].map(function(x) { return h('line', { key: 'v' + x, className: 'atc-diagram-grid', x1: x, x2: x, y1: 8, y2: 136 }); }),
          h('ellipse', { className: 'atc-diagram-ring', cx: 120, cy: 72, rx: 88, ry: 60 }),
          apt.runways.map(function(rwy, index) {
            var x = 120 + (rwy.x - centerX) * .65, y = 72 + (rwy.y - centerY) * .65;
            var angle = toRad(rwy.hdg), dx = Math.sin(angle) * rwy.len * .6, dy = -Math.cos(angle) * rwy.len * .6;
            return h('g', { key: index, 'data-atctower-runway-symbol': String(index) },
              h('line', { className: 'atc-diagram-runway', x1: x - dx, y1: y - dy, x2: x + dx, y2: y + dy }),
              h('line', { className: 'atc-diagram-centerline', x1: x - dx, y1: y - dy, x2: x + dx, y2: y + dy }),
              h('text', { x: x + Math.cos(angle) * 14, y: y + Math.sin(angle) * 14 + 4, textAnchor: 'middle' }, runwayLabel(rwy, apt)));
          }));

@media(max-width:360px){.atc-explorer-metric>strong{font-size:18px}}
.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-explorer-display,.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-explorer-summary,.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-lesson-nav button[aria-current=page]{background:#000;color:#fff;border-color:#ffff00}.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-lab-runway{fill:#000;stroke:#fff}.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-lab-ring{stroke:#fff}.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-lab-target{stroke:#ffff00}.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-explorer-point{background:#ffff00}.atc-lesson-workspace[data-contrast=true] .atc-lesson-source{color:#ffff00}
@media(forced-colors:active){.atc-explorer-display,.atc-explorer-summary,.atc-explorer-range,.atc-lesson-formula,.atc-explorer-message,.atc-lesson-ai-answer{background:Canvas!important;color:CanvasText!important;border-color:CanvasText!important}.atc-explorer-rail{background:CanvasText}.atc-explorer-point{background:Highlight!important;border-color:CanvasText}.atc-explorer-limit{border-color:CanvasText}.atc-explorer-compass text{fill:CanvasText}.atc-explorer-compass .atc-lab-ring,.atc-explorer-compass .atc-lab-current{stroke:CanvasText}.atc-explorer-compass .atc-lab-target{stroke:Highlight}.atc-lab-runway{fill:Canvas;stroke:CanvasText}.atc-lab-centerline{stroke:CanvasText}.atc-lesson-nav button[aria-current=page]{outline:2px solid Highlight;outline-offset:-4px}.atc-lesson-source{color:LinkText}.atc-explorer-metric>small,.atc-explorer-timeline-row small{color:CanvasText}}

      var lessonViewRef = useRef(null), menuViewRef = useRef(null), previousViewRef = useRef(view);
      // Each mounted tower owns its requests. Keep the current context in a
      // ref so a response is invalid as soon as a different lesson renders.
      var lessonRequestRef = useRef({ generation: 0, mounted: false, pending: null });
      var lessonContextRef = useRef('');
      var currentLesson = view === 'lesson' && hasSelectedLesson ? d.selectedLesson : '';
      useEffect(function() {
        if (view === 'lesson' && lessonViewRef.current) lessonViewRef.current.focus();
        if (view === 'menu' && (previousViewRef.current === 'lesson' || previousViewRef.current === 'debrief') && menuViewRef.current) {
          var card = previousViewRef.current === 'lesson' && hasSelectedLesson && menuViewRef.current.querySelector('[data-atctower-lesson="' + d.selectedLesson + '"]');
          var target = card || menuViewRef.current.querySelector('[role="tab"][aria-selected="true"]');
          if (target) target.focus();
        }
        previousViewRef.current = view;
      }, [view, currentLesson]);
      var currentLessonLevel = currentLesson ? (d['aiLevel_' + currentLesson] || 'grade5') : '';
      var lessonRequestContext = currentLesson ? currentLesson + ':' + currentLessonLevel : '';
      lessonContextRef.current = lessonRequestContext;
      useEffect(function() {
        var requests = lessonRequestRef.current;
        requests.generation += 1;
        requests.mounted = true;
        var reset = {};
        if (requests.pending && requests.pending.context !== lessonRequestContext) {
          reset[requests.pending.loadingKey] = false;
          requests.pending = null;
        }
        if (currentLesson) {
          if (!requests.pending && d['aiLoading_' + currentLesson]) reset['aiLoading_' + currentLesson] = false;
          var savedLevel = d['aiExplainLevel_' + currentLesson];
          if (savedLevel && savedLevel !== currentLessonLevel) {
            reset['aiExplain_' + currentLesson] = '';
            reset['aiError_' + currentLesson] = '';
          }
        }
        if (Object.keys(reset).length) updMulti(reset);
        return function() {
          requests.mounted = false;
          requests.generation += 1;
        };
      }, [lessonRequestContext]);
      var totalScore = (typeof d.totalScore === 'number' && isFinite(d.totalScore)) ? d.totalScore : 0;
      var totalLanded = (typeof d.totalLanded === 'number' && isFinite(d.totalLanded)) ? d.totalLanded : 0;
      var bestStreak = (typeof d.bestStreak === 'number' && isFinite(d.bestStreak)) ? d.bestStreak : 0;

      // ── Game State Refs ──
      var canvasRef = useRef(null);
      var animRef = useRef(null);
      var gameRef = useRef(null);
      if (!gameRef.current) gameRef.current = (view === 'playing' ? restoreAtcShift(d.activeShift) : null) || {
        aircraft: [], time: 0, score: 0, landed: 0, conflicts: 0, streak: 0,
        selected: null, airport: null, gameOver: false, paused: false,
        wind: { dir: 270, speed: 10 }, messages: [], mathChallenge: null
      };
      var runningRef = useRef(view === 'playing' && !!gameRef.current.airport);
      var latestDataRef = useRef(d);
      latestDataRef.current = d;
      var persistenceRef = useRef(null);
      persistenceRef.current = function(patch) { ctx.updateMulti('atcTower', patch); };
      var checkpointShift = function() {
        var saved = serializeAtcShift(gameRef.current, Date.now());
        if (!saved) return;
        latestDataRef.current = Object.assign({}, latestDataRef.current, { activeShift: saved });
        persistenceRef.current({ activeShift: saved });
      };
      var commandRef = useRef(null);
      var _snapshot = useState(null), snapshot = _snapshot[0], setSnapshot = _snapshot[1];
      var _commandDraft = useState(null), commandDraft = _commandDraft[0], setCommandDraft = _commandDraft[1];
      var _mathInput = useState(''), mathInput = _mathInput[0], setMathInput = _mathInput[1];
      var _commandFeedback = useState(null), commandFeedback = _commandFeedback[0], setCommandFeedback = _commandFeedback[1];
      var _evidenceFilter = useState('all'), evidenceFilter = _evidenceFilter[0], setEvidenceFilter = _evidenceFilter[1];
      var _reflectionEvent = useState(''), reflectionEvent = _reflectionEvent[0], setReflectionEvent = _reflectionEvent[1];
      var _lessonExplorers = useState({}), lessonExplorers = _lessonExplorers[0], setLessonExplorers = _lessonExplorers[1];
      useEffect(function() { if (view === 'debrief') { setEvidenceFilter('all'); setReflectionEvent(''); } }, [view, d.lastShift && d.lastShift.shiftId]);
      var mathFocusRef = useRef('');
      var flightOrderRef = useRef(null);
      var radarTagsRef = useRef([]);
      var focusShiftId = view === 'playing' ? gameRef.current.shiftId : null;
      useEffect(function() {
        if (view !== 'playing' || !focusShiftId) return;
        var tower = canvasRef.current && canvasRef.current.closest('[data-atctower-playing]');
        var missionTitle = tower && (tower.querySelector('#atc-exercise-title') || tower.querySelector('#atc-shift-title'));
        if (missionTitle) missionTitle.focus();
      }, [view, focusShiftId]);
      var focusMath = snapshot ? snapshot.mathChallenge : gameRef.current.mathChallenge;
      var refreshTower = function() {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        setSnapshot({
          time: game.time, score: game.score, landed: game.landed,
          streak: game.streak, bestStreak: game.sessionBestStreak || game.streak,
          conflicts: game.conflicts, conflictEpisodes: game.conflictEpisodes || 0, goArounds: game.goArounds || 0,
          selected: game.selected, paused: game.paused, gameOver: game.gameOver,
          commandEntry: !!game.commandEntry,
          trainingMode: game.trainingMode || 'standard',
          radarAvailable: game.radarAvailable,
          showHelp: game.showHelp, airport: game.airport, wind: game.wind,
          runwayBusyUntil: Object.assign({}, game.runwayBusyUntil || {}),
          mathChallenge: game.mathChallenge ? Object.assign({}, game.mathChallenge) : null,
          practice: atcPracticeSnapshot(game.practice),
          reviewNote: atcReviewNote(game.reviewNote, game.practice && game.practice.id, game.airport && game.airport.id),
          reviewNoteDismissed: game.reviewNoteDismissed === true,
          aircraft: game.aircraft.filter(function(ac) { return ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed'; }).map(function(ac) { return Object.assign({}, ac); }),
          messages: game.messages.slice(0, 8)
        });
      };
      useEffect(function() {
        if (view !== 'playing' || !canvasRef.current) return;
        var tower = canvasRef.current.closest('[data-atctower-playing]');
        var list = tower && tower.querySelector('.atc-aircraft-list');
        var strip = list && list.querySelector('[data-atctower-aircraft="' + gameRef.current.selected + '"]');
        if (!strip) return;
        // Reveal the selected strip inside its own scroll container. Radar
        // keyboard selection must not move document scroll or keyboard focus.
        var bounds = list.getBoundingClientRect(), item = strip.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (item.top < bounds.top + 8) list.scrollTop += item.top - bounds.top - 8;
        else if (item.bottom > bounds.top + list.clientHeight - 8) list.scrollTop += item.bottom - bounds.top - list.clientHeight + 8;
      }, [view, snapshot && snapshot.selected]);
      useEffect(function() {
        if (view !== 'playing') return;
        refreshTower();
        var timer = setInterval(refreshTower, 250);
        return function() { clearInterval(timer); };
      }, [view]);
      useEffect(function() {
        var token = view === 'playing' && focusMath ? focusMath.type + ':' + !!focusMath.answered : '';
        if (token === mathFocusRef.current) return;
        mathFocusRef.current = token;
        if (!token || !canvasRef.current) return;
        var tower = canvasRef.current.closest('[data-atctower-playing]');
        var panel = tower && tower.querySelector('[data-atctower-math-challenge]');
        var control = panel && panel.querySelector(focusMath.answered ? '[data-atctower-math-continue]' : 'input,button');
        if (control) control.focus();
        if (!focusMath.answered) announce('Traffic paused for ATC math. ' + mathQuestion(focusMath));
      }, [view, focusMath ? focusMath.type : '', focusMath ? !!focusMath.answered : false]);
      useEffect(function() {
        if (view !== 'playing') return;
        checkpointShift();
        var timer = setInterval(checkpointShift, 5000);
        var onVisibility = function() { if (document.hidden) checkpointShift(); };
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
        window.addEventListener('pagehide', checkpointShift);
        return function() {
          clearInterval(timer);
          document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibility);
          window.removeEventListener('pagehide', checkpointShift);
          checkpointShift();
        };
      }, [view]);
      var finishShift = function() {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        if (!game.airport || game.committed) return;
        if (!game.shiftId) game.shiftId = atcShiftId();
        var patch = recordAtcShift(latestDataRef.current, game, Date.now());
        if (!patch) return;
        game.committed = true;
        runningRef.current = false;
        latestDataRef.current = Object.assign({}, latestDataRef.current, patch);
        updMulti(patch);
        setCommandDraft(null);
        setMathInput('');
        announce('Shift recorded. ' + game.landed + ' aircraft landed. Score ' + game.score + '.');
      };
      var selectAircraft = function(id) {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        if (game.commandEntry) { showCommandFeedback('blocked', 'Finish or cancel the current instruction before selecting another aircraft.', null); return; }
        var ac = game.aircraft.find(function(a) { return a.id === id && a.state !== 'landed' && a.state !== 'departed'; });
        if (!ac) return;
        game.selected = ac.id;
        setCommandFeedback(null);
        if (tutorialRef.current.step === 0) tutorialRef.current.step = 1;
        announce(ac.callsign + ', ' + ac.type.name + ', ' + Math.round(ac.altitude) + ' feet, ' + Math.round(ac.speed) + ' knots.');
        refreshTower();
      };
      var showCommandFeedback = function(status, text, aircraft) {
        setCommandFeedback({ status: status, text: text, aircraftId: aircraft ? aircraft.id : null });
        announce(text);
      };
      var submitMath = function(value, skip) {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        var mc = game.mathChallenge;
        if (!mc || mc.answered) return;
        if (!skip && mc.type !== 'arrival_order' && (!String(value).trim() || !isFinite(Number(value)))) {
          announce('Enter a number before checking your answer.');
          return;
        }
        mc.answered = true;
        mc.skipped = !!skip;
        mc.correct = !skip && mathAnswerCorrect(mc, value);
        if (mc.correct) {
          game.score += 25;
          game.mathCorrect = (game.mathCorrect || 0) + 1;
          playATCSound('correct');
        }
        var feedback = (skip ? 'Worked example. ' : mc.correct ? 'Correct. ' : 'Try the worked example. Answer: ' + mc.answer + '. ') + mc.explanation;
        game.messages.unshift({ text: feedback, time: game.time, color: mc.correct ? '#4ade80' : '#fbbf24' });
        recordAtcDecision(game, 'math', null, skip ? 'Reviewed worked example: ' + mc.type + '.' : (mc.correct ? 'Solved ' : 'Reviewed incorrect answer: ') + mc.type + '.');
        announce(feedback);
        setMathInput('');
        refreshTower();
      };

      // ── Tutorial System ──
      var tutorialSteps = [
        { msg: '👋 Welcome, Controller! Aircraft are entering your airspace. Click one to select it.', trigger: 'start', key: 'click' },
        { msg: '🛬 Good! Now press R to assign a runway. The aircraft needs to know where to land.', trigger: 'selected', key: 'R' },
        { msg: '🧭 Press H to give a heading. Guide the aircraft toward the runway.', trigger: 'runway_assigned', key: 'H' },
        { msg: '✅ Now press C to clear for approach. The aircraft will fly the ILS to the runway.', trigger: 'heading_given', key: 'C' },
        { msg: '🎉 Great — your first aircraft is on approach! Watch it land. You\'re a natural!', trigger: 'cleared', key: null },
        { msg: '💡 Tip: Watch the ETA timeline at the bottom. Keep aircraft spaced apart to avoid conflicts!', trigger: 'landed_first', key: null },
      ];
      var tutorialRef = useRef({ step: 0, active: !d.activeShift, dismissed: !!d.activeShift });

      // ── ATC Sound Effects ──
      var atcAudioRef = useRef({ ctx: null });
      var playATCSound = function(type) {
        if (latestDataRef.current.soundEnabled === false) return;
        try {
          var a = atcAudioRef.current;
          if (!a.ctx) a.ctx = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)();
          if (a.ctx.state === 'suspended') a.ctx.resume();
          var osc = a.ctx.createOscillator();
          var gain = a.ctx.createGain();
          osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(a.ctx.destination);
          if (type === 'select') { osc.frequency.value = 800; gain.gain.value = 0.03; osc.type = 'sine'; }
          else if (type === 'clear') { osc.frequency.value = 600; gain.gain.value = 0.04; osc.type = 'sine'; }
          else if (type === 'land') { osc.frequency.value = 500; gain.gain.value = 0.03; osc.type = 'triangle'; }
          else if (type === 'conflict') { osc.frequency.value = 300; gain.gain.value = 0.06; osc.type = 'square'; }
          else if (type === 'correct') { osc.frequency.value = 880; gain.gain.value = 0.03; osc.type = 'sine'; }
          else { osc.frequency.value = 440; gain.gain.value = 0.02; osc.type = 'sine'; }
          gain.gain.setTargetAtTime(0, a.ctx.currentTime + 0.1, 0.05);
          osc.start(); osc.stop(a.ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
        } catch(e) {}
      };

      // ── ATC Achievements ──
      var ATC_BADGES = [
        { id: 'first_land', name: t('stem.atctower.first_landing', 'First Landing'), icon: '🛬', desc: t('stem.atctower.land_your_first_aircraft', 'Land your first aircraft'), check: function(g) { return g.landed >= 1; } },
        { id: 'streak_5', name: t('stem.atctower.hot_streak', 'Hot Streak'), icon: '🔥', desc: t('stem.atctower.5_consecutive_safe_landings', '5 consecutive safe landings'), check: function(g) { return g.streak >= 5; } },
        { id: 'streak_10', name: t('stem.atctower.perfect_sequence', 'Perfect Sequence'), icon: '⭐', desc: t('stem.atctower.10_consecutive_safe_landings', '10 consecutive safe landings'), check: function(g) { return g.streak >= 10; } },
        { id: 'land_10', name: t('stem.atctower.busy_controller', 'Busy Controller'), icon: '📋', desc: t('stem.atctower.land_10_aircraft_in_one_session', 'Land 10 aircraft in one session'), check: function(g) { return g.landed >= 10; } },
        { id: 'land_25', name: t('stem.atctower.tower_chief', 'Tower Chief'), icon: '🗼', desc: t('stem.atctower.land_25_aircraft_in_one_session', 'Land 25 aircraft in one session'), check: function(g) { return g.landed >= 25; } },
        { id: 'math_5', name: t('stem.atctower.quick_calculator', 'Quick Calculator'), icon: '🧮', desc: t('stem.atctower.solve_5_math_challenges_correctly', 'Solve 5 math challenges correctly'), check: function(g) { return g.mathCorrect >= 5; } },
        { id: 'no_conflict', name: t('stem.atctower.safety_first', 'Safety First'), icon: '🛡️', desc: t('stem.atctower.land_10_aircraft_with_zero_conflicts', 'Land 10+ aircraft with zero conflicts'), check: function(g) { return g.landed >= 10 && g.conflictEpisodes === 0 && g.conflicts === 0; } },
        { id: 'heavy_land', name: t('stem.atctower.heavy_metal', 'Heavy Metal'), icon: '✈️', desc: t('stem.atctower.land_a_heavy_or_super_aircraft', 'Land a Heavy or Super aircraft'), check: function(g) { return g.landedHeavy; } },
        { id: 'efficiency_90', name: t('stem.atctower.steady_sequencer', 'Steady Sequencer'), icon: '📊', desc: t('stem.atctower.steady_sequencer_copy', 'Land 10+ aircraft with at most one separation alert'), check: function(g) { return g.landed >= 10 && g.conflictEpisodes <= 1; } },
      ];
      var atcBadgesRef = useRef({});
      var atcBadgePopRef = useRef(null);
      var earnedATCBadges = d.atcBadges || {};

      var checkATCBadges = function(game, time) {
        var extra = {
          landed: game.landed, streak: game.streak, conflicts: game.conflicts, conflictEpisodes: game.conflictEpisodes || 0,
          mathCorrect: game.mathCorrect || 0, landedHeavy: game.landedHeavy || false,
          efficiency: game.landed > 0 ? Math.max(0, 100 - Math.round(game.conflicts / game.landed * 20)) : 100
        };
        var newBadges = {};
        var justEarned = null;
        ATC_BADGES.forEach(function(badge) {
          if (earnedATCBadges[badge.id] || atcBadgesRef.current[badge.id]) return;
          if (badge.check(extra)) { newBadges[badge.id] = true; justEarned = badge; }
        });
        if (Object.keys(newBadges).length > 0) {
          var updated = Object.assign({}, latestDataRef.current.atcBadges || {}, atcBadgesRef.current, newBadges);
          atcBadgesRef.current = updated;
          upd('atcBadges', updated);
          if (justEarned) { atcBadgePopRef.current = { badge: justEarned, time: time }; playATCSound('correct'); }
        }
      };

      // ── Accessibility: keyboard aircraft cycling ──
      var cycleSelection = function(direction) {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        if (game.commandEntry) return;
        setCommandFeedback(null);
        var active = sortedFlightStrips(game.aircraft, game.airport);
        if (active.length === 0) return;
        var currentIdx = active.findIndex(function(a) { return a.id === game.selected; });
        var nextIdx = currentIdx < 0 ? (direction > 0 ? 0 : active.length - 1) : direction > 0 ? (currentIdx + 1) % active.length : (currentIdx - 1 + active.length) % active.length;
        game.selected = active[nextIdx].id;
        var sel = active[nextIdx];
        game.messages.unshift({ text: '📻 ' + sel.callsign + ' (' + sel.type.name + ')', time: game.time, color: '#60a5fa' });
        playATCSound('select');
        announce('Selected ' + sel.callsign + '. ' + sel.type.name + '. Altitude ' + sel.altitude + '. Heading ' + Math.round(sel.heading) + '. ' + Math.round(sel.speed) + ' knots. State: ' + sel.state);
        if (tutorialRef.current.step === 0) tutorialRef.current.step = 1;
        refreshTower();
      };

      // ── WCAG 2.1 AA: Accessible state narration ──
      // Creates/updates an aria-live region that announces game events to screen readers
      var a11yRef = useRef(null);
      var announceTimerRef = useRef(null);
      var announceQueueRef = useRef({ messages: [], extra: 0 });
      useEffect(function() {
        if (a11yRef.current) return;
        var region = document.createElement('div');
        region.id = 'atc-a11y-live';
        region.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
        region.setAttribute('aria-atomic', 'true');
        region.setAttribute('role', 'status');
        region.className = 'sr-only';
        region.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;width:1px;height:1px;padding:0;margin:-1px;overflow:hidden;clip:rect(0,0,0,0);border:0';
        document.body.appendChild(region);
        a11yRef.current = region;
        return function() { clearTimeout(announceTimerRef.current); a11yRef.current = null; if (region.parentNode) region.parentNode.removeChild(region); };
      }, []);
      var announce = function(msg) {
        if (!a11yRef.current) return;
        var queue = announceQueueRef.current;
        if (queue.messages.indexOf(msg) !== -1) return;
        if (!queue.messages.length) {
          a11yRef.current.textContent = '';
          clearTimeout(announceTimerRef.current);
          announceTimerRef.current = setTimeout(function() {
            var ready = announceQueueRef.current;
            if (a11yRef.current) a11yRef.current.textContent = ready.messages.join(' ') + (ready.extra ? ' ' + ready.extra + ' additional tower events are in the radio log.' : '');
            announceQueueRef.current = { messages: [], extra: 0 };
          }, 50);
        }
        if (queue.messages.length < 12) queue.messages.push(msg); else queue.extra++;
      };

      // ── WCAG 2.2.1: Pause mechanism ──
      var togglePause = function() {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        if (game.practice && game.practice.completed) { announce('Exercise objectives are complete. End the shift to record your result or retry.'); return; }
        game.paused = !game.paused;
        announce(game.paused ? 'Game paused' : 'Game resumed');
        refreshTower();
      };

      // ── WCAG 1.4.3/1.4.11: High contrast mode ──
      var highContrast = d.highContrast || false;

      // ── WCAG 2.3.1: Reduced motion detection ──
      var reducedMotion = useRef(false);
      useEffect(function() {
        var mq = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
        reducedMotion.current = mq && mq.matches;
        if (mq && mq.addEventListener) {
          var handler = function(e) { reducedMotion.current = e.matches; };
          mq.addEventListener('change', handler);
          return function() { mq.removeEventListener('change', handler); };
        }
      }, []);

      // ── Spawn aircraft ──
      var spawnAircraft = function(airport, firstArrival) {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        if (game.aircraft.filter(function(ac) { return ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed'; }).length >= airport.maxTraffic) return;
        var type = AC_TYPES[Math.floor(Math.random() * AC_TYPES.length)];
        var side = Math.floor(Math.random() * 4); // 0=top, 1=right, 2=bottom, 3=left
        var x, y2, hdg;
        if (side === 0) { x = Math.random() * 400; y2 = 0; hdg = 150 + Math.random() * 60; }
        else if (side === 1) { x = 400; y2 = Math.random() * 400; hdg = 210 + Math.random() * 60; }
        else if (side === 2) { x = Math.random() * 400; y2 = 400; hdg = 330 + Math.random() * 60; }
        else { x = 0; y2 = Math.random() * 400; hdg = 30 + Math.random() * 60; }
        if (firstArrival) { x = 330; y2 = airport.runways[0].y; hdg = airport.runways[0].hdg; }
        // Emergency scenarios (10% chance after 5 landings)
        var emergency = null;
        if (game.landed >= 5 && Math.random() < 0.1) {
          var emergTypes = [
            { type: 'engine', label: t('stem.atctower.engine_failure', 'ENGINE FAILURE'), msg: 'has lost an engine! Priority landing required.', priority: true, fuelOverride: 8, lesson: 'Single-engine aircraft must land immediately. Multi-engine can fly on one engine but with reduced performance. Controllers clear the runway and give direct vectors.' },
            { type: 'medical', label: t('stem.atctower.medical_emergency', 'MEDICAL EMERGENCY'), msg: 'reports medical emergency on board! Requesting priority.', priority: true, lesson: 'Medical emergencies get priority sequencing. Controllers coordinate with airport emergency services. The aircraft doesn\'t need a special runway — just the fastest path to the gate.' },
            { type: 'birdstrike', label: t('stem.atctower.bird_strike', 'BIRD STRIKE'), msg: 'reports bird strike! Checking aircraft systems.', priority: false, lesson: 'Bird strikes happen ~13,000 times per year in the US. Most are minor, but they can damage engines. The pilot may request to return for inspection.' },
            { type: 'hydraulic', label: t('stem.atctower.hydraulic_failure', 'HYDRAULIC FAILURE'), msg: 'reports partial hydraulic failure. May have difficulty steering on ground.', priority: true, lesson: 'Hydraulic systems control flaps, landing gear, and brakes. Pilots can use backup systems but need extra runway length. Controllers keep other aircraft clear.' },
          ];
          emergency = emergTypes[Math.floor(Math.random() * emergTypes.length)];
        }

        var newAC = {
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          callsign: genCallsign(),
          type: type,
          x: x, y: y2,
          heading: hdg,
          targetHeading: null,
          speed: type.speed,
          targetSpeed: null,
          altitude: 5000 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) * 1000,
          targetAltitude: null,
          state: 'inbound',
          assignedRunway: null,
          fuel: emergency && emergency.fuelOverride ? emergency.fuelOverride : 30 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 30),
          spawnTime: game.time,
          emergency: emergency,
        };
        var quickestApproach = Math.min.apply(Math, airport.runways.map(function(rwy) {
          var geometry = runwayGeometry(rwy);
          return (distNm(newAC.x, newAC.y, geometry.finalEntry.x, geometry.finalEntry.y) + 36) / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM / newAC.speed * 60;
        }));
        // Every arrival starts with enough fuel for a direct approach. Emergencies
        // have a smaller reserve, so priority sequencing still matters.
        newAC.fuel = Math.max(newAC.fuel, quickestApproach + (emergency ? 5 : 15 + Math.random() * 15));
        game.aircraft.push(newAC);

        if (emergency) {
          game.messages.unshift({ text: '🚨 ' + newAC.callsign + ' ' + emergency.msg, time: game.time, color: '#ef4444' });
          announce(newAC.callsign + ' declares ' + emergency.label + '. ' + emergency.lesson);
          playATCSound('conflict');
        }

        // Radio check-in message
        if (!emergency) {
          var PHONETIC = { A: 'Alpha', B: 'Bravo', C: 'Charlie', D: 'Delta', E: 'Echo', F: 'Foxtrot', G: 'Golf', H: 'Hotel', I: 'India', J: 'Juliet', K: 'Kilo', L: 'Lima', M: 'Mike', N: 'November', O: 'Oscar', P: 'Papa', Q: 'Quebec', R: 'Romeo', S: 'Sierra', T: 'Tango', U: 'Uniform', V: 'Victor', W: 'Whiskey', X: 'Xray', Y: 'Yankee', Z: 'Zulu' };
          var firstLetter = newAC.callsign.charAt(0).toUpperCase();
          var phonetic = PHONETIC[firstLetter] || firstLetter;
          game.messages.unshift({ text: '📻 "' + airport.code + ' Approach, ' + newAC.callsign + ', ' + Math.round(newAC.altitude / 100) * 100 + ' descending, inbound"', time: game.time, color: '#60a5fa' });
        }
      };

      // ── Check separation conflicts ──
      var checkConflicts = function() {
        var game = gameRef.current;
        var ac = game.aircraft.filter(function(a) { return a.state !== 'landed' && a.state !== 'departed'; });
        var conflicts = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < ac.length; i++) {
          for (var j = i + 1; j < ac.length; j++) {
            var separation = aircraftSeparation(ac[i], ac[j]);
            if (separation.conflict) {
              conflicts.push({ a: ac[i], b: ac[j], dist: separation.dist, min: separation.min });
            }
          }
        }
        return conflicts;
      };

      // ── Check if aircraft is on approach/landing ──
      var checkLanding = function(ac, airport) {
        if (ac.state !== 'approach') return false;
        var rwy = airport.runways.find(function(r) { return r === ac.assignedRunway; });
        if (!rwy) return false;
        return ac.touchdownReady || aircraftCanLand(ac, rwy);
      };

      // ── Main game loop ──
      var startPractice = function(id) {
        if (latestDataRef.current.activeShift) return;
        var game = createPracticeShift(id, atcShiftId());
        if (!game) return;
        game.reviewNote = atcPreviousReflection(latestDataRef.current, id, game.airport.id);
        game.reviewNoteDismissed = false;
        gameRef.current = game;
        runningRef.current = true;
        flightOrderRef.current = null;
        tutorialRef.current = { step: 0, active: true, dismissed: false };
        setCommandDraft(null); setMathInput(''); setSnapshot(null);
        setCommandFeedback(null);
        checkpointShift();
        updMulti({ view: 'playing', trainingMode: 'guided' });
        announce('Guided exercise started and paused. ' + game.messages[0].text);
      };
            var practiceMeta = function(id) {
        return ({
          first_landing: { skill: t('stem.atctower.skill_runway', 'Runway control'), goal: t('stem.atctower.goal_runway', 'Assign runway 27 and guide TRAIN101 to touchdown.') },
          arrival_sequence: { skill: t('stem.atctower.skill_sequence', 'Arrival sequencing'), goal: t('stem.atctower.goal_sequence', 'Compare distance and speed, then space two landings.') },
          separation_recovery: { skill: t('stem.atctower.skill_separation', 'Traffic separation'), goal: t('stem.atctower.goal_separation', 'Build 1,000 feet of spacing before the tracks cross.') },
          crossing_priority: { skill: t('stem.atctower.skill_priority', 'Priority decisions'), goal: t('stem.atctower.goal_priority', 'Give MED101 priority while protecting the shared runway area.') }
        })[id];
      };
      var practicePathwayMenu = function(pathway) {
        var recommended = pathway.entries.find(function(entry) { return entry.id === pathway.recommendedId; });
        return h('section', { className: 'atc-pathway', 'data-atctower-pathway': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-pathway-title' },
          h('div', { className: 'atc-pathway-intro' },
            h('div', null, h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, t('stem.atctower.practice_journey', 'Your controller journey')),
              h('h3', { id: 'atc-pathway-title' }, t('stem.atctower.four_skills', 'Four skills. One confident controller.')),
              h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.pathway_copy', 'Start paused, make a plan, and watch your decisions unfold. Every exercise is open to you.'))),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-pathway-count', 'data-atctower-pathway-count': String(pathway.completedCount) },
              h('strong', null, pathway.completedCount + ' / 4'), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.skills_completed', 'skills completed')))),
          h('div', { className: 'atc-pathway-track', 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.pathway_progress', 'Practice pathway progress') }, pathway.entries.map(function(entry, index) {
            return h('span', { key: entry.id, className: 'atc-pathway-step', 'data-complete': entry.complete ? 'true' : 'false' },
              h('b', { 'aria-hidden': true }, entry.complete ? '✓' : String(index + 1)), practiceMeta(entry.id).skill);
          })),
          recommended ? h('div', { className: 'atc-recommended' },
            h('div', null, h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, t('stem.atctower.recommended_practice', 'Recommended next')),
              h('h4', null, recommended.scenario.title), h('p', null, practiceMeta(recommended.id).goal)),
            h('button', { className: 'atc-primary', 'data-atctower-recommended-start': recommended.id, disabled: !!d.activeShift, onClick: function() { startPractice(recommended.id); } }, t('stem.atctower.continue_training', 'Continue training') + ' →')) :
            h('div', { className: 'atc-recommended atc-success' }, h('div', null, h('h4', null, t('stem.atctower.pathway_complete', 'All four skills completed')),
              h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.pathway_complete_copy', 'Revisit a skill below or bring your decisions together in an airport shift.'))),
              h('button', { onClick: function() { upd('atcMenuPanel', 'airports'); } }, t('stem.atctower.choose_airport', 'Choose airport'))),
          h('div', { className: 'atc-pathway-cards' }, pathway.entries.map(function(entry, index) {
            var meta = practiceMeta(entry.id);
            return h('article', { key: entry.id, className: 'atc-pathway-card', 'data-atctower-pathway-card': entry.id, 'data-practice-state': entry.state, 'data-recommended': entry.id === pathway.recommendedId ? 'true' : 'false' },
              h('div', { className: 'atc-mission-heading' }, h('div', { className: 'atc-practice-identity' }, atcVisualSymbol(entry.id, 'atc-skill-symbol'), h('span', { className: 'atc-step-number', 'aria-hidden': true }, String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0'))),
                h('span', { className: 'atc-pill' }, entry.complete ? t('stem.atctower.skill_completed', 'Completed') : entry.attempts ? t('stem.atctower.skill_attempted', 'Practiced') : t('stem.atctower.skill_new', 'Ready to try'))),
              h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, meta.skill), h('h4', null, entry.scenario.title), h('p', null, meta.goal),
              h('details', { className: 'atc-practice-preview', 'data-atctower-practice-preview': entry.id },
                h('summary', null, t('stem.atctower.preview_exercise', 'Preview exercise')),
                h('p', { className: 'atc-preview-airport' }, AIRPORTS.find(function(apt) { return apt.id === entry.scenario.airportId; }).name),
                h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.preview_fixed_roster', 'Begins paused with only the traffic below. Resume when your plan is ready.')),
                h('p', null, entry.scenario.briefing),
                h('ul', { className: 'atc-preview-traffic', 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.starting_traffic', 'Starting traffic') }, entry.scenario.aircraft.map(function(seed) {
                  var apt = AIRPORTS.find(function(item) { return item.id === entry.scenario.airportId; });
                  return h('li', { key: seed.id }, h('strong', null, seed.callsign + (seed.emergency ? ' · ' + t('stem.atctower.priority', 'Priority') : '')),
                    h('span', null, seed.typeId + ' · ' + Math.round(seed.altitude).toLocaleString() + ' ft · ' + seed.speed + ' kt'),
                    h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.heading', 'Heading') + ' ' + String(seed.heading).padStart(3, '0') + '° · ' + (seed.runwayIndex < 0 ? t('stem.atctower.no_runway', 'No runway') : 'RWY ' + runwayLabel(apt.runways[seed.runwayIndex], apt))));
                }))),
              entry.lastResult && h('div', { className: 'atc-attempt-result', 'data-atctower-last-attempt': entry.id },
                h('strong', null, t('stem.atctower.latest_attempt', 'Last attempt') + ': ' + practiceOutcomeText(entry.lastResult)),
                entry.complete && !entry.lastResult.complete && h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.earlier_completion_kept', 'Your earlier completion is still recorded.')),
                entry.hasReflection && h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.attempt_note_saved', 'Reflection note saved'))),
              h('div', { className: 'atc-pathway-card-footer' }, h('span', { className: 'atc-muted' }, entry.attempts ? entry.attempts + ' ' + (entry.attempts === 1 ? t('stem.atctower.recorded_attempt', 'recorded attempt') : t('stem.atctower.recorded_attempts', 'recorded attempts')) : t('stem.atctower.paused_start', 'Begins paused')),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-attempt-actions' }, entry.lastShift && h('button', { 'data-atctower-review-practice': entry.id, onClick: function() { updMulti({ lastShift: entry.lastShift, view: 'debrief' }); } }, t('stem.atctower.review_last_attempt', 'Review last attempt')),
                  h('button', { 'data-atctower-practice-start': entry.id, disabled: !!d.activeShift, onClick: function() { startPractice(entry.id); } }, entry.complete ? t('stem.atctower.practice_again', 'Practice again') : t('stem.atctower.start_exercise', 'Start exercise')))));
          })));
      };
      var practiceOutcomeText = function(progress) {
        return progress.complete ? t('stem.atctower.attempt_objectives_met', 'Objectives met') : progress.failed ? t('stem.atctower.attempt_review_needed', 'Review needed') : t('stem.atctower.attempt_unfinished', 'Unfinished');
      };
      var practiceCard = function(progress, summary, result) {
        if (!progress) return null;
        var scenario = progress.scenario || ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === progress.id; });
        if (!scenario) return null;
        var meta = practiceMeta(scenario.id);
        var titleId = summary ? 'atc-exercise-result-title' : 'atc-exercise-title';
        var actions = atcPracticeActionProgress(progress, summary ? result && result.practice : gameRef.current.practice);
        var decisionState = !summary && atcPracticeDecisionState(gameRef.current);
        var goals = progress.checks.filter(function(check) { return ['landings', 'order', 'vertical', 'time'].indexOf(check.id) !== -1; });
        var guards = progress.checks.filter(function(check) { return ['landings', 'order', 'vertical', 'time'].indexOf(check.id) === -1; });
        var checkRow = function(check, guard) { return h('li', { key: check.id, 'data-atctower-exercise-criterion': check.id, 'data-criterion-kind': guard ? 'guardrail' : 'action', 'data-complete': check.done ? 'true' : 'false' },
          h('span', { className: 'atc-check-icon', 'aria-hidden': true }, check.done && (!guard || summary || progress.complete) ? '✓' : guard && !check.done ? '!' : '○'),
          h('span', null, h('strong', null, guard && !summary && !progress.complete ? (check.done ? t('stem.atctower.clear_so_far', 'Clear so far') : t('stem.atctower.review_needed', 'Review needed')) : check.done ? t('stem.atctower.criterion_met', 'Met') : t('stem.atctower.criterion_remaining', 'Remaining')), ': ' + check.label)); };
        return h('section', { className: 'atc-card atc-mission' + (progress.complete ? ' atc-success' : ''), 'data-atctower-exercise': scenario.id, 'data-exercise-status': progress.complete ? 'complete' : progress.failed ? 'review' : 'in-progress', 'aria-labelledby': titleId },
          h('div', { className: 'atc-mission-heading' }, h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, meta.skill + ' · ' + (ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.indexOf(scenario) + 1) + ' / 4'),
            h('span', { className: 'atc-pill' }, progress.complete ? t('stem.atctower.objectives_achieved', 'Objectives achieved') : progress.failed ? t('stem.atctower.review_attempt', 'Review this attempt') : summary ? t('stem.atctower.attempt_recorded', 'Attempt recorded') : t('stem.atctower.in_progress', 'In progress'))),
          h('h3', { id: titleId, tabIndex: -1 }, (summary ? t('stem.atctower.exercise_debrief', 'Exercise debrief') : t('stem.atctower.exercise_objectives', 'Exercise objectives')) + ' · ' + scenario.title),
          !summary && !progress.complete && h('p', { className: 'atc-mission-goal' }, meta.goal),
          h('div', { className: 'atc-objective-meter' }, h('progress', { 'data-atctower-objective-progress': 'true', max: actions.max, value: actions.value, 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.action_progress', 'Exercise action progress') }),
            h('span', null, actions.value + ' / ' + actions.max + ' ' + (actions.unit === 'landings' ? t('stem.atctower.progress_landings', 'landings') : t('stem.atctower.progress_actions', 'actions')))),
          decisionState && decisionState.verticalFeet != null && h('div', { className: 'atc-practice-measurements', 'data-atctower-separation-progress': 'true' },
            h('div', null, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.actual_vertical_gap', 'Actual altitude gap')), h('strong', { 'data-atctower-vertical-gap': String(decisionState.verticalFeet) }, Math.floor(decisionState.verticalFeet).toLocaleString() + ' / 1,000 ft')),
            h('div', null, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.elapsed_shift_time', 'Elapsed shift time')), h('strong', { 'data-atctower-practice-time': String(decisionState.shiftSeconds) }, decisionState.shiftSeconds.toFixed(1) + ' / 6 s'))),
          h('ul', { className: 'atc-checks' }, goals.map(function(check) { return checkRow(check, false); })),
          (summary || progress.complete || progress.failed) && h('p', { className: progress.failed ? 'atc-notice' : 'atc-muted' }, progress.complete ? (summary ? t('stem.atctower.completed_debrief_feedback', 'All objectives met. Review your decisions, then choose what to practice next.') : t('stem.atctower.completed_live_feedback', 'Objectives achieved. Save your debrief to keep this attempt and review your decisions.')) : progress.feedback),
          h('details', { 'data-atctower-objective-details': 'true', className: 'atc-mission-details' },
            h('summary', null, summary || progress.complete ? t('stem.atctower.safety_evidence', 'Safety evidence') : t('stem.atctower.briefing_safety', 'Briefing & safety rules')),
            !summary && !progress.complete && h('p', null, scenario.briefing), h('ul', { className: 'atc-checks' }, guards.map(function(check) { return checkRow(check, true); })),
            !summary && !progress.complete && h('details', { className: 'atc-mission-details' }, h('summary', null, t('stem.atctower.exercise_hint', 'Show exercise hint')), h('p', null, scenario.hint))),
          !summary && progress.complete && h('button', { className: 'atc-primary', 'data-atctower-save-debrief': 'true', onClick: finishShift }, t('stem.atctower.save_debrief', 'Save debrief') + ' →'),
          !summary && progress.failed && h('button', { 'data-atctower-practice-retry': scenario.id, onClick: function() { finishShift(); startPractice(scenario.id); } }, t('stem.atctower.record_retry_exercise', 'Record attempt and retry')));
      };

      var resumeShift = function() {
        var restored = restoreAtcShift(latestDataRef.current.activeShift);
        if (!restored) { updMulti({ activeShift: null, view: 'menu' }); return; }
        gameRef.current = restored;
        setCommandFeedback(null);
        flightOrderRef.current = null;
        runningRef.current = true;
        tutorialRef.current = { step: 0, active: false, dismissed: true };
        setCommandDraft(null);
        setMathInput('');
        refreshTower();
        updMulti({ view: 'playing', trainingMode: restored.trainingMode });
        announce('Saved shift restored and paused. Resume when you are ready.');
      };
      var discardShift = function() {
        if (gameRef.current.airport && !gameRef.current.committed) gameRef.current.committed = true;
        runningRef.current = false;
        latestDataRef.current = Object.assign({}, latestDataRef.current, { activeShift: null });
        updMulti({ activeShift: null, view: 'menu' });
        announce('Unfinished shift discarded. Career totals unchanged.');
      };
      var startGame = function(airportId) {
        if (latestDataRef.current.activeShift) return;
        var airport = AIRPORTS.find(function(a) { return a.id === airportId; });
        if (!airport) return;
        gameRef.current = {
          shiftId: atcShiftId(), aircraft: [], time: 0, score: 0, landed: 0, conflicts: 0, conflictEpisodes: 0, conflictPairKeys: {}, streak: 0, sessionBestStreak: 0,
          selected: null, airport: airport, gameOver: false, paused: false,
          wind: { dir: Math.floor(Math.random() * 36) * 10, speed: 5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 20) },
          messages: [], mathChallenge: null, nextSpawn: airport.spawnRate, runwayBusyUntil: {}, goArounds: 0
        };
        gameRef.current.trainingMode = latestDataRef.current.trainingMode === 'guided' ? 'guided' : 'standard';
        gameRef.current.reviewNote = atcPreviousReflection(latestDataRef.current, null, airport.id);
        gameRef.current.reviewNoteDismissed = false;
        gameRef.current.paused = gameRef.current.trainingMode === 'guided';
        gameRef.current.decisionLog = [];
        flightOrderRef.current = null;
        tutorialRef.current = { step: 0, active: true, dismissed: false };
        spawnAircraft(airport, true);
        runningRef.current = true;
        setSnapshot(null);
        setCommandFeedback(null);
        checkpointShift();
        upd('view', 'playing');
      };

      useEffect(function() {
        if (view !== 'playing' || !canvasRef.current) return;
        // Versioned drafts restore during mount and always start paused. Older
        // playing states without a valid draft safely return to the menu.
        if (!gameRef.current || !gameRef.current.airport) { upd('view', 'menu'); return; }
        var canvas = canvasRef.current;
        var gfx = null;
        try { gfx = canvas.getContext('2d'); } catch (error) { /* The HTML controls remain usable without radar drawing. */ }
        gameRef.current.radarAvailable = !!gfx;

        var lastFrameTime = null;
        var loop = function(frameTime) {
          if (!runningRef.current) return;
          var game = gameRef.current;
          var elapsed = lastFrameTime == null ? 0 : Math.max(0, Math.min(0.1, (frameTime - lastFrameTime) / 1000));
          lastFrameTime = frameTime;
          var frozen = atcTrafficFrozen(game, document.hidden);
          // Resize canvas resolution to match display (only when size changes)
          var W = canvas.offsetWidth, H = canvas.offsetHeight;
          var pixelRatio = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
          var backingWidth = Math.round(W * pixelRatio), backingHeight = Math.round(H * pixelRatio);
          if (canvas.width !== backingWidth || canvas.height !== backingHeight) { canvas.width = backingWidth; canvas.height = backingHeight; }
          if (gfx && gfx.setTransform) gfx.setTransform(pixelRatio, 0, 0, pixelRatio, 0, 0);
          if (gfx) gfx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
          var dt = frozen ? 0 : elapsed;
          game.time += dt;

          var airport = game.airport;
          var scale = Math.min(W, H) / 400;

          // Spawn timer
          game.nextSpawn -= dt;
          if (dt > 0 && game.trainingMode !== 'guided' && game.nextSpawn <= 0) {
            spawnAircraft(airport);
            game.nextSpawn = airport.spawnRate + Math.random() * 3;
          }

          // Update aircraft positions
          game.aircraft.forEach(function(ac) {
            if (dt <= 0 || ac.state === 'landed' || ac.state === 'departed') return;
            advanceAircraft(ac, dt);
            // Check landing
            if (checkLanding(ac, airport)) {
              if (!runwayAvailable(ac.assignedRunway, airport, game.runwayBusyUntil, game.time)) {
                ac.state = 'goAround';
                ac.targetHeading = ac.assignedRunway.hdg;
                ac.targetAltitude = Math.max(3000, ac.altitude);
                ac.targetSpeed = ac.type.speed; ac.holdCenter = null;
                ac.approachStage = null; ac.touchdownReady = false;
                game.goArounds++;
                recordAtcDecision(game, 'goAround', ac, 'Runway occupied; automatic go-around.');
                game.messages.unshift({ text: '🔄 ' + ac.callsign + ' automatic GO AROUND — runway ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport) + ' occupied. Fly runway heading and climb to ' + Math.round(ac.targetAltitude).toLocaleString() + ' ft.', time: game.time, color: '#f97316' });
                announce(ac.callsign + ' go around. Runway occupied. Fly runway heading and climb to ' + Math.round(ac.targetAltitude) + ' feet.');
              } else {
              ac.state = 'landed';
              ac.landedTime = game.time;
              reserveRunway(ac.assignedRunway, airport, game.runwayBusyUntil, game.time);
              game.landed++;
              game.streak++;
              game.sessionBestStreak = Math.max(game.sessionBestStreak, game.streak);
              var baseScore = ac.type.cat === 'Super' ? 50 : ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' ? 30 : ac.type.cat === 'Medium' ? 20 : 10;
              var emergBonus = ac.emergency ? 40 : 0;
              game.score += baseScore + emergBonus;
              if (ac.emergency) {
                game.messages.unshift({ text: '🏥 ' + ac.callsign + ' emergency resolved! +' + emergBonus + ' bonus. ' + ac.emergency.lesson, time: game.time, color: '#a78bfa' });
                announce(ac.callsign + ' emergency aircraft landed safely. Bonus ' + emergBonus + ' points. ' + ac.emergency.lesson);
              }
              game.messages.unshift({ text: '✅ ' + ac.callsign + ' landed RWY ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport), time: game.time, color: '#4ade80' });
              recordAtcDecision(game, 'landing', ac, 'Landed runway ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport) + (ac.emergency ? '; emergency resolved.' : '.'));
              // Touchdown visual effect
              if (!game.touchdowns) game.touchdowns = [];
              game.touchdowns.push({ x: ac.x, y: ac.y, time: game.time, color: ac.type.color });
              playATCSound('land');
              announce(ac.callsign + ' landed. Score: ' + game.score + '. Streak: ' + game.streak);
              if (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super') game.landedHeavy = true;
              if (tutorialRef.current.active && tutorialRef.current.step === 4) tutorialRef.current.step = 5;
              // Trigger math challenge every 3 landings
              if (!game.practice && game.landed % 3 === 0 && !game.mathChallenge) {
                var template = MATH_CHALLENGES[Math.floor(Math.random() * MATH_CHALLENGES.length)];
                game.mathChallenge = template.generate();
                game.mathChallenge.type = template.type;
              }
              }
            }
            // Off-screen departure
            if (ac.x < -20 || ac.x > 420 || ac.y < -20 || ac.y > 420) {
              if (ac.state === 'goAround') { ac.state = 'departed'; recordPracticeOutcome(game, 'lost', ac); }
              else if (ac.state !== 'landed') {
                ac.state = 'departed';
                game.score -= 10; game.streak = 0;
                recordAtcDecision(game, 'missed', ac, 'Left airspace before landing.');
                game.messages.unshift({ text: '❌ ' + ac.callsign + ' left airspace without clearance!', time: game.time, color: '#ef4444' });
              }
            }
            // Fuel emergency
            if (ac.fuel < 5 && !ac.fuelWarningIssued && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed') {
              ac.fuelWarningIssued = true;
              recordAtcDecision(game, 'fuel', ac, 'Fuel below five flight minutes; priority review.');
              game.messages.unshift({ text: '🔴 ' + ac.callsign + ' FUEL EMERGENCY — priority landing!', time: game.time, color: '#ef4444' });
              announce(ac.callsign + ' fuel emergency. Priority landing required.');
            }
            if (ac.fuel <= 0 && ac.state !== 'landed' && ac.state !== 'departed') {
              ac.state = 'departed'; game.score -= 20; game.streak = 0;
              recordAtcDecision(game, 'fuel', ac, 'Fuel exhausted before landing.');
              game.messages.unshift({ text: '❌ ' + ac.callsign + ' exhausted fuel before landing. Shift safety review required.', time: game.time, color: '#ef4444' });
            }
          });

          // Remove old aircraft
          game.aircraft = game.aircraft.filter(function(ac) { return ac.state !== 'landed' || game.time - ac.landedTime < 3; });
          game.aircraft = game.aircraft.filter(function(ac) { return ac.state !== 'departed'; });
          if (!game.aircraft.some(function(ac) { return ac.id === game.selected && ac.state !== 'landed'; })) game.selected = null;

          // Check conflicts
          var conflicts = checkConflicts();
          var pairKeys = {};
          conflicts.forEach(function(conflict) {
            var key = [conflict.a.id, conflict.b.id].sort().join(':');
            pairKeys[key] = true;
            if (!game.conflictPairKeys[key] && dt > 0) {
              game.conflictEpisodes++;
              recordAtcDecision(game, 'conflict', conflict.a, conflict.a.callsign + ' / ' + conflict.b.callsign + ': ' + conflict.dist.toFixed(1) + ' nm; minimum ' + conflict.min + ' nm.');
              game.messages.unshift({ text: '⚠️ ' + conflict.a.callsign + ' / ' + conflict.b.callsign + ': ' + conflict.dist.toFixed(1) + ' nm, minimum ' + conflict.min + ' nm.', time: game.time, color: '#ef4444' });
              announce('Separation alert. ' + conflict.a.callsign + ' and ' + conflict.b.callsign + '. ' + conflict.dist.toFixed(1) + ' nautical miles apart.');
            }
          });
          if (dt > 0) game.conflictPairKeys = pairKeys;
          if (conflicts.length > 0 && dt > 0) { game.conflicts += dt; game.streak = 0; }
          game.messages = game.messages.slice(0, 40);
          // Game over if too many conflicts
          if (game.trainingMode !== 'guided' && game.conflicts > 15 && !game.gameOver) { game.gameOver = true; announce('Game over. Too many separation conflicts. Final score: ' + game.score + '. Aircraft landed: ' + game.landed); }
          var exercise = scenarioProgress(game);
          if (exercise && exercise.complete && !game.practice.completed) {
            game.practice.completed = true; game.practice.completedAt = game.time; game.paused = true;
            recordAtcDecision(game, 'exercise', null, exercise.scenario.title + ': objectives met.');
            checkpointShift(); refreshTower();
            announce('Exercise objectives met. Traffic paused. End the shift to save your debrief.');
          }

          // ═══ RENDER ═══
          checkATCBadges(game, game.time);
          if (game.touchdowns) game.touchdowns = game.touchdowns.filter(function(td) { return game.time - td.time < 3; });
          if (gfx) {
          // High contrast color scheme
          var hc = !!latestDataRef.current.highContrast || !!(window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(forced-colors: active)').matches);
          var colors = {
            bg: hc ? '#000000' : '#071522',
            radar: hc ? 'rgba(255,255,0,' : 'rgba(94,234,212,',
            text: hc ? '#ffff00' : '#99f6e4',
            textDim: hc ? 'rgba(255,255,0,0.7)' : 'rgba(173,219,225,0.55)',
            rwy: hc ? '#ffff00' : '#99f6e4',
            conflict: hc ? '#ff0000' : '#ef4444',
          };

          // Background — dark radar scope with subtle radial gradient
          var bgGrad = gfx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 0, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
          bgGrad.addColorStop(0, hc ? '#0a0a0a' : '#0c2431');
          bgGrad.addColorStop(0.5, hc ? '#050505' : '#071923');
          bgGrad.addColorStop(1, hc ? '#000000' : '#050f19');
          gfx.fillStyle = bgGrad;
          gfx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

          // Subtle scanline effect (CRT phosphor look)
          if (!reducedMotion.current && !hc) {
            gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.03)';
            for (var sl = 0; sl < H; sl += 3) { gfx.fillRect(0, sl, W, 1); }
          }

          // Outer scope ring (bezel)
          if (!hc) {
            var scopeR = Math.min(W, H) * 0.48;
            gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(147,194,210,0.22)'; gfx.lineWidth = 2;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, scopeR, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(147,194,210,0.08)'; gfx.lineWidth = 1;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, scopeR + 3, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
          }

          // Focus ring on canvas (WCAG 2.4.7)
          if (document.activeElement === canvas) {
            gfx.strokeStyle = hc ? '#ffff00' : '#4ade80'; gfx.lineWidth = 3;
            gfx.strokeRect(1, 1, W - 2, H - 2);
          }

          // Radar sweep effect — realistic rotating beam with phosphor decay
          if (!reducedMotion.current) {
            var sweepAngle = (game.time * 0.5) % (Math.PI * 2);
            var sweepGrad = gfx.createConicGradient(sweepAngle, W / 2, H / 2);
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(0, colors.radar + '0.12)');
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(0.02, colors.radar + '0.08)');
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(0.06, colors.radar + '0.03)');
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(0.15, colors.radar + '0.015)');
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(0.35, colors.radar + '0)');
            sweepGrad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(34,197,94,0)');
            gfx.fillStyle = sweepGrad;
            gfx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
            // Sweep line (bright leading edge)
            gfx.save();
            gfx.translate(W / 2, H / 2);
            gfx.rotate(sweepAngle);
            var sweepLen = Math.max(W, H) * 0.7;
            var lineGrad = gfx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, -sweepLen);
            lineGrad.addColorStop(0, colors.radar + '0.4)');
            lineGrad.addColorStop(0.3, colors.radar + '0.15)');
            lineGrad.addColorStop(1, colors.radar + '0)');
            gfx.strokeStyle = lineGrad; gfx.lineWidth = 1.5;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, 0); gfx.lineTo(0, -sweepLen); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.restore();
          }

          // Range rings — with intermediate dotted rings
          [40, 60, 80, 120, 160, 180].forEach(function(r2) {
            var rPx = r2 * scale;
            var isMajor = r2 % 60 === 0;
            gfx.strokeStyle = isMajor ? (colors.radar + '0.22)') : (colors.radar + '0.08)');
            gfx.lineWidth = isMajor ? 0.8 : 0.4;
            if (!isMajor) { gfx.setLineDash([2, 4]); }
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(W / 2, H / 2, rPx, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
            if (!isMajor) { gfx.setLineDash([]); }
          });
          // Range ring labels
          gfx.fillStyle = colors.radar + '0.82)'; gfx.font = '10px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'right';
          [60, 120, 180].forEach(function(r2) {
            gfx.fillText(Math.round(r2 / ATC_UNITS_PER_NM) + 'nm', W / 2 + r2 * scale - 5, H / 2 - 3);
          });
          // Center crosshair
          gfx.strokeStyle = colors.radar + '0.08)'; gfx.lineWidth = 0.5;
          gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(W / 2 - 6, H / 2); gfx.lineTo(W / 2 + 6, H / 2); gfx.stroke();
          gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(W / 2, H / 2 - 6); gfx.lineTo(W / 2, H / 2 + 6); gfx.stroke();

          // Compass cardinals + intercardinals
          gfx.fillStyle = hc ? '#ffff00' : 'rgba(173,219,225,0.8)'; gfx.font = 'bold 11px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
          gfx.fillText('N', W / 2, 14); gfx.fillText('S', W / 2, H - 6);
          gfx.fillText('E', W - 10, H / 2 + 4); gfx.fillText('W', 12, H / 2 + 4);
          gfx.fillStyle = hc ? '#ffff00' : 'rgba(147,194,210,0.72)'; gfx.font = '9px monospace';
          var cRad = Math.min(W, H) * 0.45;
          [['NE', 45], ['SE', 135], ['SW', 225], ['NW', 315]].forEach(function(ic) {
            var rad = toRad(ic[1]);
            gfx.fillText(ic[0], W / 2 + Math.sin(rad) * cRad, H / 2 - Math.cos(rad) * cRad + 3);
          });

          // Compass tick marks (every 10 degrees)
          gfx.strokeStyle = hc ? '#ffff00' : 'rgba(147,194,210,0.35)'; gfx.lineWidth = 0.5;
          for (var ct = 0; ct < 36; ct++) {
            var cAngle = ct * 10 * Math.PI / 180;
            var inner2 = ct % 3 === 0 ? cRad - 8 : cRad - 4;
            gfx.beginPath();
            gfx.moveTo(W / 2 + Math.sin(cAngle) * inner2, H / 2 - Math.cos(cAngle) * inner2);
            gfx.lineTo(W / 2 + Math.sin(cAngle) * cRad, H / 2 - Math.cos(cAngle) * cRad);
            gfx.stroke();
          }

          // Wind sock (bottom-left corner) — animated with multi-stripe pattern
          var wsX = 40; var wsY = H - 55;
          gfx.save();
          gfx.translate(wsX, wsY);
          // Pole with ball top
          gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)'; gfx.lineWidth = 2;
          gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, 0); gfx.lineTo(0, -24); gfx.stroke();
          gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)';
          gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(0, -24, 2, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
          // Sock segments (alternating red/white, drooping with wind)
          var windRad = toRad(game.wind.dir);
          var sockLen = Math.min(28, game.wind.speed * 0.9);
          var sockWave = reducedMotion.current ? 0 : Math.sin(game.time * 4) * 2;
          var sockWave2 = reducedMotion.current ? 0 : Math.sin(game.time * 3 + 1) * 1.5;
          var segCount = 5;
          for (var sg = 0; sg < segCount; sg++) {
            var t1 = sg / segCount; var t2 = (sg + 1) / segCount;
            var sx1 = Math.sin(windRad) * sockLen * t1 + sockWave * t1;
            var sy1 = -24 - Math.cos(windRad) * sockLen * t1 + sockWave2 * t1 * t1;
            var sx2 = Math.sin(windRad) * sockLen * t2 + sockWave * t2;
            var sy2 = -24 - Math.cos(windRad) * sockLen * t2 + sockWave2 * t2 * t2;
            gfx.strokeStyle = sg % 2 === 0 ? (game.wind.speed > 20 ? '#ef4444' : game.wind.speed > 10 ? '#f97316' : '#ef4444') : '#ffffff';
            gfx.lineWidth = 4 - sg * 0.5;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(sx1, sy1); gfx.lineTo(sx2, sy2); gfx.stroke();
          }
          gfx.restore();
          // Wind info text
          gfx.fillStyle = hc ? '#ffff00' : 'rgba(240,247,251,0.85)'; gfx.font = 'bold 9px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
          gfx.fillText(String(game.wind.dir).padStart(3, '0') + '°/' + game.wind.speed + 'kt', wsX, H - 32);

          // Runways — enhanced with approach lights, threshold, taxiway stubs
          airport.runways.forEach(function(rwy) {
            var rx = rwy.x * scale; var ry = rwy.y * scale;
            var rwyRad = toRad(rwy.hdg);
            var dx = Math.sin(rwyRad) * rwy.len * scale / 2;
            var dy = -Math.cos(rwyRad) * rwy.len * scale / 2;
            // Runway surface (darker base)
            gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(100,100,100,0.4)'; gfx.lineWidth = 6 * scale;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(rx - dx, ry - dy); gfx.lineTo(rx + dx, ry + dy); gfx.stroke();
            // Runway centerline
            gfx.strokeStyle = colors.rwy; gfx.lineWidth = 1.5 * scale;
            gfx.setLineDash([4 * scale, 3 * scale]);
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(rx - dx, ry - dy); gfx.lineTo(rx + dx, ry + dy); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.setLineDash([]);
            // Runway edge lights
            gfx.strokeStyle = colors.rwy + (hc ? '' : '80'); gfx.lineWidth = 0.5;
            var perpX = Math.cos(rwyRad) * 3 * scale; var perpY = Math.sin(rwyRad) * 3 * scale;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(rx - dx + perpX, ry - dy + perpY); gfx.lineTo(rx + dx + perpX, ry + dy + perpY); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(rx - dx - perpX, ry - dy - perpY); gfx.lineTo(rx + dx - perpX, ry + dy - perpY); gfx.stroke();
            // Threshold bars
            for (var tb = -2; tb <= 2; tb++) {
              var tbOff = tb * 1.2 * scale;
              var tbPx = perpX * (tb / 2.5); var tbPy = perpY * (tb / 2.5);
              gfx.fillStyle = colors.rwy;
              gfx.fillRect(rx - dx + tbPx - 1, ry - dy + tbPy - 1, 2, 2);
            }
            // Runway label
            gfx.fillStyle = colors.rwy; gfx.font = 'bold ' + (9 * scale) + 'px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
            var rwyNum = runwayLabel(rwy, airport);
            gfx.fillText(rwyNum, rx, ry + 16 * scale);
            // ILS localizer cone (approach path visualization)
            gfx.save();
            gfx.translate(rx - dx, ry - dy);
            gfx.rotate(rwyRad);
            var coneLen = 36 * scale;
            var coneGrad = gfx.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, coneLen);
            coneGrad.addColorStop(0, colors.radar + '0.08)');
            coneGrad.addColorStop(1, colors.radar + '0)');
            gfx.fillStyle = coneGrad;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, 0);
            gfx.lineTo(-15 * scale, coneLen);
            gfx.lineTo(15 * scale, coneLen);
            gfx.closePath(); gfx.fill();
            // ILS centerline
            gfx.strokeStyle = colors.radar + '0.12)'; gfx.lineWidth = 0.8; gfx.setLineDash([4, 6]);
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, 0); gfx.lineTo(0, coneLen); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.setLineDash([]);
            // Approach lights (series of dots along centerline)
            for (var al = 1; al <= 5; al++) {
              var alY = al * 12 * scale;
              var alAlpha = (0.4 - al * 0.06) * (reducedMotion.current ? 1 : 0.65 + 0.35 * Math.max(0, Math.sin(game.time * 2.5 + al * 1.1)));
              gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,200,50,' + alAlpha + ')';
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(0, alY, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
              // Side lights
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(-5 * scale, alY, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(5 * scale, alY, 1, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
            }
            gfx.restore();
            // Taxiway stubs (short lines perpendicular to runway ends)
            gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(59,130,246,0.15)'; gfx.lineWidth = 2 * scale;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(rx + dx, ry + dy); gfx.lineTo(rx + dx + perpX * 4, ry + dy + perpY * 4); gfx.stroke();
          });

          // Aircraft labels reserve room for selected and urgent traffic.
          var radarTags = atcRadarTagLayout(game.aircraft, W, H, game.selected, conflicts.reduce(function(ids, pair) { return ids.concat([pair.a.id, pair.b.id]); }, []));
          radarTagsRef.current = radarTags;
          radarTags.forEach(function(tag) {
            var ac = game.aircraft.find(function(item) { return item.id === tag.id; });
            if (ac.state === 'landed' || ac.state === 'departed') return;
            var ax = ac.x * scale; var ay = ac.y * scale;
            var isSelected = game.selected === ac.id;
            var isConflict = conflicts.some(function(c) { return c.a.id === ac.id || c.b.id === ac.id; });

            // Target line (if heading assigned)
            if (isSelected && ac.targetHeading !== null) {
              gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(251,191,36,0.3)'; gfx.lineWidth = 1; gfx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(ax, ay);
              gfx.lineTo(ax + Math.sin(toRad(ac.targetHeading)) * 60 * scale, ay - Math.cos(toRad(ac.targetHeading)) * 60 * scale);
              gfx.stroke(); gfx.setLineDash([]);
            }

            // Radar afterglow trail (fading blips from previous sweep)
            var blipColor = hc ? '#ffff00' : isConflict ? '#fb7185' : tag.priority ? '#fbbf24' : ac.fuel < 10 ? '#fb923c' : ac.emergency ? '#c4b5fd' : '#67e8f9';
            // Emergency aircraft: pulsing red ring + label
            if (ac.emergency) {
              var emAlpha = reducedMotion.current ? 0.8 : 0.65 + Math.sin(game.time * 5) * 0.15;
              gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,' + emAlpha + ')'; gfx.lineWidth = 2;
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(ax, ay, 20 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
              gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,' + emAlpha + ')'; gfx.font = 'bold ' + (7 * scale) + 'px system-ui'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
              gfx.fillText('🚨 ' + ac.emergency.label, ax, ay - 18 * scale);
            }
            for (var tr = 6; tr >= 1; tr--) {
              var trX = ax - Math.sin(toRad(ac.heading)) * tr * 4 * scale;
              var trY = ay + Math.cos(toRad(ac.heading)) * tr * 4 * scale;
              var trAlpha = (1 - tr / 7) * 0.2;
              gfx.fillStyle = isConflict ? 'rgba(239,68,68,' + trAlpha + ')' : 'rgba(34,197,94,' + trAlpha + ')';
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(trX, trY, (2 + (6 - tr) * 0.3) * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
            }

            // Radar blip glow (bloom effect) — brighter for selected/emergency
            var glowMult = isSelected ? 2.2 : ac.emergency ? 2.0 : 1.5;
            var glowSize = ac.type.size * scale * glowMult;
            var glowGrad = gfx.createRadialGradient(ax, ay, 0, ax, ay, glowSize);
            glowGrad.addColorStop(0, blipColor + (isSelected ? '60' : '40'));
            glowGrad.addColorStop(0.5, blipColor + '15');
            glowGrad.addColorStop(1, blipColor + '00');
            gfx.fillStyle = glowGrad;
            gfx.fillRect(ax - glowSize, ay - glowSize, glowSize * 2, glowSize * 2);

            // Aircraft blip (detailed silhouette with altitude-scaled size)
            var altScale = 0.7 + Math.min(ac.altitude, 10000) / 10000 * 0.6; // bigger at higher altitude
            gfx.fillStyle = blipColor;
            gfx.save(); gfx.translate(ax, ay); gfx.rotate(toRad(ac.heading));
            var sz = ac.type.size * scale * altScale;
            if (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super') {
              // Wide-body — fuselage + swept wings + tail
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, -sz * 0.8); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.12, -sz * 0.2);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.6, sz * 0.05); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.55, sz * 0.15);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.12, sz * 0.1); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.12, sz * 0.5);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.3, sz * 0.65); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.25, sz * 0.7);
              gfx.lineTo(0, sz * 0.55);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.25, sz * 0.7); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.3, sz * 0.65);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.12, sz * 0.5); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.12, sz * 0.1);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.55, sz * 0.15); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.6, sz * 0.05);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.12, -sz * 0.2); gfx.closePath(); gfx.fill();
            } else if (ac.type.cat === 'Light') {
              // Small prop — simple shape with high wing
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, -sz * 0.6);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.08, -sz * 0.1); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.45, -sz * 0.05);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.4, sz * 0.05); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.08, sz * 0.1);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.08, sz * 0.4); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.2, sz * 0.5);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.2, sz * 0.5); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.08, sz * 0.4);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.08, sz * 0.1); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.4, sz * 0.05);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.45, -sz * 0.05); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.08, -sz * 0.1);
              gfx.closePath(); gfx.fill();
            } else {
              // Narrow-body — B737/A320 shape
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(0, -sz * 0.7);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.1, -sz * 0.15); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.5, sz * 0.05);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.45, sz * 0.12); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.1, sz * 0.08);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.1, sz * 0.45); gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.22, sz * 0.55);
              gfx.lineTo(-sz * 0.18, sz * 0.6);
              gfx.lineTo(0, sz * 0.48);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.18, sz * 0.6); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.22, sz * 0.55);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.1, sz * 0.45); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.1, sz * 0.08);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.45, sz * 0.12); gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.5, sz * 0.05);
              gfx.lineTo(sz * 0.1, -sz * 0.15); gfx.closePath(); gfx.fill();
            }
            // Anti-collision strobe (white flash at wing tips)
            if (!reducedMotion.current) {
              var strobeA = Math.max(0, Math.sin(game.time * 4.71)) * 0.7;
              gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + strobeA.toFixed(3) + ')';
              var wingW = (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super') ? sz * 0.6 : ac.type.cat === 'Light' ? sz * 0.4 : sz * 0.5;
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(-wingW, 0, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(wingW, 0, 1.2, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
            }
            // Navigation lights (red left, green right)
            if (!hc) {
              var navW = (ac.type.cat === 'Heavy' || ac.type.cat === 'Super') ? sz * 0.58 : ac.type.cat === 'Light' ? sz * 0.38 : sz * 0.48;
              gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,0.6)'; gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(-navW, 0, 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
              gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(34,197,94,0.6)'; gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(navW, 0, 0.8, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
            }
            gfx.restore();

            // Speed vector line (predicted position in 60 seconds)
            if (isSelected || ac.state === 'approach') {
            var predDist = ac.speed / 3600 * ATC_UNITS_PER_NM * 60 * scale;
              var predX = ax + Math.sin(toRad(ac.heading)) * predDist;
              var predY = ay - Math.cos(toRad(ac.heading)) * predDist;
              gfx.strokeStyle = blipColor + '30'; gfx.lineWidth = 1;
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(ax, ay); gfx.lineTo(predX, predY); gfx.stroke();
              // 60-second position marker
