              gfx.strokeStyle = blipColor + '50'; gfx.lineWidth = 0.5;
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(predX, predY, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
            }

            // Selection ring with rotating dashes
            if (isSelected) {
              gfx.strokeStyle = '#fbbf24'; gfx.lineWidth = 1.5;
              gfx.save(); gfx.translate(ax, ay); gfx.rotate(reducedMotion.current ? 0 : game.time * 2);
              gfx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(0, 0, 14 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
              gfx.setLineDash([]);
              gfx.restore();
            }

            // Conflict ring (pulsing + distance line between conflicting aircraft)
            if (isConflict) {
              var confAlpha = reducedMotion.current ? 0.8 : 0.5 + Math.sin(game.time * 6) * 0.3;
              gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,' + confAlpha + ')';
              gfx.lineWidth = 2;
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(ax, ay, 16 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
              // Draw separation line to conflicting partner
              conflicts.forEach(function(c) {
                if (c.a.id === ac.id) {
                  gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(239,68,68,' + (confAlpha * 0.5) + ')'; gfx.lineWidth = 1;
                  gfx.setLineDash([2, 2]);
                  gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(ax, ay); gfx.lineTo(c.b.x * scale, c.b.y * scale); gfx.stroke();
                  gfx.setLineDash([]);
                  // Distance label on the line
                  var midX = (ax + c.b.x * scale) / 2; var midY = (ay + c.b.y * scale) / 2;
                  gfx.fillStyle = '#ef4444'; gfx.font = 'bold 8px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
                  gfx.fillText(c.dist.toFixed(1) + 'nm', midX, midY - 4);
                }
              });
            }

          });

          // Screen-sized tags with units, full callsigns, bounded placement and click targets.
          radarTags.forEach(function(tag) {
            var ac = game.aircraft.find(function(item) { return item.id === tag.id; });
            var accent = hc ? '#ffff00' : tag.conflict ? '#fb7185' : tag.priority ? '#fbbf24' : tag.selected ? '#99f6e4' : '#638da5';
            gfx.strokeStyle = accent; gfx.lineWidth = tag.selected ? 1.8 : 1;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.moveTo(tag.anchorX, tag.anchorY); gfx.lineTo(Math.max(tag.x, Math.min(tag.x + tag.width, tag.anchorX)), Math.max(tag.y, Math.min(tag.y + tag.height, tag.anchorY))); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.fillStyle = hc ? '#000000' : tag.selected ? '#15383e' : '#0e2030';
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.roundRect(tag.x, tag.y, tag.width, tag.height, 5); gfx.fill(); gfx.stroke();
            gfx.fillStyle = accent; gfx.fillRect(tag.x + 1, tag.y + 5, 3, tag.height - 10);
            gfx.textAlign = 'left'; gfx.fillStyle = '#f1f9ff'; gfx.font = 'bold 12px monospace';
            gfx.fillText(tag.callsign, tag.x + 9, tag.y + 16, tag.width - 16);
            gfx.font = '11px monospace'; gfx.fillStyle = hc ? '#ffffff' : '#d7e8f3';
            gfx.fillText(Math.round(ac.altitude).toLocaleString() + ' ft · ' + Math.round(ac.speed) + ' kt', tag.x + 9, tag.y + 32, tag.width - 16);
            var phase = ac.state === 'approach' ? (ac.approachStage === 'final' ? 'FINAL' : 'ENTRY') : ac.state === 'holding' ? 'HOLD' : ac.state === 'goAround' ? 'GO AROUND' : ac.type.id;
            var info = ac.fuel < 10 ? 'FUEL ' + Math.max(0, ac.fuel).toFixed(1) + ' min' : ac.assignedRunway ? 'RWY ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport) : String(Math.round(ac.heading)).padStart(3, '0') + '°';
            gfx.fillText(phase + ' · ' + info, tag.x + 9, tag.y + 47, tag.width - 16);
          });

          // ── Touchdown Effects (expanding rings on landing) ──
          if (game.touchdowns) {
            game.touchdowns = game.touchdowns.filter(function(td) { return game.time - td.time < 3; });
            game.touchdowns.forEach(function(td) {
              if (reducedMotion.current) return;
              var age = game.time - td.time;
              var alpha = Math.max(0, 1 - age / 3);
              var radius = age * 25 * scale;
              // Expanding ring
              gfx.strokeStyle = td.color + Math.round(alpha * 60).toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
              gfx.lineWidth = 2 * (1 - age / 3);
              gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(td.x * scale, td.y * scale, radius, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.stroke();
              // Inner glow
              if (age < 1) {
                var tdGlow = gfx.createRadialGradient(td.x * scale, td.y * scale, 0, td.x * scale, td.y * scale, radius * 0.6);
                tdGlow.addColorStop(0, td.color + Math.round(alpha * 30).toString(16).padStart(2, '0'));
                tdGlow.addColorStop(1, td.color + '00');
                gfx.fillStyle = tdGlow;
                gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(td.x * scale, td.y * scale, radius * 0.6, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
              }
              // Checkmark flash
              if (age < 0.8) {
                gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (alpha * 0.8) + ')';
                gfx.font = 'bold ' + Math.round(14 * scale * (1 + age * 0.5)) + 'px system-ui'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
                gfx.fillText('✓', td.x * scale, td.y * scale - radius - 5);
              }
            });
          }

          // ── Holding Pattern Visualization ──
          game.aircraft.forEach(function(ac) {
            if (ac.state !== 'holding') return;
            var hx = (ac.holdCenter ? ac.holdCenter.x : ac.x) * scale;
            var hy = (ac.holdCenter ? ac.holdCenter.y : ac.y) * scale;
            gfx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(167,139,250,0.2)'; gfx.lineWidth = 1;
            gfx.setLineDash([3, 3]);
            // The simplified circular hold matches the actual guidance path.
            gfx.beginPath();
            gfx.arc(hx, hy, 24 * scale, 0, Math.PI * 2);
            gfx.stroke();
            gfx.setLineDash([]);
            gfx.fillStyle = '#a78bfa'; gfx.font = '7px system-ui'; gfx.textAlign = 'center';
            gfx.fillText('HOLD', hx, hy - 27 * scale);
          });

          // ── Runway Status Indicators (near each runway) ──
          airport.runways.forEach(function(rwy) {
            var rx = rwy.x * scale; var ry = rwy.y * scale;
            // Count aircraft on approach to this runway
            var approachCount = game.aircraft.filter(function(a) { return a.state === 'approach' && a.assignedRunway === rwy; }).length;
            var inboundCount = game.aircraft.filter(function(a) { return a.assignedRunway === rwy && a.state === 'inbound'; }).length;
            var isOccupied = !runwayAvailable(rwy, airport, game.runwayBusyUntil, game.time);
            // Status light
            var statusColor = isOccupied ? '#f97316' : approachCount > 0 ? '#4ade80' : inboundCount > 0 ? '#fbbf24' : '#94a3b8';
            gfx.fillStyle = statusColor;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.arc(rx + 25 * scale, ry, 3, 0, Math.PI * 2); gfx.fill();
            gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)'; gfx.font = '7px monospace'; gfx.textAlign = 'left';
            gfx.fillText((isOccupied ? 'BUSY' : approachCount > 0 ? approachCount + ' ILS' : inboundCount > 0 ? inboundCount + ' seq' : 'idle'), rx + 30 * scale, ry + 3);
          });

          // ── Achievement Badge Popup ──
          var pop = atcBadgePopRef.current;
          if (pop && game.time - pop.time < 5) {
            var popAlpha = Math.min(1, (5 - (game.time - pop.time)) / 1.5);
            gfx.globalAlpha = popAlpha;
            gfx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.85)';
            var popW2 = 200; var popX2 = W - popW2 - 10; var popY2 = H - 160;
            gfx.beginPath(); gfx.roundRect(popX2, popY2, popW2, 45, 8); gfx.fill();
            gfx.strokeStyle = '#fbbf24'; gfx.lineWidth = 2; gfx.stroke();
            gfx.fillStyle = '#fbbf24'; gfx.font = 'bold 9px system-ui'; gfx.textAlign = 'left';
            gfx.fillText('🏆 BADGE EARNED!', popX2 + 8, popY2 + 14);
            gfx.fillStyle = '#fff'; gfx.font = 'bold 12px system-ui';
            gfx.fillText(pop.badge.icon + ' ' + pop.badge.name, popX2 + 8, popY2 + 32);
            gfx.globalAlpha = 1;
          }

          }
          // ── Conflict sound ──
          if (conflicts.length > 0 && Math.floor(game.time * 2) % 2 === 0 && Math.floor((game.time - dt) * 2) % 2 !== 0) {
            playATCSound('conflict');
          }

          animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        };

        // Mouse click handler
        var onClick = function(e) {
          var game = gameRef.current;
          if (!game || !game.aircraft) return;
          if (game.commandEntry) return;
          var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
          // Convert mouse coords from CSS display space to canvas pixel space
          var cssX = e.clientX - rect.left;
          var cssY = e.clientY - rect.top;
          var tagHit = radarTagsRef.current.slice().reverse().find(function(tag) { return cssX >= tag.x && cssX <= tag.x + tag.width && cssY >= tag.y && cssY <= tag.y + tag.height; });
          if (tagHit) { selectAircraft(tagHit.id); return; }
          var mx = cssX * (canvas.width / rect.width);
          var my = cssY * (canvas.height / rect.height);
          var scl = Math.min(canvas.width, canvas.height) / 400;
          var closest = null; var closestDist = 35 * (canvas.width / rect.width);
          game.aircraft.forEach(function(ac) {
            if (ac.state === 'landed' || ac.state === 'departed') return;
            var dist2 = distNm(mx, my, ac.x * scl, ac.y * scl);
            if (dist2 < closestDist) { closestDist = dist2; closest = ac; }
          });
          game.selected = closest ? closest.id : null;
          setCommandFeedback(null);
          if (closest) {
            game.messages.unshift({ text: '📻 Selected: ' + closest.callsign + ' (' + closest.type.name + ')', time: game.time, color: '#60a5fa' });
            playATCSound('select');
            announce('Selected ' + closest.callsign + ', ' + closest.type.name + ', altitude ' + closest.altitude + ' feet, heading ' + Math.round(closest.heading) + ' degrees, ' + Math.round(closest.speed) + ' knots');
            if (tutorialRef.current.active && tutorialRef.current.step === 0) tutorialRef.current.step = 1;
          }
        };

        // Shortcuts apply only while the radar itself has focus. Tab leaves the scope.
        var issueCommand = function(key, runwayIndex) {
          var game = gameRef.current;
          if (key === 'pause') { togglePause(); return; }
          if (key === 'help') { game.showHelp = !game.showHelp; refreshTower(); return; }
          if (key === 'end') { finishShift(); return; }
          if (key === 'next') { cycleSelection(1); return; }
          if (key === 'previous') { cycleSelection(-1); return; }
          if (game.gameOver || game.commandEntry || (game.practice && game.practice.completed)) return;
          if (key === 'add') {
            if (game.trainingMode !== 'guided' || game.practice || game.mathChallenge) return;
            spawnAircraft(game.airport);
            refreshTower();
            return;
          }
          var sel = game.aircraft.find(function(a) { return a.id === game.selected && a.state !== 'landed' && a.state !== 'departed'; });
          if (!sel) { showCommandFeedback('blocked', 'Select an aircraft first.', null); return; }
          var message;
          var immediate = atcImmediateCommandInfo(sel, key, game.airport, runwayIndex);
          if (key === 'h' || key === 's') {
            game.commandEntry = true;
            setCommandFeedback(null);
            setCommandDraft({ aircraftId: sel.id, callsign: sel.callsign, kind: key === 'h' ? 'heading' : 'speed',
              value: String(Math.round(key === 'h' ? (sel.targetHeading == null ? sel.heading : sel.targetHeading) : (sel.targetSpeed == null ? sel.speed : sel.targetSpeed))), error: '' });
            refreshTower();
            return;
          }
          if (key === 'r') {
            var nextRunway = immediate.nextRunway;
            if (nextRunway === sel.assignedRunway) { showCommandFeedback('unchanged', sel.callsign + ' runway assignment is unchanged.', sel); return; }
            sel.assignedRunway = nextRunway;
            sel.touchdownReady = false;
            if (sel.state === 'approach') { sel.state = 'inbound'; sel.approachStage = null; sel.targetHeading = null; sel.targetAltitude = sel.altitude; }
            message = sel.callsign + (nextRunway ? ' assigned runway ' + runwayLabel(nextRunway, game.airport) + '. Approach clearance is required.' : ' runway assignment removed.');
            if (tutorialRef.current.step === 1) tutorialRef.current.step = 2;
          } else if (key === 'c') {
            if (!sel.assignedRunway) { showCommandFeedback('blocked', 'Assign a runway before clearing approach.', sel); return; }
            if (sel.state === 'approach') { showCommandFeedback('unchanged', sel.callsign + ' is already cleared for this approach. Current guidance and speed instruction are retained.', sel); return; }
            sel.state = 'approach';
            sel.approachStage = 'entry';
            sel.touchdownReady = false;
            sel.holdCenter = null;
            sel.targetSpeed = sel.type.speed;
            message = sel.callsign + ' cleared approach runway ' + runwayLabel(sel.assignedRunway, game.airport) + '. Automatic guidance will intercept final and descend.';
            tutorialRef.current.step = 4;
            playATCSound('clear');
          } else if (key === 'g') {
            if (sel.state === 'goAround') { showCommandFeedback('unchanged', sel.callsign + ' go-around instruction is already active.', sel); return; }
            if (sel.state === 'approach') sel.targetSpeed = sel.type.speed;
            if (sel.state !== 'goAround') game.goArounds = (game.goArounds || 0) + 1;
            sel.state = 'goAround'; sel.approachStage = null; sel.holdCenter = null; sel.touchdownReady = false;
            sel.targetHeading = sel.assignedRunway ? sel.assignedRunway.hdg : ((sel.heading + 180) % 360);
            sel.targetAltitude = Math.min(45000, Math.max(3000, sel.altitude + 1000));
            message = sel.callsign + ' go around, heading ' + Math.round(sel.targetHeading) + ', climb to ' + Math.round(sel.targetAltitude) + ' feet.';
          } else if (key === 'p') {
            if (immediate.cancelsApproach) { sel.targetAltitude = immediate.targetAltitude; sel.targetHeading = null; }
            sel.state = sel.state === 'holding' ? 'inbound' : 'holding';
            sel.holdCenter = null; sel.approachStage = null; sel.touchdownReady = false;
            message = sel.callsign + (sel.state === 'holding' ? ' enter a teaching hold.' : ' resume inbound.');
          } else if (key === 'd' || key === 'u') {
            if (immediate.unchanged) { showCommandFeedback('unchanged', sel.callsign + ' altitude instruction is already ' + Math.round(immediate.targetAltitude).toLocaleString() + ' feet. Guidance is retained.', sel); return; }
            if (sel.state === 'approach') { sel.state = 'inbound'; sel.approachStage = null; sel.touchdownReady = false; }
            sel.targetAltitude = immediate.targetAltitude;
            message = sel.callsign + ' maintain ' + Math.round(sel.targetAltitude) + ' feet.';
          }
          if (message) {
            if (immediate && immediate.cancelsApproach) message += ' Automatic approach guidance ended.';
            if (immediate && immediate.releasesHold) message += ' Teaching hold released; approach clearance is still required.';
            recordAtcDecision(game, key === 'g' ? 'goAround' : 'command', sel, message); game.messages.unshift({ text: message, time: game.time, color: '#99f6e4' }); showCommandFeedback('sent', message, sel);
          }
          refreshTower();
        };
        commandRef.current = issueCommand;
        var onKey = function(e) {
          if (e.target !== canvas || e.altKey || e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.repeat) return;
          var key = e.key.toLowerCase();
          if (key === 'tab') return;
          var game = gameRef.current;
          if (key === 'escape') {
            e.preventDefault();
            if (game.showHelp) { game.showHelp = false; refreshTower(); }
            else finishShift();
            return;
          }
          if (key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); togglePause(); return; }
          if (key === '?' || key === '/') { e.preventDefault(); issueCommand('help'); return; }
          if (key === 'arrowright' || key === 'arrowleft') { e.preventDefault(); cycleSelection(key === 'arrowright' ? 1 : -1); return; }
          if (key === 'v') { e.preventDefault(); upd('highContrast', !latestDataRef.current.highContrast); return; }
          if (key === 't') { e.preventDefault(); tutorialRef.current.dismissed = !tutorialRef.current.dismissed; refreshTower(); return; }
          if (key === 'i') {
            e.preventDefault();
            announce(game.airport.name + '. ' + game.aircraft.filter(function(a) { return a.state !== 'landed' && a.state !== 'departed'; }).length + ' aircraft. ' + game.landed + ' landed. Score ' + game.score + '.');
            return;
          }
          if ('hrcgp dus'.replace(/ /g, '').indexOf(key) !== -1 && key.length === 1) { e.preventDefault(); issueCommand(key); }
        };

        canvas.addEventListener('click', onClick);
        canvas.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
        runningRef.current = true;
        animRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(loop);
        return function() {
          runningRef.current = false;
          cancelAnimationFrame(animRef.current);
          canvas.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
          canvas.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
          commandRef.current = null;
          // Close the AudioContext on view-change/unmount — otherwise each game session leaks one
          // and browsers cap concurrent contexts (~6), after which ATC audio silently dies.
          try { var _a = atcAudioRef.current; if (_a && _a.ctx && _a.ctx.state !== 'closed') { _a.ctx.close(); _a.ctx = null; } } catch (e) {}
        };
      }, [view]);

      // ═══ MENU VIEW ═══
      if (view === 'menu') {
        var savedShift = restoreAtcShift(d.activeShift);
        var recentShifts = atcShiftHistory(d.shiftHistory);
        var atcMenuPanel = d.view === 'lesson' && !hasSelectedLesson ? 'lessons' : d.atcMenuPanel || 'airports';
        var earnedCount = Object.keys(earnedATCBadges).length;
        var selectedAirportId = d.atcSelectedAirport || 'simple';
        var selectedAirport = AIRPORTS.find(function(a) { return a.id === selectedAirportId; }) || AIRPORTS[0];
        var quickPracticeId = pathway.recommendedId || 'first_landing';
        var quickPractice = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === quickPracticeId; });
        var difficultyTone = function(level) {
          if (level === 'Beginner') return { bg: '#06351f', border: '#22c55e', text: '#bbf7d0', soft: '#86efac' };
          if (level === 'Intermediate') return { bg: '#3a2f05', border: '#eab308', text: '#fef08a', soft: '#fde68a' };
          if (level === 'Advanced') return { bg: '#3b1f0a', border: '#f97316', text: '#fed7aa', soft: '#fdba74' };
          return { bg: '#3f1111', border: '#ef4444', text: '#fecaca', soft: '#fca5a5' };
        };
        var selectedTone = difficultyTone(selectedAirport.difficulty);
        var opsDefaults = { wind: 10, spawn: 5, sep: 8, descent: 1.0, timeout: 30, hypothesis: '', stuckRevealed: false, understood: false, explanation: '', log: [] };
        var iq = Object.assign({}, opsDefaults, d.opsControl || {});
        function setIQ(patch) { upd('opsControl', Object.assign({}, iq, patch)); }
        function setKey(k, v) { var p = {}; p[k] = v; setIQ(p); }
        var loadIdx = (iq.wind / 30) * 0.25 + (1 - (iq.spawn - 1) / 9) * 0.30 + (1 - (iq.sep - 5) / 15) * 0.25 + ((iq.descent - 0.5) / 1.5) * 0.10 + (1 - (iq.timeout - 10) / 50) * 0.10;
        var loadState = loadIdx < 0.35 ? 'sandbox' : loadIdx < 0.55 ? 'training' : loadIdx < 0.75 ? 'operational' : loadIdx < 0.9 ? 'stressed' : 'overload';
        var loadMeta = ({
          sandbox: { label: t('stem.atctower.sandbox', 'Sandbox'), color: '#86efac', bg: '#06351f', border: '#22c55e', desc: t('stem.atctower.light_traffic_generous_spacing_good_fo', 'Light traffic, generous spacing - good for new controllers.') },
          training: { label: t('stem.atctower.training', 'Training'), color: '#67e8f9', bg: '#083344', border: '#06b6d4', desc: t('stem.atctower.manageable_load_focus_on_technique_wit', 'Manageable load - focus on technique without time pressure.') },
          operational: { label: t('stem.atctower.operational', 'Operational'), color: '#fef08a', bg: '#3a2f05', border: '#eab308', desc: t('stem.atctower.realistic_airport_sequencing_required_', 'Realistic airport - sequencing required, conflicts likely.') },
          stressed: { label: t('stem.atctower.stressed', 'Stressed'), color: '#fdba74', bg: '#3b1f0a', border: '#f97316', desc: t('stem.atctower.high_load_expect_missed_approaches_fue', 'High load - expect missed approaches and fuel pressure.') },
          overload: { label: t('stem.atctower.overload', 'Overload'), color: '#fecaca', bg: '#3f1111', border: '#ef4444', desc: t('stem.atctower.saturated_staffing_runway_capacity_exc', 'Saturated - staffing and runway capacity exceeded.') }
        })[loadState];
        var opsSliders = [
          { k: 'wind', label: t('stem.atctower.wind_speed', 'Wind speed'), min: 0, max: 30, step: 1, unit: 'kt' },
          { k: 'spawn', label: t('stem.atctower.arrival_interval', 'Aircraft arrival interval'), min: 1, max: 10, step: 1, unit: 's' },
          { k: 'sep', label: t('stem.atctower.min_separation', 'Min separation'), min: 5, max: 20, step: 1, unit: 'nm' },
          { k: 'descent', label: t('stem.atctower.descent_multiplier', 'Descent multiplier'), min: 0.5, max: 2.0, step: 0.1, unit: 'x' },
          { k: 'timeout', label: t('stem.atctower.decision_window', 'Decision window'), min: 10, max: 60, step: 5, unit: 's' }
        ];
        var menuTabs = [
          { id: 'airports', label: t('stem.atctower.airports', 'Airports'), hint: t('stem.atctower.choose_runway_load', 'Choose runway load') },
          { id: 'practice', label: t('stem.atctower.practice_exercises', 'Practice'), hint: t('stem.atctower.repeatable_objectives', 'Repeatable exercises') },
          { id: 'ops', label: t('stem.atctower.ops_lab', 'Ops Lab'), hint: t('stem.atctower.tune_variables', 'Tune variables') },
          { id: 'lessons', label: t('stem.atctower.lessons', 'Lessons'), hint: t('stem.atctower.learn_the_math', 'Learn the math') },
          { id: 'badges', label: t('stem.atctower.badges', 'Badges'), hint: earnedCount + '/' + ATC_BADGES.length }
        ];
        var ATC_TAB_ORDER = menuTabs.map(function(tab) { return tab.id; });
        var atcTabKeyDown = function(e, index) {
          var nextIndex = -1;
          if (e.key === 'ArrowRight' || e.key === 'ArrowDown') nextIndex = (index + 1) % ATC_TAB_ORDER.length;
          else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft' || e.key === 'ArrowUp') nextIndex = (index + ATC_TAB_ORDER.length - 1) % ATC_TAB_ORDER.length;
          else if (e.key === 'Home') nextIndex = 0;
          else if (e.key === 'End') nextIndex = ATC_TAB_ORDER.length - 1;
          if (nextIndex < 0) return;
          e.preventDefault();
          var tabs = e.currentTarget && e.currentTarget.parentNode
            ? e.currentTarget.parentNode.querySelectorAll('[role="tab"]')
            : [];
          var nextTab = tabs[nextIndex];
          if (nextTab) {
            nextTab.focus();
            nextTab.click();
          }
        };
        return h('section', { className: 'atc-menu',
          ref: menuViewRef,
          'data-atctower-command': 'true',
          'aria-labelledby': 'atctower-command-title',
          style: { minHeight: '500px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #03170f 0%, #082f2a 48%, #111827 100%)', borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden', color: '#f8fafc', border: '1px solid rgba(45, 212, 191, 0.26)' }
        },
h('style', null, towerCss),
atcMenuPanel !== 'airports' && h('header', { className: 'atc-menu-heading' }, h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, t('stem.atctower.atc_tower', 'ATC Tower')), h('h2', { id: 'atctower-command-title' }, atcMenuPanel === 'practice' ? t('stem.atctower.practice_pathway', 'Practice pathway') : t('stem.atctower.tower_routes', 'Tower routes'))),
d.activeShift ? h('section', { 'data-atctower-recovery': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-recovery-title', style: { margin: '20px 24px 0', padding: '16px', border: '1px solid #fcd34d', borderRadius: '12px', background: '#302b12', color: '#fef3c7' } },
            h('h3', { id: 'atc-recovery-title', style: { margin: '0 0 8px', fontSize: '17px' } }, savedShift ? t('stem.atctower.saved_shift_ready', 'Your unfinished shift is saved') : t('stem.atctower.saved_shift_unavailable', 'This saved shift cannot be restored')),
            h('p', { style: { margin: '0 0 12px', lineHeight: 1.5 } }, savedShift
              ? savedShift.airport.name + ' · ' + Math.floor(savedShift.time / 60) + ':' + String(Math.floor(savedShift.time % 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ' · ' + savedShift.landed + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.landed', 'landed') + '. ' + t('stem.atctower.saved_shift_copy', 'Resume with the radar paused, or discard this shift to start again. Career totals update when you end a shift.')
              : t('stem.atctower.invalid_saved_shift_copy', 'The saved format is incomplete or unsupported. Discard it to start a new shift; recorded career totals are preserved.')),
            h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '10px' } },
              savedShift ? h('button', { 'data-atctower-resume': 'true', onClick: resumeShift, style: { minHeight: '44px', padding: '10px 16px', background: '#99f6e4', color: '#07332c', border: '1px solid #2dd4bf', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: 800, cursor: 'pointer' } }, t('stem.atctower.resume_saved_shift', 'Resume saved shift')) : null,
              h('button', { 'data-atctower-discard': 'true', onClick: discardShift, style: { minHeight: '44px', padding: '10px 16px', background: '#123931', color: '#fef3c7', border: '1px solid #fcd34d', borderRadius: '8px', fontWeight: 700, cursor: 'pointer' } }, t('stem.atctower.discard_saved_shift', 'Discard saved shift')))) : null,
recentShifts.length ? h('details', { 'data-atctower-history': 'true', style: { margin: '20px 24px 0', padding: '14px', border: '1px solid #315d58', borderRadius: '12px', background: '#0b2421' } },
            h('summary', { style: { minHeight: '44px', display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', color: '#ccfbf1', cursor: 'pointer', fontWeight: 800 } }, t('stem.atctower.recent_shifts', 'Recent shift debriefs') + ' (' + recentShifts.length + ')'),
            h('ol', { style: { paddingLeft: '22px', margin: '4px 0 0', display: 'grid', gap: '10px' } }, recentShifts.map(function(item) {
              var exercise = item.practice && item.exerciseResult && ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(scenario) { return scenario.id === item.practice.id && scenario.id === item.exerciseResult.id && scenario.airportId === item.airportId; });
              var note = d.shiftReflections && d.shiftReflections[item.shiftId];
              return h('li', { key: item.shiftId }, h('button', { onClick: function() { updMulti({ lastShift: item, view: 'debrief' }); }, style: { minHeight: '44px', width: '100%', padding: '10px 12px', background: '#123931', color: '#e2e8f0', border: '1px solid #52857c', borderRadius: '8px', cursor: 'pointer', textAlign: 'left', lineHeight: 1.5 } },
                exercise && h('strong', { style: { display: 'block', color: '#ccfbf1' } }, exercise.title + ' · ' + practiceOutcomeText(item.exerciseResult)),
                item.airportName + ' · ' + (item.trainingMode === 'guided' ? t('stem.atctower.guided_practice', 'Guided practice') : t('stem.atctower.standard_shift', 'Standard shift')),
                h('span', { style: { display: 'block', fontSize: '13px', color: '#cbd5e1' } }, item.landed + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.landed', 'landed') + ' · ' + item.score + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.points', 'points') + ' · ' + item.conflictEpisodes + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.conflict_events', 'conflict events')),
                typeof note === 'string' && note.trim() && h('span', { style: { display: 'block', fontSize: '12px', color: '#99f6e4' } }, t('stem.atctower.attempt_note_saved', 'Reflection note saved'))));
            }))) : null,
atcMenuPanel === 'airports' && h('div', { className: 'atc-menu-hero' },
            h('div', { className: 'atc-hero-copy' },
              h('div', { style: { color: '#99f6e4', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 800, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '1px' } }, t('stem.atctower.tower_briefing', 'Tower Briefing')),
              h('h2', { id: 'atctower-command-title' }, t('stem.atctower.command_center_title', 'ATC Tower Command Center')),

            atcMenuPanel === 'airports' && h('div', { style: { padding: '0 24px 18px' } },
            h('div', { className: 'atc-airport-choices' },
              AIRPORTS.map(function(apt) {
                var active = apt.id === selectedAirport.id;
                return h('button', { key: apt.id, className: 'atc-airport-card', 'data-atctower-airport-choice': apt.id, 'aria-pressed': active ? 'true' : 'false', onClick: function() { upd('atcSelectedAirport', apt.id); } },
                  h('div', { className: 'atc-airport-card-top' }, h('strong', { className: 'atc-airport-code' }, apt.code), h('span', { className: 'atc-airport-difficulty' }, apt.difficulty)),

          ),
          atcMenuPanel === 'ops' && (function() {
            var opsLog = Array.isArray(iq.log) ? iq.log : [];
            function opsSetupSignature(entry) {
              var descentValue = Number(entry && entry.descent);
              return [
                entry && entry.wind,
                entry && entry.spawn,
                entry && entry.sep,
                isFinite(descentValue) ? descentValue.toFixed(1) : 'na',
                entry && entry.timeout
              ].join('|');
            }
            var controlLabels = {
              wind: t('stem.atctower.wind_speed', 'Wind speed'),
              spawn: t('stem.atctower.arrival_interval', 'Aircraft arrival interval'),
              sep: t('stem.atctower.min_separation', 'Minimum separation'),
              descent: t('stem.atctower.descent_multiplier', 'Descent multiplier'),
              timeout: t('stem.atctower.decision_window', 'Decision window')
            };
            var controlHints = {
              wind: t('stem.atctower.wind_model_hint', 'Higher wind adds to the modeled workload.'),
              spawn: t('stem.atctower.arrival_interval_model_hint', 'Fewer seconds means more frequent arrivals and a larger modeled-load contribution.'),
              sep: t('stem.atctower.separation_model_hint', 'Less separation adds to this simplified conflict-pressure term.'),
              descent: t('stem.atctower.descent_model_hint', 'A larger multiplier adds to the modeled workload.'),
              timeout: t('stem.atctower.decision_window_model_hint', 'Fewer seconds means more decision pressure.')
            };
            var liveLoadDescription = ({
              sandbox: t('stem.atctower.sandbox_model_desc', 'The teaching index classifies this setup as low modeled workload.'),
              training: t('stem.atctower.training_model_desc', 'The teaching index classifies this setup as moderate-low modeled workload.'),
              operational: t('stem.atctower.operational_model_desc', 'The teaching index classifies this setup as mid-range modeled workload.'),
              stressed: t('stem.atctower.stressed_model_desc', 'The teaching index classifies this setup as high modeled workload.'),
              overload: t('stem.atctower.overload_model_desc', 'The teaching index classifies this setup as very high modeled workload.')
            })[loadState];
            var loggedSignatures = {};
            opsLog.forEach(function(entry) {
              loggedSignatures[opsSetupSignature(entry)] = true;
            });
            var currentSignature = opsSetupSignature(iq);
            var currentAlreadyLogged = !!loggedSignatures[currentSignature];
            var distinctLogCount = Object.keys(loggedSignatures).length;
            var comparisonEntries = opsLog.slice(-2);
            var changedControls = [];
            if (comparisonEntries.length === 2) {
              Object.keys(controlLabels).forEach(function(key) {
                if (String(comparisonEntries[0][key]) !== String(comparisonEntries[1][key])) changedControls.push(key);
              });
            }
            var fairTestState = comparisonEntries.length < 2
              ? 'needs-two-setups'
              : changedControls.length === 1
                ? 'one-variable'
                : changedControls.length === 0
                  ? 'duplicate'
                  : 'multiple-variables';
            var fairTestText = comparisonEntries.length < 2
              ? t('stem.atctower.fair_test_needs_two', 'Fair-test check: log two different setups to compare them.')
              : changedControls.length === 1
                ? t('stem.atctower.fair_test_one_variable', 'Fair-test check: one control changed') + ' (' + controlLabels[changedControls[0]] + ').'
                : changedControls.length === 0
                  ? t('stem.atctower.fair_test_duplicate', 'Fair-test check: these two logs use the same settings; change one control for the next log.')
                  : t('stem.atctower.fair_test_multiple', 'Fair-test check: several controls changed. That comparison cannot isolate one control; try changing only one next.');
            var explanationText = String(iq.explanation || iq.hypothesis || '');
            var evidenceReady = distinctLogCount >= 2;
            var explanationComplete = evidenceReady && !!explanationText.trim();

            return h('div', { style: { padding: '0 24px 18px' } },
              h('div', {
                style: { padding: '16px', borderRadius: '14px', background: loadMeta.bg, border: '1px solid ' + loadMeta.border, color: '#f8fafc' },
                'data-atctower-live-inquiry': 'observe-log-explain',
                'data-atctower-inquiry-credit': 'two-distinct-logs-plus-explanation',
                'aria-labelledby': 'atctower-ops-title'
              },
                h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '10px', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' } },
                  h('div', null,
                    h('div', { 'data-atctower-inquiry-label': 'live-evidence', style: { color: '#bae6fd', fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 900, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '0.8px', marginBottom: '3px' } }, t('stem.atctower.inquiry_widget_live_evidence', 'Inquiry widget · live evidence')),
                    h('h3', { id: 'atctower-ops-title', style: { margin: 0, color: loadMeta.color, fontSize: '17px', fontWeight: 900 } }, t('stem.atctower.ops_live_model_title', 'Ops Control — Live Load Model')),
                    h('p', { id: 'atctower-ops-instructions', style: { margin: '4px 0 0', color: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: '12px', lineHeight: 1.45, maxWidth: '72ch' } },
                      t('stem.atctower.ops_live_model_instructions', 'The result is visible and updates live, so this is an observe–log–explain investigation, not a prediction quiz. Change one control at a time, log at least two different setups, then explain the evidence.')
                    )
                  ),
                  h('span', {
                    role: 'status',
                    'aria-live': 'polite',
                    'aria-atomic': 'true',
                    'data-atctower-live-load': 'visible-model-output',
                    style: { borderRadius: '16px', padding: '6px 10px', background: loadMeta.color, color: '#0f172a', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 900 }
                  }, t('stem.atctower.live_modeled_load', 'Live modeled load') + ': ' + loadMeta.label)
                ),
                h('p', { style: { margin: '8px 0 0', color: '#e2e8f0', fontSize: '12px', lineHeight: 1.45 } }, liveLoadDescription),
                h('div', {
                  'data-atctower-model-boundary': 'simplified-weighted-index',
                  style: { marginTop: '10px', padding: '9px 10px', borderRadius: '9px', background: 'rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.48)', border: '1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.28)', color: '#dbeafe', fontSize: '11px', lineHeight: 1.45 }
                },
                  h('strong', null, t('stem.atctower.model_boundary_label', 'Model boundary: ')),
                  t('stem.atctower.model_boundary_copy', 'This standalone teaching index combines arrival frequency (30%), wind (25%), reduced-separation pressure (25%), descent rate (10%), and decision-window pressure (10%). It does not configure the tower shift or predict real-airport safety.')
                ),
                h('div', { style: { display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(auto-fit, minmax(180px, 1fr))', gap: '12px', marginTop: '14px' } },
                  opsSliders.map(function(s) {
                    return h('label', { key: s.k, style: { display: 'block', color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 800 } },
                      h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: '8px', marginBottom: '5px' } },
                        h('span', null, s.label),
                        h('span', { style: { color: loadMeta.color, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontWeight: 900 } }, (s.step < 1 ? iq[s.k].toFixed(1) : iq[s.k]) + ' ' + s.unit)
                      ),
                      h('input', { type: 'range', 'aria-label': s.label, 'aria-describedby': 'atctower-' + s.k + '-hint', min: s.min, max: s.max, step: s.step, value: iq[s.k], onChange: function(e) { setKey(s.k, parseFloat(e.target.value)); }, style: { width: '100%' } }),
                      h('span', { id: 'atctower-' + s.k + '-hint', style: { display: 'block', marginTop: '3px', color: '#cbd5e1', fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 500, lineHeight: 1.35 } }, controlHints[s.k])
                    );

                h('div', {
                  'data-atctower-evidence-log': 'distinct-setups',

                },
                  h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '8px', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' } },
                    h('div', null,
                      h('div', { style: { color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 900 } }, t('stem.atctower.evidence_log_title', 'Evidence log') + ' · ' + distinctLogCount + '/2 ' + t('stem.atctower.distinct_setups', 'distinct setups')),
                      h('div', { style: { marginTop: '2px', color: '#cbd5e1', fontSize: '10px', lineHeight: 1.35 } }, t('stem.atctower.evidence_log_hint', 'For the clearest comparison, keep four controls fixed and change one.'))
                    ),
                    h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: '6px' } },
                      h('button', {
                        type: 'button',
                        disabled: currentAlreadyLogged,
                        'aria-disabled': currentAlreadyLogged ? 'true' : 'false',
                        'aria-label': currentAlreadyLogged ? t('stem.atctower.current_setup_logged_a11y', 'Current ATC setup already logged') : t('stem.atctower.log_setup_a11y', 'Log current ATC setup as evidence'),
                        onClick: function() {
                          if (currentAlreadyLogged) return;
                          var stamp = new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19);
                          setIQ({ log: opsLog.concat([{ t: stamp, wind: iq.wind, spawn: iq.spawn, sep: iq.sep, descent: iq.descent, timeout: iq.timeout, state: loadMeta.label, loadIndex: Number(loadIdx.toFixed(3)) }]) });

'not-allowed' : 'pointer', opacity: currentAlreadyLogged ? 0.68 : 1 }

                      return h('div', { key: opsSetupSignature(entry) + '-' + index, role: 'listitem', 'aria-label': entryText }, entryText);
                    })
                  ),
                  h('p', { role: 'status', 'aria-live': 'polite', 'data-atctower-fair-test-check': fairTestState, style: { margin: '8px 0 0', color: changedControls.length === 1 ? '#bbf7d0' : '#fde68a', fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 750, lineHeight: 1.4 } }, fairTestText)
                ),
                h('div', { style: { marginTop: '10px' } },
                  h('button', {
                    type: 'button',
                    'aria-expanded': iq.stuckRevealed ? 'true' : 'false',
                    'aria-controls': 'atctower-ops-investigation-prompts',
                    onClick: function() { setIQ({ stuckRevealed: !iq.stuckRevealed }); },
                    style: { padding: '7px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid ' + loadMeta.border, background: 'rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.5)', color: loadMeta.color, fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 850, cursor: 'pointer' }
                  }, iq.stuckRevealed
                    ? t('stem.atctower.hide_investigation_prompts', 'Hide investigation prompts')
                    : t('stem.atctower.show_investigation_prompts', "I'm stuck — show investigation prompts")
                  ),
                  iq.stuckRevealed && h('div', {
                    id: 'atctower-ops-investigation-prompts',
                    role: 'note',
                    'data-atctower-investigation-prompts': 'scaffold-only',
                    style: { marginTop: '7px', padding: '9px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', background: 'rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.5)', border: '1px dashed ' + loadMeta.border, color: '#dbeafe', fontSize: '10px', lineHeight: 1.45 }
                  },

                      h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.prompt_compare_logs', 'What stayed fixed between your last two logs, and what changed?')),

                h('label', { style: { display: 'block', marginTop: '12px', color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: 850 } },
                  h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_prompt', 'Evidence-based explanation from your logged setups')),
                  h('textarea', {
                    value: explanationText,
                    disabled: !evidenceReady,
                    'aria-disabled': evidenceReady ? 'false' : 'true',
                    'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_input', 'Evidence-based ATC load explanation'),
                    'aria-describedby': 'atctower-ops-explanation-help',
                    onChange: function(e) { setIQ({ explanation: e.target.value, hypothesis: '' }); },
                    rows: 3,
                    placeholder: t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_example', 'What changed, what stayed fixed, and how did the live modeled load respond? Cite both logged setups.'),
                    style: { width: '100%', marginTop: '6px', padding: '8px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid ' + loadMeta.border, background: 'rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.66)', color: '#f8fafc', fontSize: '12px', resize: 'vertical', opacity: evidenceReady ? 1 : 0.66 }
                  })
                ),
                h('p', { id: 'atctower-ops-explanation-help', style: { margin: '5px 0 0', color: '#cbd5e1', fontSize: '10px', lineHeight: 1.4 } },
                  evidenceReady
                    ? t('stem.atctower.explanation_ready_hint', 'Use the logs to name the control you changed and the direction of the modeled response.')
                    : t('stem.atctower.explanation_locked_hint', 'Log two distinct setups before writing the explanation.')
                ),
                h('p', {
                  role: 'status',
                  'aria-live': 'polite',
                  'data-atctower-inquiry-progress': explanationComplete ? 'complete' : evidenceReady ? 'explain' : 'collect-evidence',
                  style: { margin: '9px 0 0', color: explanationComplete ? '#bbf7d0' : '#dbeafe', fontSize: '11px', fontWeight: 800, lineHeight: 1.4 }
                }, explanationComplete
                  ? t('stem.atctower.inquiry_complete', 'Evidence explanation recorded. Investigation complete; no answer is scored for agreement.')
                  : evidenceReady
                    ? t('stem.atctower.inquiry_explain_next', 'Evidence collected. Explain the comparison to complete the investigation.')
                    : t('stem.atctower.inquiry_collect_next', 'Collect two distinct setups. Completion depends on logging and explaining, not matching a hidden answer.')
                )
              )
            );
          })(),
          atcMenuPanel === 'lessons' && h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-index' },
            h('h3', null, t('stem.atctower.explore_a_concept', 'Explore a concept')),

              Object.keys(ATC_LESSONS).map(function(key) {

                  atcVisualSymbol(lessonTopics[key].symbol, 'atc-skill-symbol'),

                h('div', { style: { fontSize: '11px', color: '#cbd5e1', marginTop: '4px', fontWeight: 700 } }, item[1])
              );

          ));
        return h('div', { style: { minHeight: '500px', background: 'linear-gradient(135deg, #021a0a 0%, #0a2e1a 50%, #0d3320 100%)', borderRadius: '16px', overflow: 'hidden' } },
          h('div', { style: { textAlign: 'center', padding: '28px 24px 14px' } },
            h('div', { style: { fontSize: '48px', marginBottom: '8px' } }, '🗼'),
