                );
              })
            )
          ),
          // ═══ OPS CONTROL inquiry widget (H7b'') ═══
          (function() {
            var iq = d.opsControl || { wind: 10, spawn: 5, sep: 8, descent: 1.0, timeout: 30, hypothesis: '', stuckRevealed: false, understood: false, explanation: '', log: [] };
            function setIQ(patch) { upd('opsControl', Object.assign({}, iq, patch)); }
            function setKey(k, v) { var p = {}; p[k] = v; setIQ(p); }
            var loadIdx = (iq.wind / 30) * 0.25 + (1 - (iq.spawn - 1) / 9) * 0.30 + (1 - (iq.sep - 5) / 15) * 0.25 + ((iq.descent - 0.5) / 1.5) * 0.10 + (1 - (iq.timeout - 10) / 50) * 0.10;
            var state = loadIdx < 0.35 ? 'sandbox' : loadIdx < 0.55 ? 'training' : loadIdx < 0.75 ? 'operational' : loadIdx < 0.9 ? 'stressed' : 'overload';
            var sm = ({
              sandbox: { label: t('stem.atctower.sandbox', 'Sandbox'), color: '#4ade80', bg: '#0a2e1a', border: '#16a34a', desc: t('stem.atctower.light_traffic_generous_spacing_good_fo', 'Light traffic, generous spacing — good for new controllers.') },
              training: { label: t('stem.atctower.training', 'Training'), color: '#22d3ee', bg: '#0c2a3a', border: '#0891b2', desc: t('stem.atctower.manageable_load_focus_on_technique_wit', 'Manageable load — focus on technique without time pressure.') },
              operational: { label: t('stem.atctower.operational', 'Operational'), color: '#facc15', bg: '#2a2410', border: '#eab308', desc: t('stem.atctower.realistic_airport_sequencing_required_', 'Realistic airport — sequencing required, conflicts likely.') },
              stressed: { label: t('stem.atctower.stressed', 'Stressed'), color: '#fb923c', bg: '#2a1a0a', border: '#ea580c', desc: t('stem.atctower.high_load_expect_missed_approaches_fue', 'High load — expect missed approaches, fuel pressure.') },
              overload: { label: t('stem.atctower.overload', 'Overload'), color: '#f87171', bg: '#2a0a0a', border: '#dc2626', desc: t('stem.atctower.saturated_staffing_runway_capacity_exc', 'Saturated — staffing/runway capacity exceeded.') }
            })[state];
            var rings = [
              { label: t('stem.atctower.wind', 'Wind'), val: iq.wind / 30, num: iq.wind + 'kt' },
              { label: t('stem.atctower.spawn', 'Spawn'), val: 1 - (iq.spawn - 1) / 9, num: iq.spawn + 's' },
              { label: 'Sep', val: 1 - (iq.sep - 5) / 15, num: iq.sep + 'nm' },
              { label: t('stem.atctower.desc', 'Desc'), val: (iq.descent - 0.5) / 1.5, num: iq.descent.toFixed(1) + 'x' },
              { label: t('stem.atctower.timeout', 'Timeout'), val: 1 - (iq.timeout - 10) / 50, num: iq.timeout + 's' }
            ];
            var sliders = [
              { k: 'wind', label: t('stem.atctower.wind_speed', 'Wind speed'), min: 0, max: 30, step: 1, unit: 'kt' },
              { k: 'spawn', label: t('stem.atctower.arrival_interval', 'Aircraft arrival interval'), min: 1, max: 10, step: 1, unit: 's' },
              { k: 'sep', label: t('stem.atctower.min_separation', 'Min separation'), min: 5, max: 20, step: 1, unit: 'nm' },
              { k: 'descent', label: t('stem.atctower.descent_multiplier', 'Descent multiplier'), min: 0.5, max: 2.0, step: 0.1, unit: 'x' },
              { k: 'timeout', label: t('stem.atctower.decision_window', 'Decision window'), min: 10, max: 60, step: 5, unit: 's' }
            ];
            return h('div', { style: { padding: '14px', margin: '0 24px 12px', borderRadius: '12px', background: sm.bg, border: '1px solid ' + sm.border, color: '#e8f0f5' } },
              h('h4', { style: { margin: '0 0 4px', fontSize: '13px', fontWeight: 800, color: sm.color, textTransform: 'uppercase', letterSpacing: '1px' } }, t('stem.atctower.ops_control_inquiry_widget', '🎚️ Ops Control — Inquiry Widget')),
              h('p', { style: { margin: '0 0 10px', fontSize: '11px', opacity: 0.85, lineHeight: 1.4 } }, t('stem.atctower.live_model_observe_log_explain', 'This standalone load index updates live. Log at least two different setups, compare what changed, and explain a pattern from the visible evidence.')),
              h('div', { style: { display: 'inline-block', padding: '4px 10px', borderRadius: '16px', background: sm.color, color: '#000', fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 800, marginBottom: '6px' } }, 'Live modeled load: ' + sm.label),
              h('p', { style: { margin: '0 0 10px', fontSize: '10px', opacity: 0.8 } }, sm.desc),
              h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-around', flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: '10px' } },
                rings.map(function(ring, i) {
                  var pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ring.val));
                  var dash = (pct * 138).toFixed(1);
                  return h('div', { key: i, style: { textAlign: 'center' } },
                    h('svg', { width: 56, height: 56, viewBox: '0 0 56 56', role: 'img', 'aria-label': ring.label + ': ' + ring.num },
                      h('circle', { cx: 28, cy: 28, r: 22, fill: 'none', stroke: '#1a3a2a', strokeWidth: 5 }),
                      h('circle', { cx: 28, cy: 28, r: 22, fill: 'none', stroke: sm.color, strokeWidth: 5, strokeDasharray: dash + ' 138', strokeLinecap: 'round', transform: 'rotate(-90 28 28)' }),
                      h('text', { x: 28, y: 32, textAnchor: 'middle', fill: sm.color, fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 800 }, ring.num)
                    ),
                    h('div', { style: { fontSize: 9, fontWeight: 700, opacity: 0.85, marginTop: 2 } }, ring.label)
                  );
                })
              ),
              h('div', { style: { display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 1fr', gap: '8px 12px', marginBottom: '10px' } },
                sliders.map(function(s) {
                  return h('label', { key: s.k, style: { display: 'block', fontSize: 10 } },
                    h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 2 } },
                      h('span', null, s.label),
                      h('span', { style: { fontFamily: 'monospace', color: sm.color, fontWeight: 700 } }, (s.step < 1 ? iq[s.k].toFixed(1) : iq[s.k]) + ' ' + s.unit)
                    ),
                    h('input', { type: 'range', 'aria-label': s.label, min: s.min, max: s.max, step: s.step, value: iq[s.k], onChange: function(e) { setKey(s.k, parseFloat(e.target.value)); }, style: { width: '100%' } })
                  );
                })
              ),
              h('div', { style: { display: 'flex', gap: 8, marginBottom: 10 } },
                h('button', { onClick: function() {
                  var t = new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19);
                  setIQ({ log: iq.log.concat([{ t: t, wind: iq.wind, spawn: iq.spawn, sep: iq.sep, descent: iq.descent, timeout: iq.timeout, state: sm.label }]) });
                }, style: { flex: 1, padding: '6px', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid ' + sm.border, background: sm.bg, color: sm.color, cursor: 'pointer' } }, t('stem.atctower.log_current_setup', '📋 Log current setup')),
                h('button', { onClick: function() { setIQ({ wind: 10, spawn: 5, sep: 8, descent: 1.0, timeout: 30 }); }, style: { padding: '6px 10px', fontSize: 10, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid #1a3a2a', background: '#0a1a0a', color: '#94a3b8', cursor: 'pointer' } }, t('stem.atctower.reset', 'Reset'))
              ),
              iq.log.length > 0 && h('div', { style: { maxHeight: 80, overflow: 'auto', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6, background: '#0a1a0a', border: '1px solid #1a3a2a', marginBottom: 10, fontSize: 9, fontFamily: 'monospace', lineHeight: 1.4 } },
                iq.log.slice(-5).map(function(e, i) {
                  return h('div', { key: i }, e.t + '  ' + e.state + ' · w' + e.wind + ' sp' + e.spawn + ' s' + e.sep + ' d' + e.descent.toFixed(1) + ' t' + e.timeout);
                })
              ),
              h('label', { style: { display: 'block', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, opacity: 0.9, marginBottom: 4 } },
                t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_prompt', 'Evidence-based explanation from your logged setups'),
                h('textarea', {
                  value: iq.explanation || iq.hypothesis || '',
                  disabled: !(iq.log && iq.log.length >= 2),
                  'aria-disabled': iq.log && iq.log.length >= 2 ? 'false' : 'true',
                  onChange: function(e) { setIQ({ explanation: e.target.value, hypothesis: '' }); },
                  rows: 2,
                  'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_input', 'Evidence-based ATC load explanation'),
                  'aria-describedby': 'atctower-legacy-explanation-help',
                  placeholder: t('stem.atctower.evidence_explanation_example', 'What changed, what stayed fixed, and how did the live modeled load respond? Cite both logged setups.'),
                  style: { width: '100%', padding: 6, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid ' + sm.border, background: '#0a1a0a', color: '#e8f0f5', fontSize: 10, marginTop: 4, marginBottom: 6, resize: 'vertical' }
                })
              ),
              h('p', { id: 'atctower-legacy-explanation-help', style: { margin: '0 0 8px', fontSize: 9, opacity: 0.75, lineHeight: 1.4 } },
                iq.log && iq.log.length >= 2
                  ? t('stem.atctower.explanation_ready_hint', 'Use the logs to name the control you changed and the direction of the modeled response.')
                  : t('stem.atctower.explanation_locked_hint', 'Log two different setups before writing the explanation.')
              ),
              !iq.stuckRevealed && h('button', { type: 'button', onClick: function() { setIQ({ stuckRevealed: true }); }, style: { padding: '6px 10px', fontSize: 10, fontWeight: 700, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid #1a3a2a', background: '#0a1a0a', color: sm.color, cursor: 'pointer', marginBottom: 10 } }, t('stem.atctower.i_m_stuck_show_open_questions', "I'm stuck — show investigation prompts")),
              iq.stuckRevealed && h('div', { style: { padding: 8, borderRadius: 6, background: '#0a1a0a', border: '1px dashed ' + sm.border, fontSize: 10, marginBottom: 10, lineHeight: 1.5 } },
                h('div', { style: { fontWeight: 700, color: sm.color, marginBottom: 4 } }, t('stem.atctower.investigation_prompts', 'Investigation prompts')),
                h('ul', { style: { margin: 0, paddingLeft: 16 } },
                  h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.prompt_hold_four', 'Which four controls can you hold fixed for a fair comparison?')),
                  h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.prompt_same_band', 'Can two different setups fall in the same modeled load band?')),
                  h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.prompt_direction', 'When one control changes, which direction does the load band move?'))
                )
              ),
              h('p', { style: { margin: 0, fontSize: 9, fontStyle: 'italic', opacity: 0.72 } }, t('stem.atctower.live_model_inquiry_note', 'Live-model inquiry: the result stays visible, and completion comes from logging evidence and explaining it — not from matching an answer.'))
            );
          })(),
          // Controls legend
          h('div', { style: { padding: '12px 24px', borderTop: '1px solid #1a3a2a', display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center', gap: '12px', flexWrap: 'wrap' } },
            [['Click/Enter', 'Select'], ['H', 'Heading'], ['S', 'Speed'], ['R', 'Runway'], ['C', 'Clear approach'], ['G', 'Go Around'], ['P', 'Hold'], ['D/U', 'Alt'], ['T', 'Tutorial'], ['ESC', 'Exit']].map(function(item) {
              return h('div', { key: item[0], style: { textAlign: 'center' } },
                h('div', { style: { fontSize: '10px', fontWeight: 700, color: '#4ade80', background: '#0a2e1a', padding: '2px 8px', borderRadius: '4px', fontFamily: 'monospace' } }, item[0]),
                h('div', { style: { fontSize: '8px', color: 'var(--allo-stem-text-soft, #94a3b8)', marginTop: '2px' } }, item[1])
              );
            })
          )
        );
      }

      // ═══ LESSON VIEW ═══
      if (view === 'lesson' && hasSelectedLesson) {
        var les = ATC_LESSONS[d.selectedLesson];
        var aiKey = 'aiExplain_' + d.selectedLesson;
        var aiLevelKey = 'aiLevel_' + d.selectedLesson;
        var aiLoadingKey = 'aiLoading_' + d.selectedLesson;
        var aiErrorKey = 'aiError_' + d.selectedLesson;
        var aiTextLevelKey = 'aiExplainLevel_' + d.selectedLesson;
        var aiLevel = d[aiLevelKey] || 'grade5';
        var aiText = d[aiTextLevelKey] === aiLevel ? (d[aiKey] || '') : '';
        var aiLoading = !!d[aiLoadingKey] && !!lessonRequestRef.current.pending && lessonRequestRef.current.pending.context === lessonRequestContext;
        var aiError = d[aiTextLevelKey] === aiLevel ? (d[aiErrorKey] || '') : '';
        var LEVELS = [
          { id: 'plain', label: t('stem.atctower.plain', 'Plain'), hint: t('stem.atctower.using_simple_everyday_words_and_short_', 'using simple everyday words and short sentences') },
          { id: 'grade5', label: t('stem.atctower.grade_5', 'Grade 5'), hint: t('stem.atctower.for_a_5th_grade_student_brief_and_frie', 'for a 5th grade student, brief and friendly') },
          { id: 'hs', label: t('stem.atctower.high_school', 'High School'), hint: t('stem.atctower.for_a_high_school_student_studying_pre', 'for a high school student studying pre-calculus or physics') }
        ];
        var explain = function() {
          var requests = lessonRequestRef.current;
          requests.generation += 1;
          var thisReqId = requests.generation;
          var requestedContext = lessonRequestContext;
          var initial = {};
          initial[aiKey] = ''; initial[aiErrorKey] = ''; initial[aiTextLevelKey] = aiLevel;
          initial[aiLoadingKey] = typeof callGemini === 'function';
          if (typeof callGemini !== 'function') {
            requests.pending = null;
            initial[aiErrorKey] = 'AI tutor not available.';
            updMulti(initial);
            return;
          }
          requests.pending = { context: requestedContext, loadingKey: aiLoadingKey };
          updMulti(initial);
          var lv = LEVELS.find(function (L) { return L.id === aiLevel; }) || LEVELS[1];
          var prompt = 'Explain this air traffic control concept ' + lv.hint + '. '
            + 'Concept: ' + les.title + '. Lesson: ' + les.content + ' Key formula: ' + les.formula + '. '
            + 'In 3 short sentences: (1) What the concept means for an ATC controller. (2) A concrete everyday-life analogy. (3) One common mistake students make with this. '
            + 'No markdown, no bullets, no headings. Plain prose.';
          Promise.resolve().then(function() { return callGemini(prompt, false, false, 0.5); }).then(function (resp) {
            if (!requests.mounted || thisReqId !== requests.generation || requestedContext !== lessonContextRef.current) return;
            requests.pending = null;
            var result = {}; result[aiKey] = String(resp || '').trim(); result[aiLoadingKey] = false; result[aiTextLevelKey] = aiLevel;
            updMulti(result);
          }).catch(function () {
            if (!requests.mounted || thisReqId !== requests.generation || requestedContext !== lessonContextRef.current) return;
            requests.pending = null;
            var failed = {}; failed[aiLoadingKey] = false; failed[aiErrorKey] = 'Could not reach AI tutor. Try again in a moment.'; failed[aiTextLevelKey] = aiLevel;
            updMulti(failed);
          });
        };
        var lessonPracticeId = { separation: 'separation_recovery', sequencing: 'arrival_sequence', vectors: 'first_landing', communication: 'first_landing' }[d.selectedLesson] || null;
        var labKind = d.selectedLesson, lab = atcLessonExplorer(labKind, lessonExplorers[labKind]), labValues = lab.values;
        var setLab = function(patch) { setLessonExplorers(function(previous) { var next = Object.assign({}, previous); next[labKind] = Object.assign({}, previous[labKind] || {}, patch); return next; }); };
        var resetLab = function() { setLessonExplorers(function(previous) { var next = Object.assign({}, previous); next[labKind] = {}; return next; }); };
        var headingText = function(value) { return String(Math.round(normalizeHeading(value))).padStart(3, '0') + '°'; };
        var labRange = function(key, label, step, unit) {
          var limits = ATC_EXPLORER_FIELDS[labKind][key], value = labValues[key], id = 'atc-explorer-' + labKind + '-' + key;
          return h('label', { key: key, className: 'atc-explorer-range', htmlFor: id }, h('span', null, label, h('strong', { 'aria-hidden': true }, value.toLocaleString() + ' ' + unit)),
            h('input', { id: id, 'data-atctower-explorer-input': key, type: 'range', min: limits[0], max: limits[1], step: step, value: value, 'aria-valuetext': value.toLocaleString() + ' ' + unit, onChange: function(e) { var patch = {}; patch[key] = Number(e.target.value); setLab(patch); } }));
        };
        var labRail = function(value, maximum, limit) { return h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-rail', 'aria-hidden': true }, limit != null && h('span', { className: 'atc-explorer-limit', style: { left: limit / maximum * 100 + '%' } }), h('span', { className: 'atc-explorer-point', style: { left: value / maximum * 100 + '%' } })); };
        var labMetric = function(key, label, value, note) { return h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-metric', 'data-atctower-explorer-metric': key }, h('span', null, label), h('strong', null, value), note && h('small', null, note)); };
        var labSummary = function(text, review) { return h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-summary', 'data-atctower-explorer-summary': labKind, 'data-state': review ? 'review' : 'ready', role: 'status', 'aria-atomic': true }, text); };
        var labCompass = function(weather) {
          return h('svg', { className: 'atc-explorer-compass', viewBox: '0 0 240 240', 'aria-hidden': true, focusable: false },
            h('circle', { className: 'atc-lab-ring', cx: 120, cy: 120, r: 94 }), h('circle', { className: 'atc-lab-ring', cx: 120, cy: 120, r: 60 }),
            [['N',120,15],['E',228,124],['S',120,237],['W',12,124]].map(function(item) { return h('text', { key: item[0], x: item[1], y: item[2], textAnchor: 'middle' }, item[0]); }),
            weather ? h('g', { transform: 'translate(120 120) rotate(' + labValues.runway + ')' }, h('rect', { className: 'atc-lab-runway', x: -6, y: -80, width: 12, height: 160, rx: 3 }), h('line', { className: 'atc-lab-centerline', x1: 0, y1: -75, x2: 0, y2: 75 })) : h('g', { className: 'atc-lab-current', transform: 'translate(120 120) rotate(' + labValues.current + ')' }, h('line', { x1: 0, y1: 0, x2: 0, y2: -76 }), h('circle', { cx: 0, cy: -76, r: 4 })),
            (!weather || labValues.windSpeed > 0) && h('g', { className: 'atc-lab-target', 'data-atctower-explorer-vector': weather ? 'wind' : 'heading', transform: 'translate(120 120) rotate(' + (weather ? labValues.windFrom : labValues.target) + ')' },
              h('line', { x1: 0, y1: weather ? -96 : 0, x2: 0, y2: weather ? -30 : -78 }), h('path', { d: weather ? 'M-5-39 0-30 5-39' : 'M-5-69 0-78 5-69' })));
        };
        var labContent;
        if (labKind === 'separation') {
          var limitText = t('stem.atctower.model_limit', 'Model limit');
          labContent = h(React.Fragment, null,
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-display' },
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-metrics' },
                h('div', null, labMetric('horizontal', t('stem.atctower.horizontal_gap', 'Horizontal gap'), labValues.horizontal.toFixed(1) + ' nm', limitText + ': ' + lab.minimum + ' nm'), labRail(labValues.horizontal, 10, lab.minimum)),
                h('div', null, labMetric('vertical', t('stem.atctower.vertical_gap', 'Vertical gap'), labValues.vertical.toLocaleString() + ' ft', limitText + ': 1,000 ft'), labRail(labValues.vertical, 3000, 1000))),
              labSummary(lab.conflict ? t('stem.atctower.explorer_spacing_conflict', 'Model conflict: both gaps are below their limits.') : t('stem.atctower.explorer_spacing_met', 'Model spacing maintained: at least one gap meets its limit.'), lab.conflict)),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-controls' }, labRange('horizontal', t('stem.atctower.horizontal_gap', 'Horizontal gap'), .5, 'nm'), labRange('vertical', t('stem.atctower.vertical_gap', 'Vertical gap'), 500, 'ft')),
            h('fieldset', null, h('legend', null, t('stem.atctower.aircraft_pair', 'Aircraft pair')), h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-options' },
              h('button', { 'aria-pressed': !labValues.heavy, onClick: function() { setLab({ heavy: false }); } }, t('stem.atctower.light_medium_pair', 'Light / medium')),
              h('button', { 'aria-pressed': labValues.heavy, onClick: function() { setLab({ heavy: true }); } }, t('stem.atctower.heavy_super_pair', 'Includes heavy / super')))),
            h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-note' }, t('stem.atctower.explorer_spacing_try', 'Try a 4 nm gap, then change the aircraft pair. Keep the altitude gap fixed to see the effect of wake category. Dashed marks show the model limits.')));
        } else if (labKind === 'sequencing') {
          labContent = h(React.Fragment, null,
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-display' },
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-axis', 'aria-hidden': true }, h('span', null, '0'), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.arrival_axis', 'Flight minutes from now')), h('span', null, lab.horizon)),
              ['A','B'].map(function(letter) { var eta = letter === 'A' ? lab.etaA : lab.etaB; return h('div', { key: letter, className: 'atc-explorer-timeline-row', 'data-flight': letter },
                h('div', null, h('span', null, letter === 'A' ? t('stem.atctower.aircraft_a', 'Aircraft A') : t('stem.atctower.aircraft_b', 'Aircraft B')), h('strong', { 'data-atctower-explorer-eta': letter }, eta.toFixed(1) + ' min')),
                labRail(eta, lab.horizon), h('small', null, labValues['distance' + letter] + ' nm ÷ ' + labValues['speed' + letter] + ' kt × 60 = ' + eta.toFixed(1) + ' min')); }),
              labSummary((lab.first === 'tie' ? t('stem.atctower.explorer_arrival_tie', 'Both estimates are equal.') : (lab.first === 'A' ? t('stem.atctower.aircraft_a', 'Aircraft A') : t('stem.atctower.aircraft_b', 'Aircraft B')) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.arrives_first', 'arrives first.')) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.arrival_gap', 'Arrival gap') + ': ' + lab.gap.toFixed(1) + ' min.')),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-controls' }, labRange('distanceA', t('stem.atctower.aircraft_a_distance', 'A · distance'), 5, 'nm'), labRange('speedA', t('stem.atctower.aircraft_a_speed', 'A · groundspeed'), 10, 'kt'), labRange('distanceB', t('stem.atctower.aircraft_b_distance', 'B · distance'), 5, 'nm'), labRange('speedB', t('stem.atctower.aircraft_b_speed', 'B · groundspeed'), 10, 'kt')),
            h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-note' }, t('stem.atctower.explorer_arrival_try', 'Keep both distances fixed and change one speed. These estimates assume constant groundspeed along the remaining route.')));
        } else if (labKind === 'vectors') {
          labContent = h(React.Fragment, null,
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-display' }, labCompass(false),
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-legend' }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.current_heading_dashed', 'Dashed · current heading')), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.target_heading_solid', 'Solid · instructed heading'))),
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-metrics' }, labMetric('turn', t('stem.atctower.shortest_turn', 'Shortest turn'), Math.abs(lab.turn) + '°'), labMetric('turnTime', t('stem.atctower.time_at_model_rate', 'At 3° per second'), lab.turnSeconds.toFixed(1) + ' s')),
              labSummary(lab.turn === 0 ? t('stem.atctower.heading_already_met', 'Already on the instructed heading.') : Math.abs(lab.turn) === 180 ? t('stem.atctower.heading_equal_turn', 'Either direction is equally short for a 180° turn.') : lab.turn > 0 ? t('stem.atctower.heading_turn_right', 'Turn right (clockwise) for the shorter path.') : t('stem.atctower.heading_turn_left', 'Turn left (counterclockwise) for the shorter path.'))),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-controls' }, labRange('current', t('stem.atctower.current_heading', 'Current heading'), 10, '°'), labRange('target', t('stem.atctower.instructed_heading', 'Instructed heading'), 10, '°')),
            h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-note' }, t('stem.atctower.explorer_heading_try', 'Try 350° to 010° across north. The time uses this simulator’s manual turn rate in flight seconds; approach guidance uses a different rate.')));
        } else if (labKind === 'weather') {
          labContent = h(React.Fragment, null,
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-display' }, labCompass(true),
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-legend' }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.runway_heading', 'Runway heading') + ' ' + headingText(labValues.runway)), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.wind_from', 'Wind from') + ' ' + headingText(labValues.windFrom))),
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-metrics' }, labMetric('headwind', lab.headwind < 0 ? t('stem.atctower.tailwind_component', 'Tailwind component') : t('stem.atctower.headwind_component', 'Headwind component'), Math.abs(lab.headwind).toFixed(1) + ' kt'), labMetric('crosswind', t('stem.atctower.crosswind_component', 'Crosswind magnitude'), lab.crosswind.toFixed(1) + ' kt')),
              labSummary(labValues.windSpeed === 0 ? t('stem.atctower.explorer_calm', 'Calm: both wind components are zero.') : t('stem.atctower.wind_arrow_note', 'The arrow points from the wind source toward the runway.'))),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-controls' }, labRange('runway', t('stem.atctower.runway_heading', 'Runway heading'), 10, '°'), labRange('windFrom', t('stem.atctower.wind_from', 'Wind from'), 10, '°'), labRange('windSpeed', t('stem.atctower.wind_speed', 'Wind speed'), 1, 'kt')),
            h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-note' }, t('stem.atctower.explorer_wind_try', 'Rotate the wind through a quarter turn while keeping its speed fixed. Components describe the wind; this example does not set an aircraft operating limit.')));
        } else {
          var controllerLine = labValues.sample === 'heading' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_controller_heading', 'TRAIN101, turn right heading 090.') : labValues.sample === 'altitude' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_controller_altitude', 'TRAIN101, maintain 6,000 feet.') : t('stem.atctower.readback_controller_runway', 'TRAIN101, cleared approach runway 27.');
          var pilotLine = labValues.sample === 'heading' ? controllerLine : labValues.sample === 'altitude' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_pilot_altitude', 'TRAIN110, maintain 6,000 feet.') : t('stem.atctower.readback_pilot_runway', 'TRAIN101, cleared approach runway 18.');
          var readbackExplanation = lab.answer === 'match' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_match_explanation', 'The callsign, turn direction, and heading all match.') : lab.answer === 'callsign' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_callsign_explanation', 'The callsign changed from TRAIN101 to TRAIN110. The altitude matches; the aircraft identity needs correction.') : t('stem.atctower.readback_instruction_explanation', 'The runway changed from 27 to 18. The callsign matches; the runway needs correction.');
          labContent = h(React.Fragment, null,
            h('fieldset', null, h('legend', null, t('stem.atctower.choose_readback_example', 'Choose an example')), h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-options' }, ['heading','altitude','runway'].map(function(sample) { return h('button', { key: sample, 'data-atctower-readback-sample': sample, 'aria-pressed': labValues.sample === sample, onClick: function() { setLab({ sample: sample, response: '' }); } }, sample === 'heading' ? t('stem.atctower.heading', 'Heading') : sample === 'altitude' ? t('stem.atctower.altitude', 'Altitude') : t('stem.atctower.runway', 'Runway')); }))),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-messages' }, h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-message' }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.controller_instruction', 'Controller instruction')), h('p', null, controllerLine)), h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-message', 'data-highlight': !!labValues.response && lab.answer !== 'match' ? 'true' : 'false' }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.pilot_readback', 'Pilot readback')), h('p', null, pilotLine))),
            h('fieldset', null, h('legend', null, t('stem.atctower.what_do_you_notice', 'What do you notice?')), h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-options' }, ['match','callsign','instruction'].map(function(response) { return h('button', { key: response, 'data-atctower-readback-response': response, 'aria-pressed': labValues.response === response, onClick: function() { setLab({ response: response }); } }, response === 'match' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_matches', 'The details match') : response === 'callsign' ? t('stem.atctower.readback_callsign_differs', 'Callsign differs') : t('stem.atctower.readback_instruction_differs', 'Instruction differs')); }))),
            labValues.response && h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-feedback atc-explorer-summary', 'data-atctower-readback-feedback': lab.correct ? 'correct' : 'review', 'data-state': lab.correct ? 'ready' : 'review', role: 'status' }, h('strong', null, lab.correct ? t('stem.atctower.readback_check_correct', 'That’s the key detail.') : t('stem.atctower.readback_check_review', 'Compare the details again.')), readbackExplanation));
        }
        return h('section', { ref: lessonViewRef, tabIndex: -1, className: 'atc-tower atc-lesson-workspace', 'data-contrast': highContrast ? 'true' : 'false', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-lesson-title', 'data-atctower-lesson-view': d.selectedLesson },
          h('style', null, towerCss),
          h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-toolbar' }, h('button', { onClick: function() { updMulti({ view: 'menu', atcMenuPanel: 'lessons' }); } }, t('stem.atctower.back_to_tower', '← Back to Tower')), h('button', { 'aria-pressed': !!highContrast, onClick: function() { upd('highContrast', !highContrast); } }, t('stem.atctower.high_contrast', 'High contrast'))),
          h('header', { className: 'atc-lesson-header' }, atcVisualSymbol(lessonTopics[labKind].symbol, 'atc-skill-symbol'), h('div', null, h('p', { className: 'atc-eyebrow' }, t('stem.atctower.interactive_lesson', 'Interactive lesson')), h('h2', { id: 'atc-lesson-title' }, les.title), h('p', null, lessonTopics[labKind].question))),
          h('nav', { className: 'atc-lesson-nav', 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.lesson_topics', 'Lesson topics') }, Object.keys(lessonTopics).map(function(key) { return h('button', { key: key, 'data-atctower-lesson-topic': key, 'aria-current': key === labKind ? 'page' : undefined, onClick: function() { if (key !== labKind) upd('selectedLesson', key); } }, lessonTopics[key].name); })),
          h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-grid' },
            h('section', { className: 'atc-card atc-explorer', 'data-atctower-explorer': labKind, 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-explorer-title' },
              h('div', { className: 'atc-explorer-heading' }, h('h3', { id: 'atc-explorer-title' }, t('stem.atctower.try_the_concept', 'Try the concept')), h('button', { 'data-atctower-explorer-reset': labKind, onClick: resetLab }, t('stem.atctower.reset_example', 'Reset example'))), labContent,
              h('p', { className: 'atc-explorer-note atc-explorer-footer' }, t('stem.atctower.explorer_scope', 'Explore freely. This example does not change your tower shift.'))),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-reading' },
              h('section', { className: 'atc-card', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-lesson-idea' }, h('h3', { id: 'atc-lesson-idea' }, t('stem.atctower.understand_the_idea', 'Understand the idea')),
                h('p', null, __alloT('stem.atctower.' + d.selectedLesson + '_content_v2', les.content)),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-formula' }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.key_formula', 'Key Formula')), h('strong', null, __alloT('stem.atctower.' + d.selectedLesson + '_formula_v2', les.formula))),
                les.source && h('a', { className: 'atc-lesson-source', href: les.source, target: '_blank', rel: 'noopener noreferrer' }, les.sourceLabel),
                h('button', { className: 'atc-primary atc-lesson-practice', 'data-atctower-lesson-practice': d.selectedLesson, disabled: !!d.activeShift && !!lessonPracticeId, onClick: function() { if (lessonPracticeId) startPractice(lessonPracticeId); else updMulti({ view: 'menu', atcMenuPanel: 'practice' }); } }, lessonPracticeId ? t('stem.atctower.practice_this_concept', 'Practice this concept') : t('stem.atctower.browse_exercises', 'Browse practice exercises'))),
              h('section', { className: 'atc-card atc-lesson-tutor', role: 'region', 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.ai_tutor', 'AI tutor') },
                h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-tutor-head' }, h('h3', null, t('stem.atctower.lesson_tutor_title', 'Explain at my level'))),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-lesson-levels', role: 'group', 'aria-label': t('stem.atctower.reading_level', 'Reading level') }, LEVELS.map(function(L) { var active = aiLevel === L.id; return h('button', { key: L.id, onClick: function() { if (active) return; lessonRequestRef.current.generation += 1; lessonRequestRef.current.pending = null; var nextLevel = {}; nextLevel[aiLevelKey] = L.id; nextLevel[aiKey] = ''; nextLevel[aiLoadingKey] = false; nextLevel[aiErrorKey] = ''; nextLevel[aiTextLevelKey] = L.id; updMulti(nextLevel); }, 'aria-label': 'Reading level: ' + L.label + (active ? ' (selected)' : ''), 'aria-pressed': active }, L.label); })),
                h('button', { onClick: explain, disabled: aiLoading, 'aria-label': 'Generate AI explanation at ' + ((LEVELS.find(function(L) { return L.id === aiLevel; }) || {}).label || 'Grade 5') + ' level' }, aiLoading ? t('stem.atctower.tutor_thinking', 'Thinking…') : aiText ? t('stem.atctower.tutor_reexplain', 'Re-explain') : t('stem.atctower.tutor_explain', 'Explain')),
                aiError && h('p', { role: 'alert' }, aiError), aiText && h('p', { className: 'atc-lesson-ai-answer' }, aiText),
                !aiText && !aiLoading && !aiError && h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.click_explain_for_a_plain_language_bre', 'Click "Explain" for a plain-language breakdown of this ATC concept.'))))));
      }

      // ═══ SHIFT DEBRIEF ═══
      if (view === 'debrief') {
        var result = d.lastShift || {};
        var debriefProgress = atcExerciseResultSnapshot(result.exerciseResult);
        var nextPractice = ATC_PRACTICE_SCENARIOS.find(function(item) { return item.id === pathway.recommendedId; });
        var decisionEvents = atcDecisionLog(result.decisionLog);
        var evidenceTypes = { instructions: ['command', 'goAround'], outcomes: ['landing', 'missed', 'fuel', 'conflict', 'exercise', 'goAround'], math: ['math'] };
        var filteredEvents = evidenceFilter === 'all' ? decisionEvents : decisionEvents.filter(function(event) { return (evidenceTypes[evidenceFilter] || []).indexOf(event.type) !== -1; });
        var debriefStats = [[t('stem.atctower.aircraft_landed', 'Aircraft landed'), result.landed || 0], [t('stem.atctower.shift_score', 'Shift score'), result.score || 0], [t('stem.atctower.best_streak', 'Best streak'), result.bestStreak || 0], [t('stem.atctower.conflict_events', 'Conflict events'), result.conflictEpisodes || 0], [t('stem.atctower.conflict_time', 'Conflict time'), Math.round(result.conflictSeconds || 0) + ' s'], [t('stem.atctower.go_arounds', 'Go-arounds'), result.goArounds || 0], [t('stem.atctower.math_solved', 'Math solved'), result.mathCorrect || 0]];
        var reflectionKey = result.shiftId || 'legacy';
        var reflection = (d.shiftReflections || {})[reflectionKey] || '';
        var reflectionMoment = /^\d+$/.test(reflectionEvent) ? decisionEvents[Number(reflectionEvent)] : null;
        var momentLabel = function(event) { return Math.floor(event.time / 60) + ':' + String(Math.floor(event.time % 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ' · ' + event.text; };
        var saveReflection = function(value) {
          var saved = d.shiftReflections && typeof d.shiftReflections === 'object' ? d.shiftReflections : {};
          var kept = {};
          Object.keys(saved).filter(function(key) { return key !== reflectionKey && typeof saved[key] === 'string'; }).slice(-7).forEach(function(key) { kept[key] = saved[key].slice(0, 2000); });
          kept[reflectionKey] = value.slice(0, 2000);
          upd('shiftReflections', kept);
        };
        return h('section', { className: 'atc-tower', 'data-atctower-debrief': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-debrief-title' },
h('style', null, towerCss),
h('header', { className: 'atc-debrief-header', 'data-outcome': debriefProgress && debriefProgress.complete ? 'complete' : debriefProgress && debriefProgress.failed ? 'review' : 'recorded' },
  atcVisualSymbol(debriefProgress && debriefProgress.complete ? 'complete' : 'review', 'atc-skill-symbol'),
  h('div', null,
    h('h2', { id: 'atc-debrief-title', tabIndex: -1, ref: function(el) { if (el && document.activeElement === document.body) el.focus(); } }, debriefProgress ? (debriefProgress.complete ? t('stem.atctower.exercise_completed', 'Exercise completed') : debriefProgress.failed ? t('stem.atctower.review_attempt', 'Review this attempt') : t('stem.atctower.attempt_recorded', 'Attempt recorded')) : t('stem.atctower.shift_recorded', 'Tower shift recorded')),
    h('p', null, (result.airportName || t('stem.atctower.atc_tower', 'ATC Tower')) + ' · ' + (result.trainingMode === 'guided' ? t('stem.atctower.guided_practice', 'Guided practice') : t('stem.atctower.standard_shift', 'Standard shift'))))),
practiceCard(debriefProgress, true, result),
debriefProgress && debriefProgress.complete && nextPractice && h('p', { id: 'atc-next-practice-destination', 'data-atctower-next-destination': nextPractice.id, className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.next_exercise_destination', 'Next exercise:') + ' ' + nextPractice.title),
h('div', { className: 'atc-toolbar atc-debrief-actions' }, debriefProgress && debriefProgress.complete && pathway.recommendedId && h('button', { className: 'atc-primary', 'data-atctower-next-practice': pathway.recommendedId, 'aria-describedby': 'atc-next-practice-destination', disabled: !!d.activeShift, onClick: function() { startPractice(pathway.recommendedId); } }, t('stem.atctower.next_exercise', 'Next exercise') + ' →'),
          result.practice && h('button', { className: debriefProgress && !debriefProgress.complete ? 'atc-primary' : undefined, 'data-atctower-practice-retry': result.practice.id, disabled: !!d.activeShift, onClick: function() { startPractice(result.practice.id); } }, t('stem.atctower.retry_exercise', 'Retry this exercise')),
          h('button', { className: !debriefProgress || (debriefProgress.complete && !pathway.recommendedId) ? 'atc-primary' : undefined, disabled: !!d.activeShift, onClick: function() { startGame(result.airportId || 'simple'); } }, t('stem.atctower.try_another_shift', 'Try another shift')),
          h('button', { onClick: function() { updMulti({ view: 'menu', atcMenuPanel: 'airports' }); } }, t('stem.atctower.choose_airport', 'Choose airport')),
          h('button', { onClick: function() { updMulti({ view: 'menu', atcMenuPanel: 'lessons' }); } }, t('stem.atctower.review_lessons', 'Review lessons')),
          debriefProgress && h('button', { onClick: function() { updMulti({ view: 'menu', atcMenuPanel: 'practice' }); } }, t('stem.atctower.return_practice', 'Practice pathway'))),
h('div', { className: 'atc-debrief-grid' }, h('div', { className: 'atc-debrief-learning' },
h('section', { className: 'atc-card atc-review-card', 'data-atctower-reflection-card': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-reflection-title' },
            h('h3', { id: 'atc-reflection-title' }, t('stem.atctower.review_one_decision', 'Review one decision')),
            decisionEvents.length > 0 && h('div', null,
              h('label', { htmlFor: 'atc-reflection-event' }, t('stem.atctower.choose_reflection_moment', 'Choose a moment (optional)')),
              h('select', { id: 'atc-reflection-event', 'data-atctower-reflection-event': 'true', value: reflectionEvent, onChange: function(e) { setReflectionEvent(e.target.value); } },
                h('option', { value: '' }, t('stem.atctower.reflect_without_event', 'Reflect on the whole attempt')),
                decisionEvents.map(function(event, index) { var label = momentLabel(event); return h('option', { key: index, value: String(index) }, label.length > 100 ? label.slice(0, 99) + '…' : label); }))),
            reflectionMoment && h('div', { className: 'atc-reflection-context', 'data-atctower-reflection-context': 'true', role: 'status' },
              h('strong', null, t('stem.atctower.selected_moment', 'Selected moment') + ' · ' + Math.floor(reflectionMoment.time / 60) + ':' + String(Math.floor(reflectionMoment.time % 60)).padStart(2, '0')),
              h('p', null, reflectionMoment.text)),
            h('ul', { className: 'atc-reflection-prompts' },
              h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.reflection_intention', 'What did you intend?')),
              h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.reflection_effect', 'What changed in the aircraft or traffic?')),
              h('li', null, t('stem.atctower.reflection_next_change', 'What will you keep or change next time?'))),
            h('label', { htmlFor: 'atc-shift-reflection' }, t('stem.atctower.shift_reflection', 'What will you try next?')),
            h('p', { id: 'atc-shift-reflection-help', className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.shift_reflection_copy_v2', 'Optional: explain a decision and one change to try. Your saved note can remind you on a matching retry.')),
            h('textarea', { id: 'atc-shift-reflection', 'aria-describedby': 'atc-shift-reflection-help', rows: 3, maxLength: 2000, value: reflection, onChange: function(e) { saveReflection(e.target.value); }, style: { width: '100%', padding: 10, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid #81ada4', background: '#061713', color: '#f8fafc', font: 'inherit', resize: 'vertical' } })),
debriefProgress ? h('details', { className: 'atc-card', 'data-atctower-shift-numbers': 'true' }, h('summary', null, t('stem.atctower.shift_numbers', 'Shift numbers')), h('div', { className: 'atc-stats' }, debriefStats.map(function(stat) { return h('div', { className: 'atc-stat', key: stat[0] }, h('strong', null, stat[1]), stat[0]); }))) : h('div', { className: 'atc-stats' }, debriefStats.map(function(stat) { return h('div', { className: 'atc-stat', key: stat[0] }, h('strong', null, stat[1]), stat[0]); })),
!debriefProgress && h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.shift_review_copy', 'Review your sequencing decisions: which aircraft needed more room, and which command helped you recover?')),
!debriefProgress && h('p', null, (result.conflictEpisodes || 0) > 0 ? t('stem.atctower.review_separation', 'Next shift: use holds and altitude changes early to protect separation. A conflict resets the landing streak.') : t('stem.atctower.review_spacing', 'Next shift: compare the arrival estimates before choosing the landing order. Leave extra room around Heavy and Super aircraft.'))),
h('details', { className: 'atc-card', style: { marginTop: 12 }, open: true, 'data-atctower-decision-log': 'true' },
            h('summary', null, t('stem.atctower.decision_evidence', 'Decision evidence · most recent 40 events')),
            h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.evidence_clock_note', 'Times below use the shift clock. Aircraft motion and fuel use accelerated flight time.')),
            h('div', { className: 'atc-evidence-filter' }, h('label', { htmlFor: 'atc-evidence-filter' }, t('stem.atctower.show_evidence', 'Show evidence')),
              h('select', { id: 'atc-evidence-filter', 'data-atctower-evidence-filter': 'true', value: evidenceFilter, onChange: function(e) { setEvidenceFilter(e.target.value); } },
                [['all',t('stem.atctower.evidence_all', 'All events')],['instructions',t('stem.atctower.evidence_instructions', 'Instructions and go-arounds')],['outcomes',t('stem.atctower.evidence_outcomes', 'Aircraft outcomes and alerts')],['math',t('stem.atctower.evidence_math', 'Math decisions')]].map(function(option) { return h('option', { key: option[0], value: option[0] }, option[1]); })),
              h('span', { 'data-atctower-evidence-count': 'true', 'aria-live': 'polite' }, filteredEvents.length + ' / ' + decisionEvents.length + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.evidence_events', 'events'))),
            h('ol', { className: 'atc-radio', 'data-atctower-evidence-events': 'true' }, filteredEvents.map(function(event, index) { return h('li', { key: index }, h('strong', null, Math.floor(event.time / 60) + ':' + String(Math.floor(event.time % 60)).padStart(2, '0') + ' · '), event.text); })),
            !decisionEvents.length ? h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.no_decision_evidence', 'No instructions or outcomes were recorded in this shift.')) : !filteredEvents.length && h('p', { 'data-atctower-evidence-empty': 'true' }, t('stem.atctower.no_matching_evidence', 'No events match this view. Try All events.')))));
      }

      // ═══ PLAYING VIEW ═══
      if (view === 'playing') {
        var live = snapshot || gameRef.current;
        var airport = live.airport;
        var aircraft = sortedFlightStrips(live.aircraft || [], airport);
        // Preserve the current tab order while a learner is inspecting a strip.
        if (flightOrderRef.current) aircraft.sort(function(a, b) {
          var ai = flightOrderRef.current.indexOf(String(a.id));
          var bi = flightOrderRef.current.indexOf(String(b.id));
          return (ai < 0 ? 100000 : ai) - (bi < 0 ? 100000 : bi);
        });
        var watch = separationWatch(aircraft, 120);
        var runwayPlan = airport ? runwayScheduleHints(aircraft, airport, live.runwayBusyUntil || {}, live.time || 0) : [];
        var arrivalBoard = atcArrivalBoard(aircraft, airport, runwayPlan);
        var trafficReview = atcTrafficReview(aircraft, watch, runwayPlan);
        var selected = aircraft.find(function(ac) { return ac.id === live.selected; });
        var readback = atcAircraftReadback(selected);
        var draftAircraft = commandDraft && aircraft.find(function(ac) { return ac.id === commandDraft.aircraftId; });
        var preview = commandDraft && atcCommandPreview(draftAircraft, commandDraft.kind, commandDraft.value, airport);
        var feedback = commandFeedback && (commandFeedback.aircraftId == null || (selected && commandFeedback.aircraftId === selected.id)) ? commandFeedback : null;
        var challenge = live.mathChallenge;
        var trafficStatus = live.gameOver ? 'closed' : (atcTrafficFrozen(live, document.hidden) || commandDraft) ? 'paused' : 'live';
        var exerciseProgress = scenarioProgress(live);
        var practiceDecision = atcPracticeDecisionState(live);
        var doCommand = function(key, value) { if (commandRef.current) commandRef.current(key, value); };
        var jumpTo = function(target) {
          var tower = canvasRef.current && canvasRef.current.closest('[data-atctower-playing]');
          var ids = { controls: 'atc-aircraft-controls-title', spacing: 'atc-watch-title', runway: 'atc-runway-title', priority: 'atc-flight-strips-title' };
          var node = target === 'radar' ? canvasRef.current : tower && tower.querySelector('#' + ids[target]);
          if (node) { node.focus(); node.scrollIntoView({ block: 'start', behavior: 'auto' }); }
        };
        var inspectAircraft = function(id) {
          if (commandDraft || challenge) return;
          selectAircraft(id);
          setTimeout(function() { jumpTo('controls'); }, 0);
        };
        var readbackValue = function(row, target) {
          var value = target ? row.target : row.current;
          // Automatic guidance recomputes on movement. A paused phase change
          // can still carry the prior manual target until the first step.
          if (target && row.automatic && (live.paused || live.showHelp || commandDraft || challenge)) return t('stem.atctower.guided_target', 'Guided');
          if (value == null) return row.automatic ? t('stem.atctower.guided_target', 'Guided') : t('stem.atctower.maintain_current', 'Maintain');
          var shown = row.id === 'heading' ? String(Math.round(value) % 360).padStart(3, '0') + '°' : Math.round(value).toLocaleString() + (row.id === 'altitude' ? ' ft' : ' kt');
          return target && row.automatic ? t('stem.atctower.automatic_target', 'Auto') + ' · ' + shown : shown;
        };
        var previewCard = function() {
          if (!commandDraft) return null;
          if (!preview) return h('p', { 'data-atctower-draft-preview': 'invalid', className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.preview_valid_value', 'Enter a whole value in the range above to preview this instruction.'));
          var heading = preview.kind === 'heading';
          var turnText = !heading ? '' : preview.turn === 'straight' ? t('stem.atctower.preview_same_heading', 'Same current heading') : preview.turnDegrees < 0.5 ? t('stem.atctower.preview_align_heading', 'Align to this heading') :
            (preview.turn === 'left' ? t('stem.atctower.preview_turn_left', 'Turn left') : t('stem.atctower.preview_turn_right', 'Turn right')) + ' ' + (preview.turnDegrees < 10 ? preview.turnDegrees.toFixed(1).replace(/\.0$/, '') : Math.round(preview.turnDegrees)) + '°';
          return h('div', { className: 'atc-command-preview', 'data-atctower-draft-preview': preview.kind },
            h('div', { className: heading ? 'atc-preview-heading' : undefined }, heading && h('svg', { className: 'atc-preview-compass', viewBox: '0 0 112 112', 'aria-hidden': true, focusable: false },
              h('circle', { cx: 56, cy: 56, r: 40, fill: 'none', stroke: '#638696' }),
              [['N',56,14],['E',104,60],['S',56,107],['W',8,60]].map(function(item) { return h('text', { key: item[0], x: item[1], y: item[2], textAnchor: 'middle', style: { fontSize: 14 } }, item[0]); }),
              h('g', { transform: 'rotate(' + preview.current + ' 56 56)', className: 'atc-current-vector' }, h('line', { x1: 56, y1: 56, x2: 56, y2: 21, strokeWidth: 2, strokeDasharray: '3 3' })),

          if (!airport) return '';

        };
        var closeEntry = function() {
          var key = commandDraft && commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? 'h' : 's';
          gameRef.current.commandEntry = false; setCommandDraft(null); refreshTower();
          setTimeout(function() { var tower = canvasRef.current && canvasRef.current.closest('[data-atctower-playing]'); var button = tower && tower.querySelector('[data-atctower-command="' + key + '"]'); if (button) button.focus(); }, 0);
        };
        var commitEntry = function(e) {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (!commandDraft) return;
          var value = Number(commandDraft.value);
          var isHeading = commandDraft.kind === 'heading';
          var valid = String(commandDraft.value).trim() !== '' && isFinite(value) && Number.isInteger(value) && (isHeading ? value >= 0 && value <= 360 : value >= 60 && value <= 250);
          if (!valid) { setCommandDraft(Object.assign({}, commandDraft, { error: isHeading ? t('stem.atctower.valid_heading', 'Enter a heading from 0 to 360 degrees.') : t('stem.atctower.valid_speed', 'Enter a whole speed from 60 to 250 knots.') })); return; }
          var game = gameRef.current;
          var ac = game.aircraft.find(function(a) { return a.id === commandDraft.aircraftId && a.state !== 'landed' && a.state !== 'departed'; });
          if (!ac || game.gameOver || (game.practice && game.practice.completed)) { closeEntry(); return; }
          if (isHeading) {
            ac.targetHeading = value % 360;
            if (ac.state === 'approach') ac.targetAltitude = ac.altitude;
            if (ac.state === 'approach' || ac.state === 'holding') { ac.state = 'inbound'; ac.approachStage = null; ac.holdCenter = null; ac.touchdownReady = false; }
            if (tutorialRef.current.step === 2) tutorialRef.current.step = 3;
          } else ac.targetSpeed = value;
          var message = ac.callsign + (isHeading ? ' heading ' : ' speed ') + value + (isHeading ? ' degrees.' : ' knots.');
          game.messages.unshift({ text: message, time: game.time, color: '#99f6e4' });
          recordAtcDecision(game, 'command', ac, message);
          showCommandFeedback('sent', message, ac);
          closeEntry();
        };

        var raiseLabel = raiseInfo && raiseInfo.targetAltitude <= altitudeBase ? t('stem.atctower.set_altitude_target', 'Set target') : t('stem.atctower.raise_altitude_target', 'Raise target');
        var destinationText = function(info) { return info ? Math.round(info.targetAltitude).toLocaleString() + ' ft' : ''; };

        ];

                  h('button', { type: 'submit', className: 'atc-primary' }, t('stem.atctower.check_answer', 'Check answer')))),
                !challenge.answered && h('button', { onClick: function() { submitMath('', true); }, style: { marginTop: 10 } }, t('stem.atctower.worked_example', 'Show worked example')),
                challenge.answered && h('div', { role: 'status' }, h('p', null, challenge.correct ? t('stem.atctower.correct', 'Correct!') : t('stem.atctower.learn_from_example', 'Learn from the worked example.')), h('p', null, challenge.explanation), h('button', { className: 'atc-primary', 'data-atctower-math-continue': 'true', onClick: function() { gameRef.current.mathChallenge = null; refreshTower(); if (canvasRef.current) canvasRef.current.focus(); announce(gameRef.current.paused ? 'Math complete. Shift remains paused.' : 'Math complete. Traffic continuing.'); } }, t('stem.atctower.continue_traffic', 'Continue traffic')))),
h('section', { className: 'atc-card', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-flight-strips-title' },
                h('h3', { id: 'atc-flight-strips-title', tabIndex: -1 }, t('stem.atctower.flight_strips', 'Flight strips')),
                h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.flight_strips_order', 'Priority and low fuel first, then earliest estimated arrival. Select a strip to inspect its instructions.')),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-aircraft-list', tabIndex: commandDraft ? 0 : undefined, role: commandDraft ? 'region' : undefined, 'aria-labelledby': commandDraft ? 'atc-flight-strips-title' : undefined, onFocus: function(e) { if (!flightOrderRef.current) flightOrderRef.current = Array.from(e.currentTarget.querySelectorAll('[data-atctower-aircraft]')).map(function(button) { return button.getAttribute('data-atctower-aircraft'); }); }, onBlur: function(e) { if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget)) flightOrderRef.current = null; } }, aircraft.length ? aircraft.map(function(ac) { return h('button', { key: ac.id, 'data-atctower-aircraft': String(ac.id), disabled: !!commandDraft, 'aria-pressed': ac.id === live.selected, onClick: function() { selectAircraft(ac.id); } },
                  h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-heading' }, ac.id === live.selected && h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-selected', 'aria-hidden': true }, t('stem.atctower.selected_aircraft', 'Selected')),
                    h('strong', null, ac.callsign + ((ac.emergency && ac.emergency.priority !== false) || ac.fuel < 5 ? ' · ' + t('stem.atctower.priority', 'Priority') : ac.emergency ? ' · ' + t('stem.atctower.incident', 'Incident') : ''))),
                  h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-route' }, ac.type.id + ' · ' + stateName(ac) + ' · ' + (ac.assignedRunway ? 'RWY ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport) : t('stem.atctower.no_runway', 'No runway'))),
                  h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-numbers' }, h('span', null, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.altitude', 'Altitude') + ': '), Math.round(ac.altitude).toLocaleString() + ' ft'), h('span', null, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.speed', 'Speed') + ': '), Math.round(ac.speed) + ' kt')),
                  h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-detail' }, t('stem.atctower.fuel_remaining', 'Fuel remaining') + ': ' + Math.max(0, ac.fuel).toFixed(1) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.flight_minutes', 'flight min')),
                  h('span', { className: 'atc-strip-detail' }, distanceText(ac))); }) : h('p', null, live.practice ? t('stem.atctower.exercise_traffic_finished', 'Exercise traffic finished. Review the objectives before ending or retrying.') : live.trainingMode === 'guided' ? t('stem.atctower.guided_empty', 'Use Add next aircraft when you are ready to practice another arrival.') : t('stem.atctower.awaiting_aircraft', 'Awaiting the next aircraft.')))),
h('section', { className: 'atc-card', 'data-atctower-aircraft-controls': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-aircraft-controls-title' },
                h('div', { className: 'atc-control-heading' }, h('h3', { id: 'atc-aircraft-controls-title', tabIndex: -1 }, selected ? selected.callsign : t('stem.atctower.aircraft_controls', 'Aircraft controls')),
                  h('div', { className: 'atc-mobile-nav' }, h('button', { 'data-atctower-jump-radar': 'true', disabled: !!commandDraft || !!challenge, onClick: function() { jumpTo('radar'); } }, t('stem.atctower.back_to_radar', 'Radar') + ' ↑'))),
                selected && h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, stateName(selected) + ' · ' + t('stem.atctower.heading', 'Heading') + ' ' + String(Math.round(selected.heading) % 360).padStart(3, '0') + '° · ' + t('stem.atctower.fuel', 'Fuel') + ' ' + Math.max(0, selected.fuel).toFixed(1) + ' min'),
                readback && h('div', { 'data-atctower-readback': readback.mode },
                  h('p', { className: 'atc-guidance-mode' }, readback.mode === 'approach' ? t('stem.atctower.mode_approach_v2', 'Approach guidance · targets update as traffic moves') : readback.mode === 'hold' ? t('stem.atctower.mode_hold_v2', 'Teaching hold · heading updates as traffic moves') : readback.mode === 'goAround' ? t('stem.atctower.mode_go_around', 'Go-around · following recovery instructions') : t('stem.atctower.mode_manual', 'Inbound · following your instructions')),
                  h('div', { className: 'atc-readback' }, h('div', { className: 'atc-readback-head', 'aria-hidden': true }, h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.flight_value', 'Flight value')), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.current_value', 'Current')), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.active_instruction', 'Instruction'))),
                    h('dl', null, readback.rows.map(function(row) { return h('div', { key: row.id, 'data-atctower-readback-row': row.id },
                      h('dt', null, row.id === 'heading' ? t('stem.atctower.heading', 'Heading') : row.id === 'altitude' ? t('stem.atctower.altitude', 'Altitude') : t('stem.atctower.speed', 'Speed')),
                      h('dd', { 'data-atctower-readback-current': row.id }, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.current_value', 'Current') + ': '), readbackValue(row, false)),
                      h('dd', { 'data-atctower-readback-target': row.id }, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.active_instruction', 'Instruction') + ': '), readbackValue(row, true))); })))),
                !tutorialRef.current.dismissed && !live.gameOver && !(exerciseProgress && exerciseProgress.complete) && h('div', { className: 'atc-local-coaching', 'data-atctower-coaching': 'true' },
                  h('h4', null, t('stem.atctower.next_action', 'Next action')), h('p', null, coachingText),
                  h('button', { onClick: function() { tutorialRef.current.dismissed = true; refreshTower(); } }, t('stem.atctower.hide_coaching', 'Hide coaching'))),
                selected && airport && airport.runways.length > 1 && h('label', { style: { display: 'block', marginBottom: 12 } }, t('stem.atctower.select_runway', 'Assigned runway'),
                  h('select', { 'data-atctower-runway-select': 'true', 'aria-describedby': guidanceNote ? 'atc-guidance-command-note' : undefined, value: String(airport.runways.indexOf(selected.assignedRunway)), onChange: function(e) { doCommand('r', Number(e.target.value)); }, disabled: !!live.gameOver || !!commandDraft || !!(live.practice && live.practice.completed), style: { display: 'block', width: '100%', minHeight: 44, marginTop: 6, padding: 8, borderRadius: 6, border: '1px solid #81ada4', color: '#f8fafc', background: '#061713' } },
                    h('option', { value: '-1' }, t('stem.atctower.no_runway', 'No runway')), airport.runways.map(function(rwy, index) { return h('option', { key: index, value: String(index) }, 'RWY ' + runwayLabel(rwy, airport)); }))),
                guidanceNote && h('p', { id: 'atc-guidance-command-note', className: 'atc-guidance-command-note', 'data-approach-note': selected && selected.state === 'approach' ? 'true' : 'false' }, guidanceNote),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-command-grid' }, commandButtons.map(function(command) { return h('button', { key: command[0], 'data-atctower-command': command[0], 'aria-describedby': guidanceNote && ['r','p','d','u'].indexOf(command[0]) !== -1 ? 'atc-guidance-command-note' : undefined, disabled: !selected || !!live.gameOver || !!commandDraft || !!(live.practice && live.practice.completed), onClick: function() { doCommand(command[0]); } }, h('span', { className: 'atc-command-name' }, atcVisualSymbol(command[0], 'atc-command-icon'), h('span', null, command[1])), command[2] && h('span', { className: 'atc-command-destination' }, command[2])); })),
                feedback && h('div', { className: 'atc-command-feedback', 'data-atctower-command-feedback': 'true', 'data-feedback-status': feedback.status },
                  h('strong', null, feedback.status === 'blocked' ? t('stem.atctower.command_needs_action', 'Action needed') : feedback.status === 'unchanged' ? t('stem.atctower.command_unchanged', 'Instruction unchanged') : t('stem.atctower.command_sent', 'Instruction sent')),
                  h('p', null, feedback.text)),
                commandDraft && h('form', { onSubmit: commitEntry, 'data-atctower-command-entry': commandDraft.kind, style: { marginTop: 12 } },
                  h('p', null, commandDraft.callsign),
                  h('label', { htmlFor: 'atc-command-value' }, commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? t('stem.atctower.heading_range', 'Heading (0–360°)') : t('stem.atctower.speed_range', 'Speed (60–250 knots)')),
                  h('input', { id: 'atc-command-value', type: 'number', inputMode: 'numeric', autoFocus: true, min: commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? 0 : 60, max: commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? 360 : 250, step: 1, required: true, value: commandDraft.value, 'aria-invalid': !!commandDraft.error, 'aria-describedby': 'atc-command-help' + (commandDraft.error ? ' atc-command-error' : ''), onChange: function(e) { setCommandDraft(Object.assign({}, commandDraft, { value: e.target.value, error: '' })); }, onKeyDown: function(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') { e.preventDefault(); closeEntry(); } } }),
                  h('p', { id: 'atc-command-help', className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.preview_paused', 'Traffic pauses while you preview. Send applies this instruction.')),
                  h('div', { className: 'atc-presets', role: 'group', 'aria-label': commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? t('stem.atctower.heading_presets', 'Heading presets') : t('stem.atctower.speed_presets', 'Speed presets') },
                    (commandDraft.kind === 'heading' ? [[0,t('stem.atctower.north_heading', 'North · 000°')],[90,t('stem.atctower.east_heading', 'East · 090°')],[180,t('stem.atctower.south_heading', 'South · 180°')],[270,t('stem.atctower.west_heading', 'West · 270°')]] : [90,120,draftAircraft && draftAircraft.type ? Math.round(draftAircraft.type.speed) : 150,180].filter(function(value,index,all) { return value >= 60 && value <= 250 && all.indexOf(value) === index; }).sort(function(a,b) { return a-b; }).map(function(value) { return [value,value + ' kt']; })).map(function(item) {
                      return h('button', { key: item[0], type: 'button', 'data-atctower-command-preset': String(item[0]), onClick: function() { setCommandDraft(Object.assign({}, commandDraft, { value: String(item[0]), error: '' })); } }, item[1]); })),
                  previewCard(),
                  commandDraft.error && h('p', { id: 'atc-command-error', role: 'alert' }, commandDraft.error),
                  h('div', { className: 'atc-toolbar' }, h('button', { type: 'submit', className: 'atc-primary' }, t('stem.atctower.send_instruction', 'Send instruction')), h('button', { type: 'button', onClick: closeEntry }, t('stem.atctower.cancel', 'Cancel')))))),
h('div', { className: 'atc-operations-panel', 'data-atctower-operations': 'true' },
h('section', { className: 'atc-card', 'data-atctower-separation-watch': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-watch-title' },
                h('h3', { id: 'atc-watch-title', tabIndex: -1 }, t('stem.atctower.separation_watch', 'Separation watch')),
                h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.watch_assumptions', 'Next two flight minutes at current heading and speed, with commanded altitude changes. Turns and holds change these teaching estimates.')),
                watch.length ? h('ul', { className: 'atc-watch-list' }, watch.slice(0, 4).map(function(pair) {
                  var label = pair.severity === 'conflict' ? t('stem.atctower.spacing_conflict', 'Spacing conflict now') : pair.severity === 'projected' ? t('stem.atctower.projected_spacing', 'Projected spacing loss') : t('stem.atctower.spacing_caution', 'Spacing caution');
                  return h('li', { key: String(pair.a.id) + ':' + pair.b.id, 'data-severity': pair.severity },
                    h('strong', null, label + ': ' + pair.a.callsign + ' / ' + pair.b.callsign),
                    h('p', null, pair.currentDistanceNm.toFixed(1) + ' nm · ' + Math.round(pair.currentVerticalFt).toLocaleString() + ' ft ' + t('stem.atctower.vertical_gap', 'vertical gap')),
                    h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.watch_minimum', 'Model spacing') + ': ' + pair.minimumNm + ' nm / 1,000 ft'),
                    pair.severity === 'projected' && h('p', null, '~' + Math.ceil(pair.breachTimeSeconds) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.flight_seconds_to_spacing', 'flight seconds until the spacing limit')),
                    h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.watch_action', 'Pause and review heading, altitude, speed or a hold.')),
                    pair.turning && h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.watch_turning', 'Turning or holding: the current-course estimate may change quickly.')),
                    h('div', { className: 'atc-toolbar' }, [pair.a, pair.b].map(function(ac) { return h('button', { key: ac.id, 'data-atctower-inspect-aircraft': String(ac.id), disabled: !!commandDraft || !!challenge, onClick: function() { inspectAircraft(ac.id); } }, t('stem.atctower.inspect', 'Inspect') + ' ' + ac.callsign); })));
                })) : h('p', null, t('stem.atctower.watch_clear', 'No spacing concerns detected within this estimate. Keep checking the arrival order.'))),
airport && h('section', { className: 'atc-card', 'data-atctower-runway-plan': 'true', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-runway-title' },
                h('h3', { id: 'atc-runway-title', tabIndex: -1 }, t('stem.atctower.runway_board', 'Runway board')),
                h('p', { className: 'atc-muted atc-arrival-intro' }, t('stem.atctower.arrival_board_intro', 'Compare estimated arrival times, then inspect an aircraft to adjust its instructions.')),
                h('div', { className: 'atc-runway-entries', 'data-runway-count': String(arrivalBoard.groups.length) }, arrivalBoard.groups.map(function(group) {
                  return h('section', { key: group.id, className: 'atc-runway-entry atc-arrival-group', 'data-atctower-arrival-group': group.id, 'data-occupied': group.occupied ? 'true' : 'false', 'aria-labelledby': 'atc-arrival-group-' + group.id },
                    h('div', { className: 'atc-arrival-group-heading' }, h('h4', { id: 'atc-arrival-group-' + group.id }, 'RWY ' + group.runways.map(function(rwy) { return runwayLabel(rwy, airport); }).join(' + ')), h('span', { className: 'atc-pill' }, group.occupied ? t('stem.atctower.runway_occupied', 'Occupied') : t('stem.atctower.runway_available', 'Available'))),
                    group.runways.length > 1 && h('p', { className: 'atc-arrival-shared' }, t('stem.atctower.shared_arrival_sequence', 'Shared runway area · one arrival sequence')),
                    group.occupied && h('p', { className: 'atc-arrival-occupancy' }, t('stem.atctower.occupancy_remaining', 'Current occupancy remaining:') + ' ~' + group.occupancyMinutes.toFixed(1) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.flight_minutes', 'flight min')),
                    group.entries.length ? h(React.Fragment, null,
                      h('p', { className: 'atc-arrival-summary' }, t('stem.atctower.estimated_arrival_order', 'Estimated arrival order') + ' · ' + group.entries.length),
                      h('div', { className: 'atc-arrival-scale', 'aria-hidden': true }, h('span', null, '0'), h('span', null, (arrivalBoard.horizonMinutes / 2).toLocaleString()), h('span', null, arrivalBoard.horizonMinutes.toLocaleString())),
                      h('p', { className: 'atc-arrival-axis' }, t('stem.atctower.arrival_axis', 'Flight minutes from now')),
                      h('ol', { className: 'atc-arrival-list' }, group.entries.map(function(entry) {
                        var ac = entry.aircraft;
                        var priority = (ac.emergency && ac.emergency.priority !== false) || (atcFinite(ac.fuel) && ac.fuel < 5);
                        return h('li', { key: ac.id, 'data-atctower-arrival-row': String(ac.id) },
                          h('button', { className: 'atc-arrival-button', 'data-atctower-arrival': String(ac.id), 'data-atctower-inspect-aircraft': String(ac.id), 'data-selected': ac.id === live.selected ? 'true' : 'false', 'aria-describedby': [entry.occupancyConcern ? 'atc-arrival-occupancy-' + ac.id : '', entry.gapMinutes != null ? 'atc-arrival-gap-' + ac.id : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ') || undefined, disabled: !!commandDraft || !!challenge, onClick: function() { inspectAircraft(ac.id); } },
                            h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-row-header' }, h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-rank', 'aria-hidden': true }, entry.order),
                              h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-identity' }, h('strong', null, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.inspect', 'Inspect') + ' '), ac.callsign), h('span', null, 'RWY ' + runwayLabel(ac.assignedRunway, airport)), priority && h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-priority' }, t('stem.atctower.priority', 'Priority'))),
                              h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-time' }, h('strong', null, '~' + entry.etaMinutes.toFixed(1)), h('span', null, t('stem.atctower.flight_minutes', 'flight min')))),
                            h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-track', 'aria-hidden': true }, group.occupied && h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-occupied-band', style: { width: Math.min(100, group.occupancyMinutes / arrivalBoard.horizonMinutes * 100) + '%' } }), h('span', { className: 'atc-arrival-dot', 'data-priority': priority ? 'true' : 'false', style: { left: Math.min(100, entry.etaMinutes / arrivalBoard.horizonMinutes * 100) + '%' } }))),
                          entry.occupancyConcern && h('p', { id: 'atc-arrival-occupancy-' + ac.id, className: 'atc-arrival-alert' }, t('stem.atctower.arrival_within_occupancy', 'Estimate falls inside current runway occupancy.')),
                          entry.gapMinutes != null && h('p', { id: 'atc-arrival-gap-' + ac.id, className: 'atc-arrival-gap', 'data-atctower-arrival-gap': String(ac.id), 'data-concern': entry.spacingConcern ? 'true' : 'false' },
                            h('strong', null, entry.spacingConcern ? t('stem.atctower.arrival_gap_review', 'Review gap') : t('stem.atctower.arrival_gap', 'Arrival gap')),
                            ' · ' + entry.gapMinutes.toFixed(1) + ' / ' + entry.requiredGapMinutes.toFixed(1) + ' ' + t('stem.atctower.arrival_gap_target', 'flight min target')));
                      }))) : h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.no_assigned_arrivals', 'No assigned arrivals with an ETA.')));
                })),
                arrivalBoard.pending.length > 0 && h('div', { className: 'atc-arrival-pending', 'data-atctower-arrival-pending': 'true' },
                  h('h4', null, t('stem.atctower.waiting_for_estimate', 'Waiting for an estimate')),
                  h('ul', null, arrivalBoard.pending.map(function(item) {
                    var reason = item.reason === 'holding' ? t('stem.atctower.estimate_held', 'Holding · release the hold for a new estimate') : item.reason === 'goAround' ? t('stem.atctower.estimate_go_around', 'Going around · estimate resumes after recovery') : item.reason === 'unassigned' ? t('stem.atctower.estimate_unassigned', 'Assign a runway to estimate arrival') : t('stem.atctower.estimate_unavailable', 'No reliable arrival estimate at the current speed or position');
                    return h('li', { key: item.aircraft.id }, h('button', { 'data-atctower-pending-arrival': String(item.aircraft.id), 'data-atctower-inspect-aircraft': String(item.aircraft.id), disabled: !!commandDraft || !!challenge, onClick: function() { inspectAircraft(item.aircraft.id); } }, h('strong', null, h('span', { className: 'sr-only' }, t('stem.atctower.inspect', 'Inspect') + ' '), item.aircraft.callsign), h('span', null, reason)));
                  }))),
                h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.runway_plan_assumptions', 'Plan uses assigned arrivals at current speed. Approach clearance is still required. The target allows for runway occupancy and simplified wake spacing; vectors change the order.')),
                airport.id === 'crossing' && h('p', { className: 'atc-muted' }, t('stem.atctower.crossing_note', 'Crossing runways share occupancy. An arrival goes around if the runway group is still occupied.')))),
h('div', { className: 'atc-review-panel', style: { minWidth: 0 } },
h('details', { className: 'atc-card', style: { marginTop: 12 } }, h('summary', null, t('stem.atctower.radio_log', 'Radio & event log')), h('ol', { className: 'atc-radio' }, (live.messages || []).map(function(msg, idx) { return h('li', { key: idx }, msg.text); }))))));
      }

      return h('div', { style: { padding: '24px', textAlign: 'center', color: 'var(--allo-stem-text-soft, #94a3b8)' } }, t('stem.atctower.loading_atc_tower', 'Loading ATC Tower...'));
    }
  });

})();
} // end duplicate guard
